### [1] [Rethinking Polling Efficiency in Service Core Network Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16408)
*Matheus Stolet,Simon Peter,Antoine Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"空闲核心即浪费"的传统假设，发现在现代多核处理器中，由于功率预算限制，回收空闲核心的收益低于预期且增加了调度复杂性，提出应以功率而非核心占用作为核心资源管理的基本单位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的网络服务核心管理假设空闲核心就是浪费的计算资源，这推动了各种复杂的核心回收机制发展。然而作者认为这一观点已不再适用于现代服务器硬件，因为当代多核处理器中活跃核心会竞争共享的功耗和热预算，当功耗预算成为限制资源时，空闲核心的等待策略会影响硬件将计算能力重新分配给有效工作的能力。

Method: 在最新的AMD EPYC处理器上进行测量实验，系统性地分析等待策略、处理器拓扑结构和空闲时长如何影响空闲核心与计算能力分配之间的权衡关系，评估回收空闲核心的实际效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，回收空闲核心通常带来的收益比普遍假设的要少，同时引入了大量的调度复杂性。研究发现等待策略、处理器拓扑和空闲持续时间共同决定了这一权衡关系。

Conclusion: 提出了以预算为中心的服务核心系统视角，主张将功率而非核心占用作为基本资源单位，并将等待策略作为一等公民级别的系统设计选择，而非简单地追求最大化核心利用率。

Abstract: Idle network service cores are treated as wasted compute. This assumption motivates increasingly sophisticated mechanisms that reclaim idle cores at microsecond timescales. We argue that this view no longer matches modern server hardware. On contemporary multicore processors, active cores compete for a shared package level power and thermal budget. Once that budget becomes the limiting resource, an idle core that waits efficiently returns compute capacity that hardware can redistribute to productive work. Measurements on a recent AMD EPYC processor show how waiting strategy, processor topology, and idle duration determine this tradeoff. Our results suggest that reclaiming idle cores often yields less benefit than commonly assumed while introducing substantial scheduling complexity. We propose a budget centric view of service core systems in which power, rather than core occupancy, becomes the fundamental resource and waiting policy becomes a first class systems design choice.

</details>


### [2] [SuperPass: Fast-Tracking Blocking Threads to Mitigate Priority Inversion on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18097)
*Lei Li,Yu Liang,Riwei Pan,Youcheng Sun,Nan Guan,Tei-Wei Kuo,Chun Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文针对Android系统中频繁发生且严重影响用户体验的优先级反转问题，提出了SuperPass轻量级内核机制，通过快速调度阻塞低优先级线程，将UI线程的99.9%分位阻塞时长降低72.0%，卡顿帧减少29.2%，系统开销仅0.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优先级反转问题在实时系统中已有深入研究，但在通用操作系统（如Android）中的影响尚未充分探索。研究发现Android系统中优先级反转频繁发生，会导致延迟关键线程（如前台应用的UI线程）被低优先级线程阻塞，阻塞时长可达210毫秒，足以导致掉帧，严重影响用户体验。现有的针对实时系统的解决方案无法有效消除Android上的长优先级反转阻塞，且会带来较高开销。

Method: 基于两点关键发现：（1）长时间阻塞主要由低优先级阻塞线程的累积CPU等待时间而非临界区延迟导致；（2）追踪有限数量的并发读者即可在大多数情况下以低开销实现良好响应性。提出了SuperPass轻量级内核机制，引入调度器快速通道，为阻塞延迟关键线程的线程授予立即CPU访问权限，并采用锁级检测器有效识别大多数此类阻塞线程。

Result: 在Google Pixel 8智能手机上的评估显示，以UI线程为例，与默认调度器相比，SuperPass将99.9%分位阻塞时长平均降低72.0%，阻塞次数减少47.7%，卡顿帧减少29.2%，系统级CPU开销仅为0.74%。SuperPass在性能上优于现有方法，包括优先级继承、实时UI提升和代理执行。

Conclusion: SuperPass成功解决了Android系统中的优先级反转问题，显著改善了延迟关键线程的响应性能，大幅降低了UI卡顿，同时保持了极低的系统开销，为移动操作系统的调度优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Priority inversion occurs when a high-priority thread is delayed by a lower-priority one. Although well studied in real-time systems, its impact in general-purpose OSes (e.g., Android) remains underexplored.On Android, we find that priority inversions happen frequently and can delay latency-critical threads, degrading user experience.For example, the foreground app's UI thread is frequently blocked by low-priority threads, with blocking durations of up to 210 ms, enough to cause dropped frames.Existing solutions designed for real-time systems fail to eliminate long priority-inversion blockings on latency-critical threads and may introduce high overhead on Android.
  To solve this problem, we uncover two insights on Android: 1) long blockings are mainly due to the accumulated CPU waiting time of low-priority blocking threads rather than their critical-section latency; and 2) although latency-critical threads can be blocked by many concurrent readers, tracking a limited number of them is sufficient to achieve good responsiveness with low overhead in most cases. Guided by these insights, we propose SuperPass, a lightweight kernel mechanism that mitigates priority inversion by fast-track scheduling of low-priority threads blocking latency-critical threads. It introduces a scheduler fast track that grants immediate CPU access to threads blocking latency-critical threads, and employs a lock-level detector that effectively identifies most such blocking threads.
  We evaluate SuperPasson a Google Pixel 8 smartphone. Taking UI thread as a case study, SuperPass decreases the 99.9th-percentile blocking duration by 72.0% and blocking count by 47.7% on average compared to the default scheduler, and reduces janky frames by 29.2% with a system-wide CPU overhead of only 0.74%. SuperPass also outperforms existing approaches including priority inheritance, real-time UI promotion, and Proxy Execution.

</details>

### [3] [Reproducible Research in Network Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16433)
*Anna M. Ermolayeva,Tatyana R. Velieva,Anna A. Zhivtsova,Anna V. Korolkova,and Dmitry S. Kulyabov*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文针对网络建模中实验数据验证的可靠性问题，提出利用全尺寸软件模型替代真实设备进行自然实验的方法，通过对主流网络建模软件包进行比较分析，为获取可靠实验数据提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络建模过程中，研究人员面临获取实验数据以验证模型方法的困难，即使存在部分实验数据，也需要确保其可靠性。因此迫切需要提出能够获取可靠实验数据的方法，以支持网络模型的验证工作。

Method: 基于网络设备本质上是软硬件综合体这一特性，论文提出全尺寸软件模型可以被视为与真实设备完全等价，因此可以用自然实验替代真实实验。该方法的核心在于通过自然实验的可重复性来保证实验数据的可靠性，并对主流的自然网络建模软件包进行了系统性的比较分析。

Result: 研究对主流的自然网络建模软件包进行了比较分析，从功能性和可重复研究的可行性两个维度对这些软件包进行了分类和评估，发现大多数软件包都能够满足可重复性的标准要求。

Conclusion: 大多数软件包均能满足可重复性标准，具体选择哪种解决方案主要取决于非技术性因素，包括软件包的流行程度以及研究团队对该软件包的熟悉程度。这为研究人员选择合适的网络建模工具提供了实用的指导建议。

Abstract: Background: When we model networks, there is a problem of obtaining experimental data to verify other model approaches. And even if there are some experimental data, it is necessary to be sure of their reliability. Purpose: It is necessary to propose methods for obtaining reliable experimental data. Method: By its nature, network equipment is a software and hardware complex. Therefore, a full-scale software model can be considered completely equivalent to real equipment. And a real experiment can be replaced by a nature experiment. The reliability of a nature experiment will be based on its reproducibility. Results A comparison of popular nature network modeling packages was carried out. These packages were divided by functionality and feasibility of reproducible studies. Conclusions: Most software packages meet the reproducibility criteria. The choice of a specific solution depends on non-technical factors: popularity and knowledge of the package.

</details>


### [4] [Robust KV Cache Management for LLM Serving under Output Token Length Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16892)
*Jiaming Cheng,Duong The Do,Duong Tung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM服务系统的KV缓存管理框架，通过分布鲁棒优化方法解决输出token长度不确定性问题，在保证延迟SLO的同时显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务系统中，KV缓存内存是主要瓶颈。核心挑战在于：请求到达时必须预留KV缓存，但输出token长度直到生成完成才能确定。预留不足会导致请求抢占（强制终止和重新计算，产生显著开销），而过度预留则浪费内存并降低吞吐量。这造成了内存效率与抢占风险之间的核心权衡问题。

Method: 提出一个鲁棒的KV缓存管理框架，联合优化：GPU并行配置、每请求类的KV缓存预留、跨异构服务组的请求路由、共享提示的前缀缓存。框架纳入延迟SLO约束，捕获内存分配、吞吐量和排队延迟之间的交互。为应对输出token长度不确定性和负载分布漂移，采用Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化(DRO)方法，并开发了可扩展的块坐标下降算法求解混合整数问题。分析揭示了关键的 fractile 结构，能够自动调整预留分位数以适应不同的抢占和内存成本场景，无需手动调参。

Result: 在BurstGPT、Azure和ShareGPT等生产环境LLM负载轨迹上的评估显示：与固定分位数预留基线相比，成本降低高达56%，同时在多样化操作场景中保持竞争性的P99延迟、goodput和SLO违规率表现。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了KV缓存预留中的不确定性问题，通过分布鲁棒优化实现了内存效率与抢占风险的平衡，无需手动调参即可自动适应不同成本场景，为LLM服务系统提供了更优的资源管理方案。

Abstract: KV cache memory is a primary bottleneck in modern LLM serving systems deployed on GPU clusters. A fundamental challenge is that the KV cache must be reserved upon request arrival, while the output token length remains unknown until generation completes. Under-reservation triggers preemption -- forcing termination and recomputation of requests and incurring significant overhead -- whereas over-reservation wastes memory and reduces throughput. This creates a central trade-off between memory efficiency and preemption risk. We present a robust KV cache management framework for LLM serving that jointly optimizes GPU parallelism configuration, KV cache reservation per request class, request routing across heterogeneous serving groups, and prefix caching for shared prompts. The framework incorporates latency SLO constraints and captures the interaction between memory allocation, throughput, and queueing delay. To address output token length uncertainty and workload distribution shift, we develop a Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) formulation together with a scalable block coordinate descent algorithm for the resulting mixed-integer problem. Our analysis reveals a critical fractile structure that automatically adapts reservation quantiles to different preemption and memory cost regimes without manual tuning. Trace-driven evaluation on production LLM workloads, including BurstGPT, Azure, and ShareGPT traces, demonstrates up to 56\% lower cost than fixed-quantile reservation baselines while maintaining competitive P99 latency, goodput, and SLO violation rates across diverse operating regimes.

</details>


### [5] [PERA: A Perceive-Reason-Act Interface Bridging Sensing, Cognitive Reasoning, and Trustworthy Agentic Response for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16896)
*Mohammad Farzanullah,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成式网络智能（Generative Network Intelligence）概念，通过感知-推理-行动（PERA）范式，将无线信道和网络状态作为连续多模态叙事处理，实现了下一代网络的自主智能，并在链路状态分类和波束预测案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法局限于单任务处理管道，无法处理复杂的跨层动态问题；大型语言模型虽然在认知推理方面表现出色，但缺乏对无线遥测数据的感知能力；下一代网络需要从预编程协议工程转向自主、可信的智能范式。

Method: 提出PERA（Perceive-Reason-Act）范式，将无线信道和网络状态视为连续多模态叙事；同步大型无线AI模型（LWAMs）的感知基础与LLMs的认知推理；设计面向资源受限无线边缘的三层架构，用高效的多任务架构替代分散的任务特定边缘模型。

Result: 案例研究验证了该方法在链路状态分类和波束预测任务中的有效性；通过将无线遥测数据嵌入认知引擎，实现了透明、可读的推理过程，为物理层诊断和波束控制提供了可信的解释。

Conclusion: PERA范式为下一代网络实现了原生智能，通过统一智能架构替代了分散的任务特定模型，在降低复杂度和能耗的同时提供实时控制能力，为完全自主、具身化的智能代理AI奠定了基础框架。

Abstract: The realization of next-generation (NG) networks hinges on a fundamental departure from preprogrammed protocol engineering towards a paradigm of self-consciously evolving, autonomous and trusted intelligence. While conventional machine learning (ML) has introduced localized automation, it remains inherently bounded by single-task processing pipelines incapable of handling complex cross-layer dynamics. As a partial remedy, large language models (LLMs) excel at generalized cognitive reasoning, but to a degree they remain detached from the rich modalities of wireless telemetry. As a solution, we unveil Generative Network Intelligence conceptualized via the Perceive-Reason-Act (PERA) paradigm. This paradigm treats the wireless channel and the underlying network states as a continuous, multimodal narrative. By synchronizing the perceptual grounding of Large Wireless AI Models (LWAMs) with the cognitive reasoning of LLMs, PERA heralds the era of native NG intelligence. Crucially, this unified intelligence replaces fragmented, task-specific edge models by an efficient multi-task architecture delivering the real-time control needed for supporting dynamic physical applications while reducing both the complexity and energy dissipation. Moreover, we contrast the structural limitations of traditional ML to generative paradigms, conceive agentic reasoning across a NG protocol stack, and detail a practical three-tier design specifically engineered for the resource-constrained wireless edge. This architectural paradigm serves as a foundational framework for realizing fully autonomous, embodied agentic AI in NG networks. To validate this vision, our case study evaluates link-state classification and beam prediction, demonstrating how grounding wireless telemetry within a cognitive engine delivers the transparent, human-readable rationales required for trusted physical-layer diagnostics and beam control.

</details>


### [6] [A Multi-Agent System for 5G Throughput Prediction in Multi-Operator Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16930)
*Muhammad Kabeer,Rosdiadee Nordin,Nadiva Nuriftitah,Sian Lun Lau*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出TMAS分层多智能体系统用于6G网络吞吐量预测，在真实数据集上实现R²=0.931、MAE=0.53 Mbps的高精度预测，推理延迟仅0.004-0.126 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单体机器学习模型在跨运营商、移动模式和流量类型等异构城市环境中难以泛化，信号条件与可实现吞吐量之间存在关键的随机性差距，无法满足AI驱动的6G资源编排需求。

Method: 提出分层多智能体系统（TMAS），将边缘遥测数据动态路由至上下文感知的域微智能体。在马来西亚双威城收集48,618个样本数据集，涵盖三个Tier-1移动网络运营商、三种移动模式（高架人行道、地面穿梭巴士、高架快速公交）和三种流量模式（持续下载、持续上传、自适应视频流）。

Result: TMAS突破可预测性瓶颈，实现最高R²=0.931的确定系数和最低0.53 Mbps的平均绝对误差。系统具有高效的操作性能：微智能体训练时间短、推理延迟低、智能路由开销仅0.004至0.126毫秒。

Conclusion: TMAS架构的低延迟特性表明其是下一代无线网络所需响应时间的有前景的候选方案，能够有效支持AI驱动的6G资源编排中的吞吐量预测需求。

Abstract: Throughput prediction is foundational for artificial intelligence-driven 6G resource orchestration. Conventional monolithic machine learning models struggle to generalize across diverse operators, mobility modes, and traffic types, leaving a critical stochasticity gap between signal conditions and achievable throughput. To overcome these constraints in heterogeneous urban environments, we propose a Tiered Multi-Agent System (TMAS) that dynamically routes edge telemetry to context-aware Domain Micro-Agents, validated on a dataset of 48,618 samples collected in Sunway City, Malaysia, with Nemo Handy drive test software, spanning three Tier-1 mobile network operators, three mobility modes, namely (i) elevated pedestrian walkway, (ii) ground-level shuttle bus, and (iii) elevated bus rapid transit; and three traffic profiles, namely (i) persistent download, (ii) persistent upload, and (iii) adaptive video streaming. Our evaluations reveal that TMAS overcomes predictability bottlenecks, achieving a coefficient of determination (R2) of up to 0.931 and a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) as low as 0.53 Mbps. The system demonstrates high operational efficiency, with rapid micro-agent training times, low inference latencies, and agentic routing overhead of 0.004 to 0.126 ms. These latency characteristics indicate the architecture is a promising candidate for the response times required by next-generation wireless networks.

</details>


### [7] [OrderMoE: An expert similarity driven distributed edge MoE inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17154)
*Xin Yuan,Ning Li,Quan Chen,Wenchao Xu,Athanasios V. Vasilakos,Song Guo,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出OrderMoE框架，通过利用专家相似性来优化边缘环境下的MoE模型推理，在保证可控质量损失的前提下显著降低延迟和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式MoE服务方法主要依赖精确的专家放置、缓存、复制或通信调度，而忽视了专家之间的功能相似性。这种相似性为减少跨服务器令牌传输提供了机会，特别是在资源受限和带宽受限的边缘基础设施上部署MoE推理时尤为重要。

Method: OrderMoE首先基于路由器诱导的logits表示构建专家相似性模型，将每个MoE层的专家划分为多个相似性组；然后开发相似性感知的专家分组和部署策略以提高边缘服务器的本地相似性覆盖率；最后设计质量感知和轨迹感知的运行时服务器-专家选择算法，动态决定令牌是调用远程目标专家还是使用可行的本地替代专家。

Result: 在真实分布式边缘测试平台上的实验结果表明，OrderMoE显著降低了平均延迟、尾延迟、跨服务器流量和远程专家调用比率，同时仅引入了小的且可控的推理质量下降。

Conclusion: OrderMoE成功实现了边缘MoE推理加速，有效平衡了推理延迟、通信开销、服务器工作负载和推理质量之间的权衡，证明了利用专家相似性进行分布式MoE部署的有效性。

Abstract: Although mixture-of-experts, MoE, models have been increasingly adopted to scale large language models with moderate computation cost, it remains challenging to deploy MoE inference over resource-constrained and bandwidth-limited edge infrastructures. Existing distributed MoE serving methods mainly rely on exact expert placement, caching, replication, or communication scheduling, while overlooking the functional similarity among experts, which provides an opportunity to reduce cross-server token transmission. Therefore, this paper introduces a similarity-aware expert allocation and distributed deployment framework, dubbed OrderMoE, which aims to accelerate edge MoE inference while balancing inference latency, communication overhead, server workload, and inference quality. OrderMoE first constructs an expert similarity model based on router-induced logits representations and partitions experts in each MoE layer into multiple similarity groups. Then, it develops a similarity-aware expert grouping and deployment strategy to improve local similarity coverage across edge servers. Since reducing remote expert invocation and preserving exact inference quality are conflicting objectives, OrderMoE further designs a quality-aware and trajectory-aware runtime server-expert selection algorithm to decide whether a token should invoke its remote target expert or use a feasible local substitute expert. Experimental results on a real distributed edge testbed show that OrderMoE significantly reduces average latency, tail latency, cross-server traffic, and remote expert invocation ratio, while introducing only small and controllable inference quality degradation.

</details>


### [8] [DAN-Scheduler: Deterministic Three-Stage Co-Optimization of Scheduling, Memory Layout, and Pipeline Overlap for General-Purpose NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17422)
*Runhao Liu,Peng Zheng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了DAN-Scheduler，一个面向NPU的确定性离线调度和编译优化框架，通过三阶段协同优化算子重排序、内存布局和流水线重叠，显著降低了峰值内存、DDR流量和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经处理单元（NPU）在内存受限的高吞吐量推理场景中广泛应用，但其层次化片上内存和异构计算/数据移动引擎使得执行顺序、内存放置和流水线重叠紧密耦合。现有编译流程通常独立优化这些维度，导致片上驻留过多、不必要的片外流量以及流水线利用率不足。

Method: 提出DAN-Scheduler框架，包含三个协同优化阶段：1) 内存压力感知拓扑调度（MPAS）重排序算子以缩短张量生命周期并降低峰值片上内存；2) 确定性线性重打包（DLR）构建无冲突内存布局，并应用层级感知、成本感知的溢出启发式以减少碎片化和片外流量；3) 关键路径增强（CPE）在保持前两阶段建立的内存行为的同时改善计算-DMA重叠。

Result: 在六个来自真实DaVinci NPU的算子级DAG上评估，与四个强基线相比，DAN-Scheduler在全部24个工作负载-指标单元上取得最佳或并列最佳结果，相比最佳外部竞争者平均降低峰值内存18.3%、额外DDR流量20.4%、溢出次数14.2%和执行时间16.3%；相对原始调度分别降低38.3%、62.0%、64.9%和57.5%。

Conclusion: 确定性分阶段协同优化对内存受限的NPU执行是有效的，证明了在NPU编译优化中统一考虑执行顺序、内存布局和流水线重叠的重要性。

Abstract: Neural Processing Units (NPUs) are increasingly deployed for high-throughput, memory-constrained inference, yet their hierarchical on-chip memories and heterogeneous compute and data-movement engines tightly couple execution order, memory placement, and pipeline overlap. Existing compiler flows often optimize these dimensions separately, causing excessive on-chip residency, unnecessary off-chip traffic, and underutilized pipelines.We present DAN-Scheduler, a deterministic offline scheduling and compiler optimization framework for intra-core NPU execution. It co-optimizes these decisions in three stages. Memory-Pressure-Aware Topological Scheduling (MPAS) reorders operators to shorten tensor lifetimes and reduce peak on-chip memory usage. Deterministic Linear Repackaging (DLR) builds conflict-free memory layouts and applies a tier-aware, cost-aware spill heuristic to reduce fragmentation and off-chip traffic under limited capacity. Critical Path Enhancement (CPE) improves compute-DMA overlap while preserving the memory behavior established by the first two stages.
  We evaluate DAN-Scheduler on six trace-derived operator-level DAGs collected from a real Davinci NPU and replayed on a generalized NPU execution model. Against four strong external baselines, DAN-Scheduler achieves the best or tied-best result on all 24 workload-metric cells, reducing peak memory, extra DDR traffic, spill count, and makespan by 18.3%, 20.4%, 14.2%, and 16.3% on average over the best external competitor. Relative to the original schedule, it reduces the same metrics by 38.3%, 62.0%, 64.9%, and 57.5%. These results show that deterministic stage-wise co-optimization is effective for memory-constrained NPU execution. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MICRO2026-5C74

</details>


### [9] [Diverge-Merge Formation and MAC Control in Structured Airspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17489)
*Kai Xiong,Xingyu Wu,Ba Zhang,Li Wei,Min Zeng,Supeng Leng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对走廊式空域中的无人机编队分流与合流问题，提出了任务驱动的控制框架和集群感知TDMA协议，实现了高密度交通下的实时编队重构和可靠通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编队飞行虽能提高走廊式空域容量，但在匝道分流、合流和拥堵场景下变得低效或不安全，亟需一种能够支持编队灵活重构的控制机制。

Method: 建立走廊-匝道分流结构空域模型，设计任务驱动的快速聚类机制实现实时分流合流，并提出集群感知分布式TDMA（CAD-TDMA）协议保障MAC层通信。

Result: 所提算法在严重物理重叠和拥堵下保持接近零的几何误分类；CAD-TDMA在延迟-丢包-吞吐权衡上优于固定TDMA和WiFi MAC。

Conclusion: 提出的编队控制框架能够协同支持走廊-匝道结构空域中的实时编队重构和可靠通信，有效解决高密度UAV交通管理问题。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of advanced air mobility (AAM) makes corridor-based structured airspace a promising infrastructure for high-density unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) traffic. Formation flight can improve corridor capacity by suppressing shockwave propagation, but rigid formations become inefficient or unsafe during ramp branching, merging, and congestion. To address this problem, this paper proposes a task-driven diverge-merge control framework for UAV formations in structured airspace. At the beginning, a corridor-ramp branching structured airspace model is established to characterize the traffic dynamics and spatial constraints. Building upon this, a fast task-driven clustering mechanism integrates spatial connectivity, flight intent, and aerial task interactions to enable real-time diverge and merge for ramp branching and traffic reshaping. To make the diverge-merge reconfigurations executable at the media access control (MAC) layer of the formation, a cluster-aware distributed time division multiple access (CAD-TDMA) protocol is further designed. It protects intra-cluster control synchronization while conservatively reusing low-risk inter-cluster slots. Simulation results show that the proposed diverge-merge algorithm maintains near-zero geometrical misclassification under severe physical overlapping and congestion. With the formation diverge-merge traces, CAD-TDMA achieves the best delay--loss--throughput tradeoff over fixed TDMA and WiFi MAC. It shows that the proposed formation control framework can jointly support real-time formation reconfiguration and reliable communication in corridor-ramp structured airspace.

</details>


### [10] [Self-Directed Spectrum Allocation Framework for Integrated TN-NTN 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17561)
*Vaskar Chakma,Wooyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Q学习的自适应信道分配框架，通过多目标奖励函数联合优化系统吞吐量、用户公平性和干扰抑制，仿真结果显示相比随机分配，干扰降低26.3%，公平性指数达到0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络环境中存在动态变化的网络负载、干扰条件和时序流量模式，传统信道分配方法难以自适应地响应这些动态变化，需要一种能够学习最优策略的自适应框架来优化系统性能。

Method: 采用基于Q学习的自适应信道分配框架，将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），智能体通过观察网络负载、干扰条件和时序流量动态来学习最优策略；设计多目标奖励函数联合优化系统吞吐量、用户公平性和干扰抑制；采用ε-贪婪策略促进有效探索。

Result: 仿真结果显示算法稳定收敛，平均奖励达到37.5，平均吞吐量为28.5 Mbps；Jain公平性指数达到0.75；相比随机分配方法，干扰降低26.3%，能够有效适应动态流量模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Q学习的自适应信道分配框架能够有效响应动态流量模式，在系统吞吐量、用户公平性和干扰抑制方面取得了良好的平衡，验证了强化学习方法在无线网络信道分配中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a self-adaptive channel assignment framework based on Q-learning, where agents learn optimal policies by observing network load, interference conditions, and temporal traffic dynamics within a Markov decision process (MDP). A multi-objective reward function is designed to jointly optimize system throughput, user fairness, and interference mitigation, while an ε-greedy strategy is employed to facilitate effective exploration. Simulation results demonstrate stable convergence, achieving an average reward of 37.5 and an average throughput of 28.5 Mbps. Moreover, the proposed approach achieves a Jain's fairness index of 0.75 and reduces interference by 26.3% compared to random allocation by adaptively responding to dynamic traffic patterns.

</details>


### [11] [Token Communications (TokCom): A Unified AI-Native Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17628)
*Yaru Fu,Liang Ji,Sabita Maharjan,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出TokCom框架，将大语言模型的token提升为6G网络信息交换的基本单元，以适应从传统以用户为中心向以AI智能体为中心的网络范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从静态感知演变为生成式推理和自主智能体，传统的以可靠比特级重构为核心的Shannon范式与新兴的以互联AI智能体为主要用户的网络场景日益不匹配，亟需新的通信理论基础。

Method: 提出Token通信（TokCom）框架，将token作为6G网络信息交换的基本实体；分析从传统通信系统到TokCom的架构转型，识别关键挑战并提出潜在解决方案；通过实际案例展示异构语言模型间token共享的有效性。

Result: 证明了token在异构语言模型间共享的有效性和实用价值，展示了TokCom框架在AI原生通信场景下的可行性。

Conclusion: TokCom为6G网络提供了适合AI原生、token驱动的通信范式，为未来研究指明了方向，有望成为6G时代AI智能体互联通信的理论基础。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) evolves from static perception to generative reasoning and autonomous agency, the fundamental principles of wireless communications are undergoing a paradigm shift. The classical Shannon paradigm, centered on reliable bit-level reconstruction for users, is increasingly misaligned with an emerging scenario in which the primary users of the network are interconnected AI agents. This article introduces token communications (TokCom), a novel framework that elevates tokens, i.e., the fundamental processing units of large language models (LLMs), to first-class entities for information exchange in the sixth generation wireless cellular networks (6G). We first examine the architectural transition from conventional communication systems to TokCom and identify the key challenges in implementing this transition, along with potential solution approaches. Thereafter, we present a practical case study to demonstrate the effectiveness of token sharing among heterogeneous language models. Finally, we outline promising future research directions toward realizing an AI-native, token-driven communication paradigm suitable for 6G.

</details>


### [12] [Mobile Network Control with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17747)
*Maxime Bouton,Ioanna Mitsioni,Simon Lindståhl,Jaeseong Jeong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于世界模型的移动网络控制方法，通过利用模型的不确定性估计实现网络参数的鲁棒优化配置，在仿真和实际网络数据中均表现出比传统方法和强化学习方法更优的能效与服务质量平衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动网络复杂度的不断增加，传统控制策略难以实现高效、节能的网络管理，亟需一种智能化、动态化的控制方法来适应网络状态变化并优化配置参数。

Method: 提出一种基于世界模型的网络控制方法：从历史数据训练世界模型以预测动作对未来网络状态的影响；控制器利用模型的不确定性估计鲁棒地寻找最优网络配置；优化目标可动态调整而无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在移动网络节能特性的仿真闭环控制实验中，该方法在平衡节能与服务质量方面优于传统方法和强化学习方法；在真实网络数据上验证了世界模型的性能，并在不同吞吐量约束下评估了控制器提出的反事实动作。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的控制方法能够有效实现移动网络的自适应配置与节能管理，具备目标灵活可变、无需重训练的优势，为复杂移动网络的智能化控制提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of mobile networks necessitates intelligent and dynamic control strategies for efficient, energy-conserving management. We propose a world model-based approach for network control that enables adaptive configuration of crucial parameters. The world model is trained from historical data and predicts the impact of its actions on future network states. Our controller leverages the model's uncertainty estimate to robustly find optimal network configuration changes. Furthermore, the optimization objective can be changed dynamically without model retraining. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in simulated closed-loop control of a mobile network energy-saving feature. Our results show improved performance in balancing energy savings with quality of service, compared to traditional methods and reinforcement learning approaches. Finally, we show the world model performance on real network data from, and evaluate counterfactual actions proposed by the controller under various throughput constraints.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Car Sharing: Uncertainty-Aware Pooling of Vehicular Compute at the Network Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17893)
*Wellington Lobato,Nadjib Achir,Aline Carneiro Viana*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMART，一种面向移动边缘计算（MEC）编排器的不确定性感知准入机制，用于在预测不确定性下机会性地利用车辆计算资源。该方法通过贝叶斯神经网络进行容量预测，并将校准的预测不确定性纳入机会约束准入程序。实验表明，SMART在保持低违规率的同时实现了高任务准入率，优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网车辆越来越多地嵌入AI加速器，为网络边缘提供了一个巨大但波动的补充计算来源。与预配置的MEC主机不同，车辆资源具有高度动态性：车辆可能离开小区、被本地占用或提供异构的计算能力。因此，利用车辆资源需要在任务执行期间可用计算容量未知的情况下做出准入决策。

Method: SMART使用贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）预测未来的车辆容量——其不确定性估计在标称95%水平下是所有评估预测器中校准最好的——并将校准的预测不确定性纳入机会约束准入程序，通过样本平均近似（SAA）和条件风险价值（CVaR）近似进行重构。

Result: 在纯计算准入模型下，SMART准入95.6%的任务，同时保持约0.81%的中位容量违规率。与七个反应式、仅均值和不确定性感知基线相比，SMART实现了有利的准入-违规权衡，在建模假设下接近计算容量预言机的性能。敏感性分析表明，基站计算可用性的变化是准入性能的关键决定因素。

Conclusion: 研究强调了需要一种针对机会性基站计算池量身定制的校准准入和资源分配机制。SMART成功地在预测不确定性下实现了对车辆计算资源的有效利用，为动态边缘计算环境中的资源管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Connected vehicles increasingly embed AI accelerators, offering a substantial yet volatile source of supplemental compute near the network edge. Unlike provisioned MEC hosts, vehicular resources are highly dynamic: vehicles may leave the cell, become locally occupied, or offer heterogeneous compute capacities. Therefore, exploiting vehicular resources requires making admission decisions without knowing the compute capacity that will be available during task execution. We present SMART, an uncertainty-aware admission mechanism that enables an \acs{ETSI} \ac{MEC} orchestrator \textit{to opportunistically exploit vehicular compute under predictive uncertainty.} SMART predicts future vehicular capacity using a \ac{BNN} -- whose uncertainty estimates are the best calibrated among the evaluated forecasters at the nominal 95\% level, and incorporates its calibrated predictive uncertainty into a chance-constrained admission program reformulated through \ac{SAA} and \ac{CVaR} approximations. Under a compute-only admission model, SMART admits 95.6\% of tasks while maintaining a median capacity-violation rate of about 0.81\%. It achieves a favorable admission-violation tradeoff compared with seven reactive, mean-only, and uncertainty-aware baselines, by approaching the performance of a compute-capacity oracle under the modeled assumptions. Finally, the sensitivity analyses show that variability in base-station compute availability is a key determinant of admission performance, highlighting the need for a calibrated admission and resource allocation mechanism tailored to opportunistic base-station compute pooling.

</details>


### [14] [Enhanced Dynamic Beamwidth Selection-based THz MAC Protocol for Wireless Data Center Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17996)
*Muhammad Absaruddin,Saim Ghafoor,Mubashir Husain Rehmani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了DBS-ADAPT和EDBS-ADAPT两种动态波束宽度选择MAC协议，通过根据节点距离动态调整天线波束宽度，解决了太赫兹无线数据中心通信中固定波束宽度带来的性能权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有太赫兹MAC协议采用固定波束宽度，存在关键性能权衡：窄波束提高长距离链路增益但降低短距离吞吐量，宽波束有益短链路但劣化长距离性能。太赫兹信号高路径损耗需要定向天线，但引入 deafness 和同步挑战。

Method: 提出DBS-ADAPT协议，根据节点间距离动态调整天线波束宽度，最大化吞吐量同时保持链路距离；提出增强版EDBS-ADAPT，进一步减少波束切换和控制开销。使用NS-3太赫兹模块进行仿真评估。

Result: DBS-ADAPT相比基线ADAPT-3协议平均吞吐量提升高达22%，延迟降低高达10%。EDBS-ADAPT将波束切换开销减少95%，在吞吐量增益基础上进一步提高效率。

Conclusion: 动态波束宽度选择方法有效解决了太赫兹无线数据中心通信中的性能权衡问题，EDBS-ADAPT协议在保持吞吐量提升的同时显著降低开销，适用于可扩展和高性能无线数据中心环境。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) wireless communication offers a promising alternative to traditional wired links in data centres (DCs), enabling ultra-high data rates, low latency, and greater scalability. However, THz signals suffer from high path loss, necessitating the use of directional antennas (DAs). While DAs enhance signal strength, they introduce challenges such as deafness and synchronisation, typically addressed through receiver-initiated MAC protocols. Most existing THz MAC protocols use fixed beamwidths, which results in a key performance trade-off: narrow beams improve gain for long-range links but reduce throughput for short distances due to increased alignment overhead, while wide beams benefit short links but degrade performance over longer distances. To overcome this limitation, we propose DBS-ADAPT, a dynamic beamwidth selection-based MAC protocol that adjusts the antenna beamwidth according to the distance between nodes, maximising throughput without compromising link range. We also introduce an enhanced version, EDBS-ADAPT, which further reduces beamwidth switching and control overhead while preserving throughput gains. Both protocols are evaluated using the NS-3 THz module. Simulation results show that DBS-ADAPT improves average throughput by up to 22% and reduces delay up to 10% compared to the baseline ADAPT-3 protocol. EDBS-ADAPT further cuts beamwidth switching overhead by 95%, making it more efficient for scalable and high-performance wireless DC environments.

</details>


### [15] [Human Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models for Optical Network Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18068)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HuGLEN评估框架，用于比较和排序LLM在网络自动化任务中的表现，发现中等规模（12B参数）的LLM在解释质量与效率之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在网络自动化中的应用日益增加，但不同LLM家族之间的输出质量和推理成本差异显著，需要一种可扩展且可复现的方法来比较和选择适合的LLM模型。

Method: 提出HuGLEN评估管道，采用LLM-as-a-judge方法结合少量专家评分，通过质量效率评分（QES）对候选LLM进行可扩展、可复现的比较和排序；在光网络质量传输（QoT）估计任务中，将可解释AI（XAI）模型的输出转化为操作员友好的解释。

Result: 实验结果表明，中等规模的LLM（12B参数）获得了最高的QES分数，在解释质量和推理效率之间达到了最佳权衡。

Conclusion: HuGLEN有效减少了人工标注负担，同时为面向操作员的自动化任务提供了 consistent 的模型选择支持，证明了中等规模模型在特定任务中可能比更大模型更具实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted for network automation, yet their output quality and inference cost can vary substantially across LLM families. We present HuGLEN, a stepwise evaluation pipeline that uses an LLM-as-a-judge together with a small set of expert ratings to enable scalable and reproducible comparison of candidate LLMs, and to rank them using a quality efficiency score (QES). We demonstrate HuGLEN for translating outputs from an explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) model for the optical network quality of transmission (QoT) estimation task into operator-friendly explanations. Our results show that a medium-sized LLM (12B parameters) achieves the highest QES, indicating the best trade-off between explanation quality and efficiency. Overall, HuGLEN reduces the human-labeling burden while supporting consistent model selection for operator-facing automation tasks.

</details>


### [16] [ClouDens: Operational Context-Aware Anomaly Detection for Large-scale Cloud System Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18127)
*Thu T. H. Doan,Mohammad Saiful Islam,Andriy Miranskyy,Ngoc-Thanh Nguyen,Rogardt Heldal,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClouDens框架，通过利用操作上下文属性和时空图神经网络来改进大规模云系统的异常检测，在IBM云遥测数据集上实现了更准确、更早的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云计算基础设施规模和复杂性的快速增长，大规模云系统（LCSs）的网络监控变得日益困难，需要自动化且可靠的异常检测来维持服务可用性。现有方法面临极端维度、分布式组件间复杂依赖关系以及间歇性活跃服务导致的严重稀疏性等挑战。

Method: 提出ClouDens异常检测框架：1）将高维遥测日志划分为领域引导的子集；2）构建上下文感知图来建模操作服务依赖关系；3）采用时空图神经网络进行基于预测的异常检测。该框架利用遥测日志模式中编码的操作上下文属性来提高检测准确性。

Result: 在IBM云遥测数据集上的实验表明，ClouDens在基于计数的遥测特征上获得更高的NAB分数，表明相比GRU模型具有更准确、更早的异常检测和更广泛的覆盖范围。研究还发现遥测特征子集、操作上下文建模、评分策略和稀疏性插补都显著影响检测性能。

Conclusion: ClouDens框架成功解决了大规模云系统监控中的关键挑战，为设计可靠的异常检测解决方案提供了实用指导，并为公平基准测试异常检测方法提供了重要见解。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of cloud computing infrastructures in scale and complexity, network monitoring for Large-scale Cloud Systems (LCSs) has become increasingly challenging, requiring automated and reliable anomaly detection to maintain service availability. Modern LCSs continuously generate telemetry logs from distributed cloud services, producing high-dimensional multivariate time series that capture system operations. Detecting anomalies in this context is difficult due to extreme dimensionality, complex dependencies among distributed components, and severe sparsity from intermittently active services. Taking these challenges into account, we first conduct an empirical study on telemetry logs from the IBM Cloud Console platform, and then propose ClouDens, an anomaly detection framework tailored to LCS monitoring that leverages operational-context attributes encoded in the telemetry log schema to improve detection accuracy and early identification of anomalies. ClouDens partitions high-dimensional telemetry logs into domain-guided subsets, constructs a context-aware graph modeling operational service dependencies, and employs Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for forecasting-based anomaly detection. We evaluate ClouDens on the recently released IBM Cloud Telemetry Dataset and provide practical insights into designing reliable anomaly detection solutions for LCS monitoring. Results show ClouDens achieves higher NAB scores in count-based telemetry features, indicating more accurate, earlier anomaly detection with broader coverage than a GRU-based model. Our study further reveals that telemetry feature subsets, operational-context modeling, scoring strategies, and sparsity imputation all substantially influence detection performance, offering practical guidance for designing and fairly benchmarking anomaly detection approaches for LCS monitoring.

</details>


### [17] [AI Agent Communications in AI-Native 6G Network: Status, Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18138)
*Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文评估了AI原生6G网络架构对AI智能体通信SOVA框架的支持能力，识别了二者间的差距，并提出了确保6G原生支持AI智能体通信的研发方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理AI和多智能体系统的快速发展，AI智能体通信成为未来互联网的基础需求。虽然已出现多种智能体通信协议，但这些方案面临互操作性危机和基础设施缺口问题。新提出的基于服务导向虚拟化架构(SOVA)为解决这些挑战提供了框架，而新兴的AI原生6G网络有望成为SOVA框架的有力支撑，但其有效性尚未得到充分评估。需要弥合AI原生6G网络与AI智能体通信两个研究轨迹之间的鸿沟。

Method: 系统调查当前和拟议的6G网络架构及协议规范对SOVA框架的支持能力；批判性审查6G的关键架构范式及其满足SOVA需求的潜力；通过差距分析识别6G标准与AI智能体通信需求之间的缺口。

Result: 识别出6G网络架构与SOVA框架需求之间存在的具体差距；明确了当前6G标准和协议规范在支持AI智能体通信方面的不足之处；建立了AI原生6G网络与AI智能体通信之间的映射关系分析。

Conclusion: 论文提出了确保未来6G网络能够在代理AI时代原生赋能AI智能体通信的研究与开发方向；强调了6G网络需要针对AI智能体通信的特定需求进行架构优化和协议完善；为AI原生6G网络与AI智能体通信的融合发展指明了技术路径。

Abstract: The rapid development of agentic AI and multi-agent systems is establishing AI agent communication as a fundamental requirement for the future Internet. While a diverse array of agent communication protocols has recently emerged, these solutions currently suffer from interoperability crises and infrastructure gaps. The newly proposed Service-Oriented Virtualization-Based Architecture (SOVA) offers an architectural framework to address these challenges for agent communication, which expects seamless support from the network infrastructure. The emerging AI-native 6G network is promising as a robust foundation for the SOVA framework, thereby greatly facilitating AI agent communication; however, its effectiveness in supporting the SOVA framework has yet to be fully assessed. To bridge the distinct research trajectories of AI-native 6G networks and AI agent communications, this paper investigates the capabilities of current and proposed 6G network architectures and protocol specifications for supporting the SOVA framework for AI agent communications. By critically examining 6G's key architectural paradigms and their potential to fulfill SOVA's requirements, this paper identifies gaps between 6G standards and the demands of AI agent communication. Based on this gap analysis, this paper outlines research and development directions to ensure that the future 6G network can natively empower AI agent communications in the era of agentic AI.

</details>

### [18] [On Hardware-Aware Design and Optimization of Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16297)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Di Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了边缘智能系统的硬件感知设计与优化方法，通过模型压缩和神经网络架构搜索等技术解决深度学习模型复杂性和边缘设备异构性带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统结合了边缘计算和人工智能，推动了AI应用的前沿发展。然而，深度学习模型的复杂性和边缘设备的异构性使得系统设计具有挑战性。传统的硬件无关方法在实现边缘系统时面临局限性，因此硬件感知方法受到越来越多的关注。

Method: 深入研究硬件感知设计与优化技术，重点关注模型压缩和神经网络架构搜索等方法，以实现高效且有效的系统设计。

Result: 提出了在边缘智能系统中应用硬件感知方法的近期研究成果，通过模型压缩和神经网络架构搜索等技术实现了系统的效率优化。

Conclusion: 硬件感知方法为边缘智能系统设计提供了有效的解决方案，但仍存在一些挑战需要进一步研究和解决。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems, the intersection of edge computing and artificial intelligence (AI), are pushing the frontier of AI applications. However, the complexity of deep learning models and heterogeneity of edge devices make the design of edge intelligence systems a challenging task. Hardware-agnostic methods face some limitations when implementing edge systems. Thus, hardware-aware methods are attracting more attention recently. In this paper, we present our recent endeavors in hardware-aware design and optimization for edge intelligence. We delve into techniques such as model compression and neural architecture search to achieve efficient and effective system designs. We also discuss some challenges in hardware-aware paradigm.

</details>


### [19] [Enabling Spatially Fine-Grained DVFS in Neural Processing Units for Energy-Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16473)
*Yuqi Xue,Jerry Wu,Corey Yu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出eNPU，通过组件级细粒度DVFS技术，在保证SLO的前提下实现LLM服务能耗降低25.8%-35.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NPU为满足大语言模型(LLMs)不断增长的算力需求而快速发展，其功耗正成为限制因素。研究表明利用DVFS来利用服务级目标(SLO)松弛是提高LLM服务NPU能效的有效途径。由于LLM中的张量算子在NPU组件间表现出多样化的瓶颈，需要为每个组件单独配置频率以最大化能效。

Method: 1. 重构NPU核心流水线，将组件划分到独立的电压/频率域；
2. 引入轻量级跨域通信机制以降低组件间同步开销；
3. 扩展NPU指令集以实现亚微秒级DVFS控制；
4. 采用编译器驱动的两级贪婪搜索算法，在SLO约束下协同优化指令调度和组件级电压/频率选择。

Result: 在TPUv4芯片上，eNPU在保持严格SLO保证的同时，将LLM服务能耗降低25.8%-35.2%，面积开销仅为3.45%。基于开源NPU核心验证了功能正确性，并使用生产级NPU仿真器对多种LLM和生产负载进行了评估。

Conclusion: eNPU证明了空间细粒度、组件级DVFS在NPU上的可行性，为LLM服务的能效优化提供了有效的硬件-软件协同设计方案。该方法在不违反SLO约束的前提下显著降低了能耗开销，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: As neural processing units (NPUs) evolve rapidly to accommodate the ever-increasing compute demand of large language models (LLMs), their power consumption is becoming a limiting factor. Our study shows that using dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) to exploit the service-level objective (SLO) slacks is a promising way to improve NPU energy efficiency for LLM services. And as tensor operators in LLMs exhibit diverse bottlenecks across NPU components, it is desirable to configure the frequency separately for each component to maximize their energy efficiency.
  In this paper, we develop eNPU that enables hardware and software support for spatially fine-grained, component-level DVFS on NPUs. eNPU refactors the NPU core pipeline to partition components into separate V/$f$ domains. It introduces lightweight cross-domain communication mechanisms to mitigate synchronization overheads across components, and extends the NPU ISA for sub-$μ$s DVFS control. eNPU uses a compiler-driven two-level greedy search to co-optimize instruction scheduling and per-component V/$f$ selection under SLO constraints. We implement eNPU's pipeline design on an open-source NPU core to verify its functionality and evaluate the energy savings with a production-level NPU simulator with various LLMs using production traces. eNPU reduces energy consumption of LLM services by 25.8%--35.2% with 3.45% area overhead on a TPUv4 chip, while preserving strict SLO guarantees.

</details>


### [20] [Auto-Scaling Heterogeneous Neural Processing Units for Energy and Cost-Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16488)
*Yuqi Xue,Jichuan Chang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuScale，一个面向云平台异构NPU的自动扩缩容框架，通过vPod抽象和基于roofline分析的资源分配，显著提升了LLM服务的成本效率和服务质量达标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云平台部署了大量NPU芯片来满足大语言模型服务的计算需求，但由于NPU芯片更新迭代快，产生了异构的NPU计算池。现有云平台缺乏管理系统和架构支持，不清楚如何高效利用异构NPUs来最大化LLM服务的能效和成本效率。

Method: 提出NeuScale框架：(1)使用vPod抽象管理异构NPU资源，封装不同版本NPU的核心硬件参数并兼容现有ML框架；(2)采用基于roofline的轻量级分析为LLM推理请求做出最优vPod分配；(3)支持细粒度动态资源调配，可调整vPod配置（纵向扩缩）和vPod数量（横向扩缩）。在生产级NPU模拟器上实现并验证。

Result: 基于生产级NPU模拟器的评估显示，NeuScale能够充分利用异构NPU资源，显著提升成本效率和服务级目标(SLO)达标率。在多种流行LLM上的实验验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: NeuScale成功解决了云平台异构NPU管理问题，通过创新的vPod抽象和智能资源分配策略，实现了异构NPU资源的高效利用，为LLM服务提供了更好的成本效益和服务质量保障。

Abstract: To meet the ever-increasing computing demands of large language model (LLM) services, modern cloud platforms have widely deployed neural processing units (NPUs). These NPU chips have been developed and evolved at an incredibly fast pace, this inevitably produces heterogeneous compute pools backed by different versions of NPU chips. Unfortunately, due to the lack of system and architecture support for managing NPU heterogeneity in the cloud, it is unclear how to best utilize heterogeneous NPUs to maximize the energy and cost efficiency for LLM services.
  In this paper, we first conduct a characterization study of various generations of real NPU chips to demonstrate the potential benefits on energy/cost efficiency and performance by utilizing heterogeneous NPU chips. To realize these benefits, we present NeuScale, an auto-scaling framework to automatically exploit heterogeneous NPUs for cloud platforms. NeuScale manages heterogeneous NPU resources with a new vPod abstraction, which abstracts the core hardware parameters of different NPU versions and provides compatibility with existing ML frameworks. It makes the best-fit vPod allocations for different LLM inference requests using an intuitive and lightweight roofline-based analysis. It supports fine-grained dynamic NPU resource provisioning by adjusting both the vPod configuration (i.e., scaling up/down) and the number of vPods (e.g., scaling in/out). To validate the benefits of NeuScale at scale, we implement it with a production-level NPU simulator. Our evaluation with popular LLMs shows that NeuScale can significantly improve cost efficiency and service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction rate by best utilizing heterogeneous NPU resources.

</details>


### [21] [Mitigating Compiler Fusion-Induced Power Bursts in Mobile NPU Inference as the Battery Depletes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16555)
*Ryoga Yuzawa,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对移动NPU在低压下因算子融合导致电流尖峰触发DVFS降频的问题，提出一种基于测量的图重写方法，通过插入屏障防止有害的超级层合并，显著降低峰值电流并改善低压稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备在低压条件下进行实时NPU推理时，单个推理会在供电网络中引起瞬时电压跌落，导致电源管理集成电路触发动态电压频率调整（DVFS）并增加延迟。现有的NPU编译器激进算子融合会形成单体超级层，产生高峰值电流脉冲，恶化DVFS问题，降低低压工作余量。

Method: 首先在商用智能手机上进行测量研究，识别出算子融合产生的峰值功率比热点。然后提出一种实用的黑盒缓解方案：基于测量引导的预编译图重写，在选定的峰值功率比热点处插入屏障，防止厂商NPU编译器进行有害的超级层合并。这是一种编译器感知但不修改编译器的方案。

Result: 在Snapdragon 8 Gen 3平台上，使用MobileNetV4（768×768分辨率）在ImageNet-1k上评估：峰值电流从3.12A降至1.94A；延迟开销仅3.76%；将推断的DVFS余量偏移约173mV；在更深的低压范围内保持稳定的延迟。

Conclusion: 通过测量引导的图重写方法有效缓解了移动NPU推理中的峰值电流问题，在不修改编译器的前提下显著改善了低压工作稳定性。该方法为移动端NPU部署提供了实用的优化手段，有助于延长设备在低压条件下的有效工作时间。

Abstract: Mobile devices increasingly rely on real-time NPU inference for camera and perception workloads. Under low-voltage conditions, however, a single inference can induce an instantaneous voltage droop in the power-delivery network, causing the power management integrated circuit to invoke dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) and increase latency. We present a measurement study of this effect on a commercial smartphone. We show that aggressive operator fusion in a mobile NPU compiler can create monolithic superlayers whose concentrated execution produces large peak-current bursts. These bursts shift the DVFS-onset voltage upward and reduce the low-voltage operating margin. We further evaluate a practical black-box mitigation: a measurement-guided, pre-compilation graph rewrite that inserts barriers at selected peak-to-average power ratio hot spots to prevent harmful superlayer merging in the vendor NPU compiler. On Snapdragon 8 Gen 3 with MobileNetV4 at 768 x 768 resolution on ImageNet-1k, this method reduces peak current from 3.12 A to 1.94 A with 3.76% latency overhead, preserves stable latency deeper into the low-voltage regime, and shifts the inferred DVFS margin by approximately 173 mV.

</details>


### [22] [Rtl2lean: Automated RTL-to-Lean Translation with Hierarchical Theorem Generation and Lemma Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16855)
*Hongqin Lyu,Junxing Dong,Yonghao Wang,Zhiteng Chao,Tiancheng Wang,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Rtl2lean框架，自动将RTL设计转换为Lean4模型并构建层次化定理库，结合LLM辅助证明，实现了高效的机器检验RTL证明库构建，重用率达80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SystemVerilog代码进行交互式定理证明验证需要大量人工语义建模和证明构造工作，亟需自动化方法降低成本。

Method: Rtl2lean框架自动将RTL设计翻译为Lean4可执行模型，采用四层定理框架表达组合语义、时序更新、单周期行为和可达性/不变量；引入LLM证明循环自动提出并筛选可复用的中间引理。

Result: 在六个SystemVerilog设计上生成403个定理，全部通过Lean检验；358个基础引理中287个可自动复用，重用率为80.2%；检查开销低。

Conclusion: Rtl2lean能够高效构建机器检验的RTL证明库，显著降低人工成本并促进跨属性引理复用，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Formal verification with interactive theorem provers can provide strong correctness guarantees for register transfer level designs, but applying it to existing SystemVerilog code requires substantial manual effort in semantic modeling and proof construction. This paper presents Rtl2lean, a framework that automatically translates RTL designs into executable Lean 4 models and builds a hierarchical theorem library for subsequent verification. The generated model represents hardware execution as a pure state transition function, while a four layer theorem framework captures combinational semantics, sequential updates, single cycle behavior, and reachability and invariants. When a high level property cannot be discharged by the existing theorem base, an LLM based proving loop proposes intermediate lemmas from the current proof context and Lean feedback. Only lemmas accepted by the Lean kernel are added to the reusable lemma pool. Experiments on six SystemVerilog designs generate 403 theorems, all of which are successfully checked by Lean. Among 358 foundational lemmas, 287 are available for automatic reuse, yielding a reusable lemma ratio of 80.2 percent. The results demonstrate that Rtl2lean can construct machine checked RTL proof libraries with low checking overhead and substantial cross property lemma reuse.

</details>


### [23] [Provably Good Prim-Dijkstra Revisited: New Theory and a Practical Algorithm for a Classical VLSI Routing Problem with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17005)
*Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文以Prim-Dijkstra路由这一经典VLSI问题为例，证明了大型语言模型能够重新激活被忽视的算法问题研究。作者证明了弱NP完全性，提出了一种具有(2,2)保证的代价-半径权衡方法，并开发了HP-RCRST求解器，在测试实例上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型为重新审视精确但休眠的算法问题提供了新契机，可能为解决基础问题开辟新路径。Prim-Dijkstra路由问题的仅终端曼哈顿复杂度在数十年实践工作后仍未解决，为验证这一可能性提供了理想案例。

Method: 作者采用理论分析与算法实现相结合的方法：1）证明Prim-Dijkstra路由问题的弱NP完全性；2）推导连续的代价-半径权衡关系，并提供平衡的(2,2)保证；3）设计并实现基于高度分区的多模式求解器HP-RCRST。

Result: 在28个开发实例上，HP-RCRST求解器的强模式在23个实例上Pareto支配所有已发表方法的组合，在5个实例上持平。理论证明建立了问题的复杂性边界，算法实践验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了冲突猜想、反例、形式化验证与实现的结合如何重新开启被忽视的研究问题。大型语言模型确实能够激活休眠的算法问题研究，Prim-Dijkstra路由问题案例为这一观点提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Large language models may make precise but dormant algorithmic problems practical to revisit, and may expose new paths toward fundamental ones. We demonstrate this possibility through Prim-Dijkstra routing, a classic VLSI problem whose terminal-only Manhattan complexity remained open despite decades of practical work. We prove weak NP-completeness, derive a continuous cost-radius tradeoff with a balanced (2,2) guarantee, and build HP-RCRST, a height-partition-based multi-mode solver. On 28 development instances, its stronger modes Pareto-dominate the published-method union on 23 and tie on five. The case shows how conflicting conjectures, counterexamples, formal checks, and implementation can reopen neglected questions. Code and reproducibility materials are available at https://github.com/CODA-Team/hp-rcrst.

</details>


### [24] [ThAME: 3D Memory-Enabled Heterogeneous Accelerator for LLM Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17074)
*Pratyush Dhingra,Pramit Kumar Pal,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了ThAME，一种用于混合专家(MoE)推理的三维异构多芯片架构，通过FeFET和DRAM存储芯片let以及优化的网络通信骨干，解决了MoE推理中的三大瓶颈，实现了显著的速度和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家(MoE)架构已成为扩展大语言模型的主流范式，但在传统硬件上的推理受到三个基本瓶颈的限制：1）获取非连续专家权重所需的大量内存带宽；2）输入依赖的令牌路由产生的非确定性散射-聚集流量；3）同步专家输出聚合带来的尾延迟依赖。这些瓶颈严重制约了MoE模型的推理效率和硬件利用率。

Method: 提出ThAME三维异构多芯片架构：1）采用基于铁电场效应晶体管(FeFET)的非易失性存储芯片let和基于DRAM的易失性存储芯片let；2）设计协同计算映射策略，使注意力机制和专家路由的计算特性相匹配；3）设计专用片上网络通信骨干，优化非确定性令牌路由流量，缓解输入依赖的MoE流量模式组合空间中的通信瓶颈。

Result: 实验结果表明，ThAME在最先进的同类架构中实现了高达15.7倍的速度提升和高达9.8倍的能效改善。

Conclusion: ThAME成功解决了MoE推理中的内存带宽、非确定性流量和尾延迟等关键瓶颈问题，通过异构存储架构和优化的通信骨干，显著提升了MoE模型的推理性能和能效，为大语言模型的MoE架构部署提供了有效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a dominant paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs). However, MoE inference on conventional hardware is constrained by three fundamental bottlenecks. These encompass the massive memory bandwidth required to fetch non-contiguous expert weights, the non-deterministic scatter-gather traffic generated by input-dependent token routing, and the tail-latency dependency imposed by synchronous expert output aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose ThAME, a three-dimensional (3D) heterogeneous multi-chiplet architecture for MoE inference. ThAME employs Ferroelectric Field-Effect Transistor (FeFET)-based non-volatile and DRAM-based volatile memory chiplets with a co-designed compute mapping strategy that aligns the distinct computational profiles of attention mechanisms and expert routing. Furthermore, we design a specialized Network-on-Chip communication backbone optimized to mitigate the bottlenecks associated with non-deterministic token routing traffic across the combinatorial space of input-dependent MoE traffic patterns. Experimental results demonstrate that ThAME outperforms state-of-the-art counterparts by up to 15.7x in terms of speedup and improves energy efficiency by up to 9.8x.

</details>


### [25] [ThRIve: Thermally Robust CNN Inference via Low-Rank Adaptation in Heterogeneous PIM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17091)
*Vibhanshu Sharma,Pratyush Dhingra,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ThRIve，一种针对存内计算（PIM）架构热噪声问题的噪声感知训练方法，通过低秩适应技术在不显著影响推理精度的前提下，实现了对热噪声的鲁棒性，并显著降低了能耗延迟积（EDP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非易失性存储器基的PIM架构在加速机器学习推理时表现出色，但其存储单元易受热噪声影响，导致存储的模型权重发生偏移，从而降低推理精度。现有系统在面对整个工作温度范围内的热噪声变化时，难以维持稳定的推理准确性，亟需一种能够兼顾能效与热鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 提出ThRIve，一种噪声感知训练方法，核心思想是在模型中引入低秩适应参数（low-rank adaptation）。将这一部分噪声感知的低秩参数存储于对热噪声更不敏感的硬件区域，从而在推理过程中对热噪声影响进行补偿与缓解。

Result: 实验表明，采用ThRIve的PIM架构在整个工作温度范围内，平均推理精度与理想（无噪声）精度相差不超过2%，且精度变化范围控制在平均值的2%以内。同时，相比热鲁棒的SRAM基PIM系统，该方法在CNN模型推理中实现了最高5.4倍的能耗延迟积（EDP）降低。

Conclusion: ThRIve成功实现了异构PIM架构在热噪声干扰下的鲁棒推理，达到了与SRAM基PIM系统相当的准确性和稳定性，同时显著提升了能效，为实用化部署提供了有效技术路径。

Abstract: Processing-In-Memory (PIM) has emerged as a promising technology for accelerating machine learning (ML) workloads. Specifically, non-volatile memory-based PIM architectures have enabled effective ML acceleration due to their ability to perform energy-efficient matrix-vector multiplication operations. However, these devices suffer from non-idealities such as thermal noise. This noise alters the stored values in the memory cells which correspond to actual model weights, compromising the inference accuracy. In this work, we introduce ThRIve, a noise-aware training methodology that leverages low-rank adaptation to enable thermally robust inference on heterogeneous PIM architectures. ThRIve selectively stores these low-rank noise-aware parameters on a hardware that is less susceptible to thermal noise, enabling robustness against temperature-induced noise variations. ThRIve mitigates the effects of thermal-noise and prevent the drop in inference accuracy across the entire operating temperature range. Experimental results demonstrate that ThRIve-enabled architectures maintain consistent inference accuracy, with the mean accuracy staying within 2% of the ideal (i.e., noise-free) accuracy, and the variation in accuracy across the entire operating temperature range remaining within 2% of the mean. The proposed methodology achieves accuracy and robustness comparable to thermally-resilient Static Random-Access Memory (SRAM)-based PIM systems, while delivering up to 5.4x reduction in energy-delay product (EDP) during CNN model inferencing.

</details>


### [26] [Inter-Set Correlation-Aware Replacement Policy for Energy Efficient RTM-based Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17274)
*Elham Cheshmikhani,Hamed Farbeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向磁赛道存储器末级缓存的数据放置与替换方案，通过智能分组相关数据块来减少冗余移位操作，在SPEC CPU2017基准测试下实现了49.0%的移位开销降低和35.9%的缓存能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRAM缓存面临面积消耗过大和漏电功率问题，而磁赛道存储器（RTM）作为非易失性替代方案虽然具有高存储密度优势，但其固有的串行访问特性导致显著的移位开销和能耗延迟问题。现有RTM缓存的数据放置策略会触发冗余移位操作，因为读取某一数据块的移位操作未能将后续访问的数据块预置于合适位置。

Method: 提出一种创新的数据放置与替换方案，通过智能分组相关联的数据块，确保单次移位操作不仅能获取目标数据块，还能将后续访问的数据块对齐到更靠近访问端口的位置，从而优化移位操作的效率。使用gem5模拟器和SPEC CPU2017基准测试套件进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方案将移位开销降低了49.0%，缓存能耗降低了35.9%，同时对性能影响微乎其微。此外，该方案对更长的纳米线轨道具有良好的可扩展性，能够实现更高的缓存密度。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据放置与替换方案有效解决了RTM缓存中的冗余移位问题，显著降低了能耗和延迟，同时保持了高性能表现，为构建高密度、高能效的非易失性缓存系统提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Today's data-centric applications demand cache architectures that can scale with growing workloads while maintaining high performance and energy efficiency. Fundamental issues such as excessive area consumption and leakage power are increasingly challenging traditional SRAM-based caches, thereby motivating the exploration of non-volatile alternatives. Among these, racetrack memory (RTM) stands out due to its remarkable storage density, achieved through nanowires hosting sequential magnetic domains that can be manipulated via domain wall or skyrmion techniques. Despite its advantages, racetrack memory's inherent serialized access introduces considerable shift overhead, leading to elevated energy consumption and latency. In this paper, we analyze the placement strategies in conventional RTM-based last-level caches and identify that current methods trigger redundant shift operations as a shift intended to read one block fails to preposition other blocks subsequently accessed. To resolve this, we introduce an innovative data placement and replacement scheme that intelligently groups correlated blocks, ensuring that a single shift not only retrieves the target block but also aligns subsequent blocks closer to the access port. Our simulation results using the gem5 simulator and the SPEC CPU2017 benchmarks reveal that our scheme reduces shift overhead by 49.0% and cache energy consumption by 35.9% with negligible performance impact. In addition, this scheme exhibits robust scalability to longer nanowire tracks for higher cache density.

</details>


### [27] [ADEPT: Architecture-Driven Energy-Efficient CNN Fine-Tuning on PIM Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17371)
*Pratyush Dhingra,Vibhanshu Sharma,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADEPT框架，通过硬件感知的方式加速PIM架构上的CNN微调，显著减少可训练参数和片外数据访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CNN微调策略对底层硬件不敏感，将所有层同等对待，导致微调过程中产生大量中间激活值和频繁的片外内存访问，影响PIM加速器的整体效率。需要一种硬件感知的微调框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出硬件感知框架ADEPT，引入新的度量指标量化基于梯度的块敏感性与硬件架构特定的能耗-延迟积(EDP)之间的权衡，生成平台相关的微调配置，自适应地训练模型，同时考虑训练开销和层敏感性。

Result: ADEPT能够减少总的可训练参数数量和微调过程中的片外数据访问，相比全参数微调，预测精度损失极小。

Conclusion: ADEPT框架成功实现了PIM架构上CNN的高效微调，通过硬件感知的自适应策略有效平衡了计算效率与模型性能。

Abstract: Processing-in-memory-based (PIM) architectures have emerged as a promising solution for accelerating Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) workloads at the edge. Fine-tuning pre-trained CNNs is a common requirement to enhance the model predictive accuracy after deployment. However, the fine-tuning process is computational and memory-intensive, generating a significant amount of intermediate activations. This leads to frequent off-chip memory access, affecting the overall efficiency of the PIM accelerator. Existing fine-tuning strategies are agnostic to the underlying hardware, as they treat all layers equally. In this paper, we propose a hardware-aware framework called ADEPT to accelerate CNN fine-tuning on PIM architectures. Unlike prior fine-tuning methods, ADEPT adaptively trains the model considering both the training overhead and layer sensitivity. Specifically, ADEPT introduces a novel metric that quantifies the trade-off between a block's gradient-based sensitivity and its hardware architecture-specific Energy-Delay Product (EDP), producing platform-dependent fine-tuning configurations. Overall, ADEPT helps reduce the total trainable parameters and the off-chip data access during fine-tuning, while incurring minimal loss in predictive accuracy compared to full-parameter fine-tuning.

</details>

*Alaaddin Goktug Ayar,Martin Margala*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向边缘Transformer推理的过渡感知后端调度方法，通过结合当前算子特征和之前选择的后端来保留有益的形状特定选择，同时避免不必要的转换，相比最佳静态策略平均降低了17.4%的延迟、14.4%的能耗和28.5%的能耗延迟积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台上的大语言模型推理效率不仅受模型大小限制，还受执行后端间形状相关性能差异的影响。静态后端分配无法利用这种变化，而独立的逐算子选择会引入昂贵的设备和框架切换开销。现有方法无法在保持形状特定优化选择的同时避免不必要的后端转换开销。

Method: 提出过渡感知后端调度方法，结合当前算子特征与先前选择的后端进行决策。收集7个Transformer模型的有序执行轨迹，在NVIDIA Jetson平台上对4类常见算子在PyTorch eager CPU、PyTorch eager CUDA和ONNX Runtime CPU三种后端进行基准测试。使用观测到的算子成本和实际后端切换的转换成本，通过测量支持的轨迹回放来评估调度策略。支持范围内的算子动态选择，范围外算子保持静态分配。

Result: 在9,584个有序算子实例和278个精确形状组上，过渡感知调度相比最佳静态策略平均降低回放延迟17.4%、能耗14.4%、能耗延迟积28.5%。相比算子局部选择减少了切换次数。留一模型评估中，7个模型中的6个在三个目标上均有改善，所有7个模型的能耗均得到改善。

Conclusion: 引入算子形状和后端转换上下文信息可以显著改善边缘Transformer工作负载的选择性后端调度效果，为边缘平台上的高效LLM推理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) inference on edge platforms is limited not only by model size, but also by shape-dependent performance differences across execution backends. Static backend assignment cannot exploit this variation, while independent per-operator selection can introduce costly device and framework switches. This paper presents a transition-aware backend dispatch approach for edge transformer inference. The approach combines current operator features with the previously selected backend to preserve beneficial shape-specific choices while avoiding unnecessary transitions. Ordered traces are collected from full-model inference runs of seven transformer models, and four common operator classes are benchmarked across PyTorch eager CPU, PyTorch eager CUDA, and ONNX Runtime CPU on an NVIDIA Jetson platform. The dispatch policies are evaluated through measurement-backed trace replay using observed operator costs and transition costs measured from actual backend switches. Supported operators are selected dynamically, while operators outside the dispatch scope retain a static assignment. Across 9,584 ordered operator instances and 278 exact shape groups, transition-aware dispatch reduces replayed latency, energy, and energy-delay product relative to the best static policy by 17.4%, 14.4%, and 28.5% on average, respectively. It also reduces switching relative to operator-local selection. Leave-one-model-out evaluation improves all three objectives for six of seven held-out models and improves energy for all seven. These results demonstrate that incorporating operator shape and backend-transition context can improve selective backend dispatch for edge transformer workloads.

</details>


### [29] [Isolation Failure From Shared Storage: Characterizing and Exploiting Page-Cache SCA Leakage Across Containers and VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17518)
*Alon Abudraham,Xingyu Chen,Itamar Levi,Ari Trachtenberg*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了跨多种云隔离边界存在的页面缓存侧信道攻击，发现当I/O路径暴露共享的主机可缓存对象时，定时信号持续存在，而直接I/O和专用块设备可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云平台通过软件隔离机制共享硬件资源来提升性能和效率，包括传统容器、沙箱运行时（如gVisor）和基于虚拟机的运行时（如Kata、QEMU/KVM）等。然而，当租户访问主机支持的文件系统状态时，主机页面缓存仍然保持共享和可观察状态。这种操作系统管理的页面缓存通道形成了一个操作系统介导的微架构定时侧信道，其信号由处理器微架构、内存和存储层次结构以及虚拟化机制共同塑造。因此需要研究非特权定时测量是否能揭示跨这些隔离边界的页面缓存驻留情况。

Method: 研究方法包括对不同隔离技术的系统评估：Docker容器；gVisor（使用systrap和KVM）；使用QEMU和Cloud Hypervisor共享主机文件系统的Kata Containers；使用QEMU、Cloud Hypervisor和Firecracker支持块设备存储的Kata；以及多种主机缓存策略下的QEMU/KVM虚拟机。通过分析这些环境中页面缓存驻留的定时信号特征，评估不同存储配置（包括OverlayFS层、virtio-fs导出、环回块设备、直接I/O和专用块设备）对定时信号的影响。并通过WordPress部署配合MySQL的案例研究验证攻击可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，只要I/O路径暴露共享的、主机可缓存的文件支持对象，定时信号就会持续存在，包括在OverlayFS层、virtio-fs导出和环回块设备下。然而，直接I/O和专用块设备能够大幅削弱或消除该信号。虚拟化通过增加延迟和算法噪声重塑了泄漏模式，但并未消除对共享硬件和缓存状态的底层依赖。案例研究成功从WordPress和MySQL部署中恢复出粗粒度活动信息。

Conclusion: 页面缓存攻击属于操作系统介导的微架构定时通道这一更广泛类别。虚拟化无法完全解决该问题，因为它仅重塑泄漏模式而未从根本上消除对共享硬件和缓存状态的依赖。研究结果强调了需要协调硬件、虚拟化和操作系统支持来实现定时隔离，以有效应对此类跨边界侧信道攻击。

Abstract: Modern cloud platforms increasingly combine strong software isolation mechanisms with shared hardware resources to improve performance and resource efficiency. Conventional containers do this by sharing the host kernel directly, whereas sandboxed runtimes (e.g., gVisor) and VM-based runtimes (e.g., Kata, QEMU/KVM) provide progressively stronger isolation. In all cases, when tenants access host-backed filesystem state, the host page cache can remain shared and observable. Although OS-managed, this page-cache channel forms an OS-mediated microarchitectural timing side channel whose signal is shaped by the processor microarchitecture, memory and storage hierarchies, and virtualization mechanisms. We thus investigate whether unprivileged timing measurements can reveal page-cache residency across these isolation boundaries. Our evaluation covers Docker; gVisor with systrap and KVM; Kata Containers using QEMU and Cloud Hypervisor with shared host filesystems; Kata using QEMU, Cloud Hypervisor, and Firecracker with block-device-backed storage; and QEMU/KVM virtual machines under multiple host cache policies. Our results show that the timing signal persists whenever the I/O path exposes shared, host-cacheable file-backed objects, including under OverlayFS layers, virtio-fs exports, and loop-backed block devices. However, direct I/O and dedicated block devices substantially attenuate or eliminate the signal. Virtualization therefore reshapes leakage through added latency and algorithmic noise but does not remove the underlying dependence on shared hardware and cache state. We showcase this through a case study in which we recover coarse-grained activity from a WordPress deployment backed by MySQL. These results place page-cache attacks within the broader class of OS-mediated microarchitectural timing channels and motivate coordinated hardware, virtualization, and OS support for timing isolation.

</details>


### [30] [D-NOVA: In-Storage Retrieval Accelerator via Dual-Bound 3D NAND-Optimized Similarity Search with Vector Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17538)
*Chang Eun Song,Sumukh Pinge,Tianqi Zhang,Sung Eun Kim,Tajana S. Rosing,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出D-NOVA，一种软硬件协同设计的存内检索加速器，通过将倒排文件(IVF)检索流水线直接嵌入NAND阵列，实现检索增强生成(RAG)系统的显著性能提升与能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中密集向量检索带来显著的延迟和能耗开销，成为主要性能瓶颈。现有存内加速器仍依赖主机或外部处理器，约70%的检索时间消耗在存储器外部，无法完全克服带宽限制，形成新的内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出D-NOVA软硬件协同设计：1) 将基于倒排文件(IVF)的分层检索流水线直接嵌入NAND存储阵列；2) 提出Dual-Bound Tight Similarity Sensing (DTS)距离度量，专门针对NAND string内搜索优化；3) 引入轻量级对比适配器，将嵌入向量映射到DTS友好域，在提升性能的同时恢复接近软件的召回率。

Result: 与CPU基线相比，D-NOVA实现最高41.7倍速度提升和71倍能效提升；与现有先进存内RAG加速器相比，吞吐量提升12.13倍，能效提升最高1.26倍，召回率接近软件水平。

Conclusion: 全存内向量搜索展现出RAG加速的可扩展性潜力，证明了将检索功能深度嵌入NAND阵列的有效性，为构建高效的RAG系统提供了新的硬件架构范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the factual grounding of large language model (LLM) inference by retrieving relevant information from external knowledge bases. However, its dense vector retrieval introduces significant latency and energy overhead, becoming the primary performance bottleneck. Although recent in-storage accelerators aim to reduce data movement, they still rely on host or embedded processors outside the memory, where nearly 70% of the total retrieval time is spent. As a result, they cannot fully overcome the bandwidth limitations, leading to yet another memory bottleneck. To tackle these limitations, we present D-NOVA, a hardware-software co-designed in-storage retrieval accelerator. D-NOVA executes an inverted file (IVF)-based hierarchical retrieval pipeline by deeply embedding the search functionality directly into the NAND memory array. This is achieved by incorporating a new distance metric, Dual-Bound Tight Similarity Sensing (DTS), which is specifically tailored for searching within the NAND string. In addition, we introduce a lightweight contrastive adapter that maps embedding vectors into a DTS-friendly domain, recovering near-software recall while improving performance and energy efficiency. D-NOVA is up to 41.7x faster and 71x more energy-efficient than a CPU baseline, and achieves 12.13x higher throughput while being up to 1.26x more energy-efficient than state-of-the-art in-storage RAG accelerators, demonstrating the potential of fully in-storage vector search for scalable RAG acceleration.

</details>


### [31] [Cross-Domain Acceleration of Open Modification Search: From Commodity Platforms to Emerging Memory and Storage Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17569)
*Sumukh Pinge,Chang Eun Song,Po-Kai Hsu,Zheyu Li,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Yanru Chen,Xiangjin Wu,Wei-Chen Chen,Eric Pop,Shimeng Yu,H. -S. Philip Wong,Tajana Rosing,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 首次跨平台对比了质谱开放修饰搜索(OMS)加速器，发现内存与存储中心架构相比传统平台可实现超100倍加速与超40000倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OMS加速器研究彼此孤立，缺乏跨平台系统级权衡的统一视角；且OMS性能瓶颈主要在于参考数据的移动而非计算，亟需面向数据移动的架构创新。

Method: 提出基于二值超维计算(HDC)的OMS公式，将相似性评估归结为轻量级位运算，并对器件非理想性与有限算力具备鲁棒性，从而在GPU、近存储FPGA、DRAM近存计算、ReRAM/PCM内存计算、3D NAND/FeNAND存储计算等平台上一致评估。

Conclusion: 内存与存储中心架构是突破大规模、高速质谱搜索加速瓶颈的关键架构方向，HDC驱动的位运算公式能有效弥合器件非理想性与计算能力受限之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Open modification search (OMS) in mass spectrometry (MS) is a data-intensive workload whose performance is dominantly limited by reference data movement rather than computation. Prior OMS accelerators have largely been evaluated in isolation, making it difficult to understand system-level trade-offs across platforms. This paper presents the first workload-driven, cross-platform survey of accelerators for MS search by studying not only commodity platforms, but also emerging memory- and storage-centric architectures, including GPUs, near-storage FPGAs, DRAM near-memory processing, ReRAM/PCM in-memory processing, and 3D NAND/FeNAND in-storage processing, under consistent algorithmic and accuracy assumptions. Leveraging a binary hyperdimensional computing (HDC)-based OMS formulation that reduces similarity evaluation to lightweight bitwise primitives and tolerates device-level non-idealities, we enable a robust execution on memory-centric architectures despite device-level non-idealities and limited computing capability. Overall, this study identifies memory- and storage-centric architectures as a key architectural breakthrough for large-scale, high-speed search acceleration, delivering up to >100x speedup and >40,000x improvement in energy efficiency.

</details>


### [32] [New Number Formats for FFT IP Cores in Optical OFDM Transceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17835)
*Lukas Krupp,Gustavo Magalhães Gomes De Souza,Sani Nassif,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对FFT内核的跨层协同设计方法，在光OFDM收发器中对比了定点与定制低精度浮点格式，证明了11-12位浮点在保持BER性能的同时，可降低19.8%功耗和12.0%面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DSP实现广泛使用定点运算以降低硬件复杂度，但机器学习加速器已证明低精度浮点格式能显著提升能效和吞吐量，这促使重新评估传统DSP工作负载的数值表示方式。核心问题是：低精度浮点能否在保持动态范围和数值鲁棒性优势的同时，实现与定点实现相竞争的功耗、性能和面积指标。

Method: 提出一种跨层协同设计方法，联合优化数值表示、算术单元和应用层性能。以FFT为案例，在光OFDM收发器场景下进行评估，使用12nm FinFET工艺的后布局功耗和面积结果进行对比，并通过BER与Eb/N0关系评估系统级性能。对比了定点格式与定制的11、12位浮点格式。

Result: 对于128 Gbit/s收发器中的256点FFT引擎，11位和12位定制浮点格式在多种调制阶数下保持接近32位浮点参考的BER性能，相比代表性定点设计，FFT核心功耗降低高达19.8%，面积减少高达12.0%。

Conclusion: 定制低精度浮点运算首次应用于光OFDM收发器的FFT核心，证明了其在保持数值精度的同时能够超越定点实现的功耗和面积表现，为高性能DSP系统提供了一种更优的数值表示方案。

Abstract: Many state-of-the-art DSP implementations use fixed-point arithmetic due to its reduced hardware complexity and high throughput compared to conventional floating-point arithmetic. In contrast, machine learning accelerators exhibit substantial gains from reduced-precision floating-point formats, enabling improvements in energy efficiency and peak throughput. These advances motivate a re-evaluation of numerical representations for classical DSP workloads. A central question is whether reduced-precision floating-point formats can achieve competitive power, performance, and area compared to fixed-point implementations, while providing advantages in dynamic range and numerical robustness. This paper presents a new cross-layer co-design methodology for DSP kernels that jointly optimizes numerical representations, arithmetic units, and application-level performance. As a case study, we focus on the FFT, a fundamental DSP block across many applications. The FFT is evaluated within optical OFDM transceivers, where it dominates power consumption and silicon area as FFT size and modulation order scale to support data rates beyond 100 Gbit/s. We compare fixed-point and reduced-precision floating-point formats using post-layout power and area results in a 12nm FinFET technology and demonstrate system-level performance in terms of BER versus Eb/N0. For a 256-point FFT engine in a 128 Gbit/s transceiver, we show that 11- and 12-bit custom floating-point formats preserve BER performance close to a 32-bit floating-point reference across multiple modulation orders, while reducing FFT core power by up to 19.8% and area by up to 12.0% compared to representative fixed-point designs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first investigation of custom reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT cores in optical OFDM transceivers.

</details>


### [33] [SEAM-V: A Hybrid-Decoupled RISC-V Vector Processor with Backend-Visible EP Context for Sustained Vector Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17899)
*Weiying Wang,Zhiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAM-V，一种面向RISC-V向量扩展的混合解耦向量执行架构，通过任务级解耦、本地指令供给和VLIW打包形成连续执行流，在17个代表性内核上相比紧密耦合实现平均加速1.34倍，短向量场景加速近3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习和科学计算的数据并行负载对处理器吞吐量、能效和可扩展性提出更高需求。RISC-V向量扩展(RVV)支持可扩展执行，但现有紧密耦合实现仍依赖标量核心逐条提供向量指令，导致向量指令供给间隙、标量端进展延迟以及短向量、循环尾部和控制/内存交织阶段的保守依赖处理问题。

Method: 提出SEAM-V混合解耦向量执行架构：通过任务级解耦、本地指令供给和VLIW打包形成连续执行包(EP)流；EP序列化后保持其EP身份和请求绑定预取上下文对动态向量后端可见，实现同EP候选冲突抑制和请求绑定预取；混合调度路径在满足安全条件时提供有限跨EP向量重叠；跨EP依赖、资源冲突和内存顺序由后端动态管理。

Result: 周期精确RTL评估显示，相比基于Ara的紧密耦合RVV实现(TC)，SEAM-V在17个代表性内核上实现几何平均1.34倍加速。一维变AVL组、BLAS和矩阵组、固定尺寸应用组分别获得1.50倍、1.25倍和1.27倍加速。在AVL=32时，六个一维向量内核的几何平均加速接近3倍。

Conclusion: SEAM-V通过混合解耦架构有效解决了传统紧密耦合RVV实现的指令供给瓶颈和依赖处理问题，显著提升了短向量、变长和内存交织场景的性能，同时保持了架构的可扩展性。

Abstract: Data-parallel workloads in deep learning and scientific computing continue to drive demand for higher processor throughput, energy efficiency, and scalability. The RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV) supports scalable execution through a vector-length-agnostic programming model. However, many tightly coupled implementations still rely on the scalar core to supply vector instructions one at a time, making execution susceptible to vector-instruction supply gaps, scalar-side progression delays, and conservative dependence handling in short-vector, loop-tail, and control/memory-interleaved phases. This paper presents SEAM-V, a hybrid-decoupled vector execution architecture for RVV. SEAM-V forms a continuous stream of execute packets (EPs) through task-level decoupling, local instruction supply, and VLIW-style packing. After an EP is serialized into individual requests, its EP identity and request-bound prefetch context remain visible to the dynamic vector backend, enabling same-EP candidate-hazard suppression and request-bound prefetching. The hybrid-dispatch path can also provide limited cross-EP vector overlap when the required safety conditions are satisfied. Cross-EP dependences, dependences not exempted by the EP contract, resource conflicts, and memory ordering remain dynamically managed by the backend. Cycle-accurate RTL evaluation shows that, compared with an Ara-based tightly coupled RVV implementation (TC), SEAM-V achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 1.34x across 17 representative kernels. The one-dimensional variable-AVL, BLAS and matrix, and fixed-size application groups achieve speedups of 1.50x, 1.25x, and 1.27x, respectively. At AVL=32, the geometric-mean speedup across six one-dimensional vector kernels approaches 3x.

</details>


### [34] [Fixed Point Exploration For CV-QKD IR QC-MET-LDPC Toward Hardware Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17960)
*Guilherme Vergne de Oliveira,Mauro Queiroz Nooblath Neto,Micael Andrade Dias,Francisco Revson Fernandes Pereira,Francisco Marcos de Assis,Valéria Loureiro da Silva,Nelson Alves Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在统一低信噪比定点框架下对比了CV-QKD中的SPA、MSA和NMS三种LDPC解码算法，发现SPA综合性能最优，且Q8.4精度在可靠性与硬件效率间达到最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速LDPC解码是连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）系统的主要瓶颈，亟需采用定点算术的硬件加速方案，因此需要系统评估不同解码算法在定点实现下的性能表现。

Method: 在统一的低信噪比定点框架下，采用相同的图结构、矩阵和量化设置，对比研究了三种解码算法：和积算法（SPA）、最小和算法（MSA）及归一化最小和算法（NMS）。通过帧错误率（FER）和平均迭代次数等指标评估了多种量化格式的性能表现。

Result: 性能严重依赖于解码规则与数值精度的交互作用。SPA取得了最佳整体性能。对于低复杂度解码器，Q16.8是最低可靠精度，其中NMS性能优于MSA。在实际应用中，Q8.4精度的SPA在大规模实现中提供了可靠性与硬件效率的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 对于CV-QKD系统中的LDPC硬件加速实现，推荐采用Q8.4精度的SPA解码器，该方案在保证可靠性的同时实现了最优的硬件效率，为大规模部署提供了实用化解决方案。

Abstract: High-speed LDPC decoding is a major bottleneck in CV-QKD and motivates hardware acceleration with fixed-point arithmetic. This work compares SPA, MSA, and NMS under a unified low-SNR fixed-point framework using common graph, matrix, and quantization settings. Multiple formats are evaluated through FER, and average iterations. The results show that performance depends strongly on the interaction between decoder rule and numerical precision. SPA achieved the best overall performance. For reduced-complexity decoders, Q16.8 was the lowest consistent precision, with NMS outperforming MSA. Practically, SPA with Q8.4 offered the best balance between reliability and hardware efficiency for large-scale implementations.

</details>


### [35] [Hardware Mechanisms to Dynamically Throttle AI Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18069)
*Haiyue Ma,Lauren Malek,Joseph Forzani,David Wentzlaff*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组微架构控制旋钮，通过动态限制GPU硬件资源（L2缓存大小、延迟、带宽和共享内存端口访问率），实现对AI模型性能的细粒度硬件级安全干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型越来越多地集成到关键计算机系统中，缺乏对AI意图的控制促使需要安全机制。现有的软件保护措施仅施加行为约束，可能被足够智能的模型绕过。硬件级安全执行被认为是必不可少的最后一道防线，但现有机制除了策略监管或粗粒度的整芯片关闭外，缺乏架构级别的细粒度、动态干预机制。

Method: 提出了一组微架构控制旋钮，动态控制可用的硬件资源以限制运行时AI性能。评估了涵盖GPU内存子系统的候选旋钮（容量、带宽、延迟和频率维度），筛选出四个强有力的候选：L2大小、L2延迟、L2带宽和共享内存端口访问率。利用成熟的微架构原语构建这四种机制：缓存路屏蔽、基于信贷的速率限制、延迟插入和bank仲裁，以最小化新逻辑和额外设计成本。

Result: 这些控制旋钮实现了高性能敏感性（在1/8资源可用性下最高可削减80%性能）、极低的实现成本（<10K触发器）、动态限制后快速稳定（5-80K周期）以及对芯片其余部分的最小附带影响。多旋钮分析揭示旋钮组合能够放大性能下降效果，超越单个旋钮的效果，从而实现更广泛的性能目标范围。

Conclusion: 所提出的微架构控制旋钮提供了一种细粒度、动态的硬件级安全干预机制，能够有效限制AI模型性能，具有高效果、低成本和快速响应的特点，为AI安全提供了一种实用的架构级解决方案。

Abstract: As more capable AI models are increasingly integrated into critical computer systems, the lack of control over AI intent motivates safety mechanisms. Existing software safeguards impose only behavioral constraints that can potentially be bypassed by sufficiently intelligent models. While hardware-level safety enforcement has been recognized as an essential last line of defense, few mechanisms have been proposed beyond policy regulations on unauthorized accesses or coarse full-chip shutdown. What is missing is a fine-grained, dynamic intervention mechanism at the architecture level.
  In this paper, we introduce a set of microarchitecture knobs which dynamically control the available hardware resources to limit AI performance at runtime. We evaluate candidate knobs spanning the GPU memory subsystem, across capacity, bandwidth, latency and frequency dimensions, and narrow down to four strong candidates: L2 size, L2 latency, L2 bandwidth, and shared memory port access rate. To minimize new logic and extra design cost, we build all four mechanisms from well-established microarchitectural primitives: cache way masking, credit-based rate limiting, latency insertion, and bank arbitration. We show that these knobs achieve high performance sensitivity (up to 80% performance cut at 1/8 resource availability), negligible implementation cost (<~10K flip flops), fast stabilization after dynamic throttling (5-80K cycles), and minimal collateral impact on the rest of the chip. Further, multi-knob analysis reveals combinations of knobs that amplify the performance degradation beyond the effect of each knob individually, which enables a broader range of performance targets.

</details>
