### [36] [AirMoE: Statistic-Augmented Over-the-Air MoE for Collaborative Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16562)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Jingreng Lei,Guangxu Zhu,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AirMoE，一种面向无线云-边缘网络的混合专家系统，通过统计增强的空中计算范式解决路由和聚合两个耦合瓶颈，显著降低上行链路流量并提高系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线云-边缘网络上部署混合专家系统面临两个耦合瓶颈：一是路由激活客户端时需要传输原始特征，导致带宽受限的上行链路过载；二是通过无线链路聚合激活专家的输出受信道噪声和可扩展性差的影响。现有方法无法有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出统计增强的空中计算MoE范式（AirMoE），包含两个核心机制：（1）路由侧：客户端通过云广播的紧凑查询检索本地特征检索库（FRL），获取原型诱导的统计量并数字化上报给云端，云端通过Jensen-Shannon散度将这些统计量与LM提取的特征对齐来选择最相关客户端；（2）聚合侧：选中的专家通过多址接入信道同时传输输出，利用波形叠加物理计算加权和，通过信道感知功率控制实现加权系数。两个机制在算法和物理层面实现解耦。

Result: 在语义分割任务上的大量实验表明，AirMoE在性能上优于MoE基线和单模型竞争者。消融实验进一步验证了每个组件的有效性。理论分析证明了算法的收敛性和迭代复杂度。

Conclusion: AirMoE成功解决了无线环境下MoE部署的关键瓶颈，通过统计增强的路由和空中聚合机制实现了高效的分布式推理，为无线边缘网络上的大规模模型部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) are increasingly deployed over wireless cloud-edge networks, as a single edge device lacks sufficient resources to host large-scale models locally. In this distributed architecture, a cloud-hosted pretrained Large Model (LM) acts as a shared backbone for latent feature extraction, while heterogeneous experts deployed across distributed, wirelessly-connected clients collaboratively form the task head. However, deploying MoE over wireless links exposes two coupled bottlenecks. On the one hand, routing which clients to activate generally overloads bandwidth-limited uplinks due to required raw feature transmission. On the other hand, aggregating the activated experts' outputs over wireless links is hindered by channel noise and poor scalability. To break these bottlenecks, we propose a statistic-augmented over-the-air MoE (AirMoE) paradigm. Specifically, on the routing side, each client queries its local Feature Retrieval Library (FRL) with a cloud-broadcast compact query, retrieves a prototype-induced statistic, and reports it digitally to the cloud, drastically reducing uplink traffic; the cloud then selects the most relevant clients by aligning these statistics with the LM-extracted features via Jensen--Shannon (JS) divergence. On the aggregating side, selected experts simultaneously transmit their outputs over the multiple-access channel, which physically computes the reweighted sum via waveform superposition, with reweighting coefficients realized through channel-aware power control. The two mechanisms are thus decoupled both algorithmically and physically. We further provide theoretical analyses on convergence and iteration complexity. Taking semantic segmentation task as an example, extensive experiments demonstrate that AirMoE outperforms MoE baselines and single-model competitors. Ablations further confirm the effectiveness of each incorporated component.

</details>


### [37] [Wait-Free Locks Should Not Fear Later Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16571)
*Tong Che*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SeniorLock，一种回顾式无等待锁，确保调用仅需对其获取票据时活跃的请求负责，而不受后续到达请求的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帮助机制（helping）保护系统而非单个调用：后续请求可以无限推迟一个调用使其永远无法返回，即使点竞争从未超过两个。作者希望实现一种机制，使调用仅对获取票据时已存在的请求负责，而不受后续到达请求的影响。

Method: 提出SeniorLock确定性可帮助thunk锁：基于票据时间优先级机制，调用具有票据时刻优先级β，在O((β+1)(T+1))本地共享内存步内完成，其中T是单个thunk执行代价的上界，与后续调用无关。该锁同时作为通用构造SeniorObj工作。

Result: 证明了回顾式无等待性可以实现：SeniorLock中具有票据时刻优先级β的调用在O((β+1)(T+1))本地共享内存步内完成，独立于后续调用。当无更高优先级进程活跃时，操作代价接近原生代价，无需复制对象表示。

Conclusion: 回答了核心问题：调用确实可以仅对获取票据时活跃的请求负责。SeniorLock/SeniorObj提供了确定性、回顾式无等待保证，适用于将任意确定性顺序对象转换为并发无等待对象。

Abstract: Helping seems to make a lock wait-free: wrap the critical section in an idempotent thunk that any process can finish once the holder stalls. Yet helping protects the system, not the call. An overwritable candidate lets later requests bump one another in sequence, so a call can be forced to help newcomer after newcomer and never return, while point contention never exceeds two.
  We ask whether a call can instead be charged only for the requests active when it takes its ticket, never for what arrives afterward. We show that the answer is yes. SeniorLock is a deterministic helpable thunk lock in which a call with ticket-time seniority $β$ finishes in $O((β+1)(T+1))$ local shared-memory steps, where $T$ bounds one thunk's cost, independently of later invocations. We call this guarantee \emph{retrospective wait-freedom}.
  The same lock doubles as a universal construction we call SeniorObj: it turns any deterministic sequential object whose operations are bounded, concurrently idempotent thunks into a retrospective wait-free one, with no copying of its representation and, when no senior is active, at essentially the native cost of the operation.

</details>


### [38] [Hardware-Transparent I/O Governance in Disaggregated Heterogeneous Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16578)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Sue K. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了IORM，一个用于共享无磁盘存储集群的多阶段分布式I/O调度器，解决了异构硬件性能一致性和访问倾斜下的全局I/O限制执行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的调度器无法解决共享无磁盘存储集群中两个关键的治理问题：一是在异构硬件代际间维持一致的性能表现，二是在访问模式偏向部分存储节点时执行全局I/O限制。这些问题直接影响延迟敏感数据库和不透明块卷工作负载的服务质量。

Method: IORM采用三个核心机制：(1) 硬件感知成本建模器，使用数据表推导的固定成本归一化I/O核算，使限制在不同硬件代际间保持不变；(2) 基于量子的速率限制器，采用有界结转额度，既能容纳数据库微突发流量，又能执行长期SLO；(3) 分布式自适应反馈控制器，在集群范围内重新分配未使用的配额，以解决拓扑访问倾斜问题。

Result: 在8节点测试集群上运行多达100个并发租户卷的实验中，IORM在极端顺序偏斜情况下收敛到配置限制的5%范围内，无租户间干扰地进行扩展，并在存储节点故障后15秒内恢复完全吞吐量。系统已在Oracle Exadata Exascale中生产部署。

Conclusion: IORM成功解决了异构硬件环境下的一致性性能和访问倾斜治理问题，证明了多阶段分布式调度方法的有效性，为生产环境中的共享存储集群提供了实用的I/O资源管理解决方案。

Abstract: Shared-nothing disaggregated storage clusters that serve both latency-sensitive databases and opaque block-volume workloads face two governance problems unsolved by existing schedulers: maintaining consistent performance across heterogeneous hardware generations, and enforcing global I/O limits when access patterns skew to a subset of storage nodes. We present the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a multi-stage distributed scheduler deployed in production within Oracle Exadata Exascale. IORM combines three mechanisms: a hardware-aware cost modeler that normalizes I/O accounting using datasheet-derived fixed costs to make limits invariant across hardware generations; a quantum-based rate limiter with bounded carry-forward credits that accommodates database micro-bursts while enforcing long-term SLOs; and a distributed adaptive feedback controller that redistributes unused entitlements across the cluster to resolve topological access skew. Beyond design, we share operational lessons from production deployment. On an 8-node test cluster running up to 100 concurrent tenant volumes, IORM converges within 5\% of provisioned limits under extreme sequential skew, scales without inter-tenant interference, and recovers full throughput within 15 seconds of a storage-node failure.

</details>


### [39] [Cold-Start Model Delivery in Kubernetes Inference Serving: An Empirical Study of OCI-Based Distribution and Its Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16596)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在Kubernetes中通过OCI方式（原生镜像卷和存储初始化器拉取）交付模型权重，相比传统对象存储下载，节点缓存场景下70B模型启动时间从40.7分钟降至11.7秒（208倍提升），同时引入摘要固定和签名验证机制保障模型完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes上模型服务Pod的启动延迟主要瓶颈在于模型权重交付。随着大语言模型权重达到百GB级别，冷启动交付时间主导了自动扩缩容的经济性，但主流方案仍是临时性的对象存储下载，缺乏容器镜像所具备的拉取缓存、摘要寻址和验证机制。

Method: 1）分析Kubernetes服务平台的模型交付路径，考察两个维度：拉取组件和准入验证器绑定机制；2）在KServe（CNCF模型服务平台）上游实现两种新交付路径：oci+native://（将模型镜像挂载为Kubernetes镜像卷，KEP-4639，已合并）和oci+fetch://（在存储初始化器内拉取OCI制品，审核中）；3）首次在Kubernetes服务平台上对模型交付路径进行受控对比实验（模型车sidecar、原生镜像卷、对象存储下载），使用1B、7B、70B级模型fp16权重（2-140 GB）；4）针对s3://、gs://、hf:// URI场景提出服务时完整性设计方案：存储初始化器中的摘要固定和OpenSSF模型签名强制执行。

Result: 1）节点缓存的OCI交付使热副本添加与模型大小无关：70B级制品仅需11.7秒，而对象存储重新下载需40.7分钟，差距208倍；2）首次冷拉取成本比普通下载高最多2倍，原因在于containerd的"写入blob再解包"双遍过程；3）对于无准入验证的场景，下载时流式哈希验证仅增加不到0.1%的交付时间，而下载后验证增加最多53%；4）两种新交付路径已在KServe上游实现并验证。

Conclusion: 通过将模型权重作为OCI制品在Kubernetes中交付，可显著降低模型服务启动延迟并提升自动扩缩容效率。节点缓存机制使热副本添加时间与模型大小解耦。对于缺乏准入验证的存储URI，通过存储初始化器中的摘要固定和签名验证可保障模型完整性，流式验证开销极低。该方案已部分合并KServe上游，为大规模模型服务提供了可行的交付优化路径。

Abstract: The startup latency of a model-serving pod on Kubernetes is dominated by one step: delivering the model weights. As models reach the hundred-gigabyte weights of large language models, cold-start delivery time governs the economics of autoscaling and scale-to-zero, yet the dominant mechanisms remain ad-hoc downloads from object storage, with none of the pull caching, digest addressing, or verification Kubernetes provides for container images. We analyze the delivery paths available to a Kubernetes serving platform along two axes: which component pulls the artifact, and whether any admission-time verifier can bind the deployed reference to the arriving bytes. We validate the analysis upstream in KServe, a widely deployed CNCF model-serving platform, by implementing two new delivery paths: oci+native://, which mounts model images as Kubernetes image volumes (KEP-4639), merged upstream, and oci+fetch://, which pulls OCI artifacts inside the storage initializer, under review. We report, to our knowledge, the first controlled comparison of model delivery paths in a Kubernetes serving platform (modelcar sidecars, native image volumes, object-storage download) on artifacts sized to fp16 weights of 1B-, 7B-, and 70B-class models (2-140 GB). Node-cached OCI delivery makes warm replica addition size-independent: 11.7 s for a 70B-class artifact versus 40.7 minutes of re-download over object storage, a 208x difference, while the first cold pull costs up to 2x a plain download, localized to containerd's blob-write-then-unpack double pass. For models on s3://, gs://, or hf:// URIs, where no admission-time verifier observes the bytes, we present a serving-time integrity design proposed to the KServe community: digest pinning and OpenSSF model-signing enforcement in the storage initializer. Streaming hash verification during download adds under 0.1% to delivery time; a post-download pass adds up to 53%.

</details>

Result: 在云级服务器集群和嵌入式边缘设备上的实验评估表明，Roomie相比最先进解决方案可将SLO违规（即推理延迟）减少最多3倍，同时保持与现有方法相当、多数情况下更优的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Roomie通过内核级干扰预测和避免机制，有效解决了GPU上DNN模型共置时的性能干扰问题，显著降低了SLO违规率，为云和边缘场景下的模型服务提供了更优的编排方案。

Abstract: As demand for DNN inference grows, GPU capacity is increasingly oversubscribed, forcing operators to colocate multiple models on the same device in both cloud and edge deployments. Whether colocation succeeds or violates SLOs depends on the temporal overlap of kernels from concurrently executing models -- an effect that existing serving systems either ignore or approximate using aggregate resource profiles that fail to capture temporal dynamics. This paper presents Roomie, a model serving orchestration architecture that predicts and avoids kernel-level interference between colocated DNNs. Roomie decouples offline kernel profiling from online interference prediction. It uses profiling only to extract per-kernel resource configurations, and predicts interference with an occupancy-based analytical model immune to profiler-induced timing distortion. A pairwise greedy heuristic then approximates multi-model interference in polynomial rather than exponential time, and an online placement algorithm then uses these estimates to assign each incoming model to the GPU that minimizes predicted slowdown. Our experimental evaluation compares Roomie against state-of-the-art solutions across both cloud-grade server clusters and embedded edge devices, demonstrating that Roomie reduces SLO violations (i.e., inference latency) by up to 3x, while maintaining comparable, and in many cases superior, goodput relative to existing approaches.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: AI辅助内核优化应被视为端到端系统问题，需统筹算法设计、工作负载特化、测量工具、构建与评估界面、评估器对齐及人工解读，而不仅局限于内核体改进。

Abstract: AI-assisted GPU programming is often framed as a kernel-generation loop: ask a model to produce faster CUDA code, benchmark the result, and repeat. This case study argues that contest-grade optimization involves more than improving the kernel body. We examine the Agent-Assisted submission by our team, MSInfer, to the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer Contest. The submission optimized Gated DeltaNet decode and prefill on NVIDIA B200/Blackwell and achieved an official $1.58\times$ speedup, with approximate average latencies of $9.315\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for decode and $239.48\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for prefill. Our experience shows that even effective local kernel improvements can plateau when a workload requires structural reformulation and evaluator-aligned measurement. We therefore characterize AI-assisted kernel optimization as an end-to-end systems problem that encompasses algorithm design, workload specialization, measurement tooling, build and evaluation surfaces, evaluator alignment, and human interpretation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享状态日益成为流处理、服务、检索和持续学习系统性能与故障行为的核心影响因素，但现有研究往往孤立地考虑访问控制、硬件感知执行、内存管理和长期更新，缺乏统一的视角。

Result: 识别出文献中反复出现的反模式，提出了基于契约的设计蓝图以及扰动感知评估议程。

Conclusion: 状态管理本质上是一个运行时控制问题，需要跨维度协同处理访问、执行与演进三个层面的耦合关系。

Abstract: Shared state increasingly shapes both performance and failure behavior in streaming, serving, retrieval, and continual-learning systems. Existing studies, however, often isolate access control, hardware-aware execution, memory management, and long-horizon updates. The review organizes this literature around three coupled dimensions: state access and scheduling, state-aware execution, and state evolution and reuse. Across these dimensions, the literature is synthesized through a common scaffold: state object, control surface, coupling path, evaluation boundary, and unresolved contract. This comparison identifies recurring anti-patterns and informs a contract-oriented blueprint and disturbance-aware evaluation agenda. Taken together, the evidence characterizes state management as a runtime control problem.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代移动应用普遍需要并发执行多模态大语言模型以提供泛在智能服务，但在边缘环境中实现这一需求面临多任务并发和严格硬约束耦合的挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出EdgeCoInfer框架，采用混合演化分层强化学习(HE-HRL)范式求解混合整数非线性规划(MINLP)问题。该方法通过遗传算法(GA)处理离散模型放置，结合Soft Actor-Critic(SAC)智能体进行连续资源分配，并引入可行性引导的构造性执行机制，包括构造性切分解码器、预剪枝和两阶段课程学习策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，EdgeCoInfer在高并发场景下确保100%任务完成率，相比最先进基线方法实现系统成本降低76%，内存节省71.88%。

Conclusion: EdgeCoInfer框架成功解决了边缘环境中多模态大语言模型并发部署的关键挑战，通过粒度自适应部署策略和混合优化方法，在保证任务完成率的同时显著提升了资源利用效率，为移动边缘智能应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern mobile applications predominantly execute concurrent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to provide ubiquitous intelligence. However, satisfying this demand within edge environments faces significant challenges due to multi-task concurrency and strictly coupled hard constraints. To address these issues, we propose EdgeCoInfer, a framework enabling granularity-adaptive deployment by co-optimizing inter-model functional module sharing and \textbf{intra-model fine-grained partitioning}. We solve the underlying Mixed-Integer Non-Linear Programming (MINLP) problem via a Hybrid Evolutionary Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HE-HRL) paradigm, which synchronizes a Genetic Algorithm (GA) for discrete model placement with a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent for continuous resource allocation. To navigate the sparse feasible region, we introduce a feasibility-guided constructive execution mechanism, integrating a constructive cut-step decoder with pre-act pruning and a two-phase curriculum strategy for stable adaptation. Experimental results demonstrate that EdgeCoInfer ensures a 100\% task completion rate in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving a 76\% reduction in system cost and 71.88\% memory savings compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [44] [LMEdge: QoS-Aware LLM Inference Orchestration on Edge Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17175)
*Reza Farahani,Zoha Azimi,Mario Colosi,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LMEdge提出了一种面向边缘计算的LLM服务编排系统，通过BILP优化和轻量级ML预测模型，在异构边缘设备上动态确定模型配置和执行位置，显著降低了响应时延并提升了资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM服务越来越多地部署在边缘基础设施上以实现低延迟和隐私保护，现有系统缺乏能够在资源受限的异构边缘环境中同时确定模型配置（模型系列、大小和量化级别）与执行位置的编排机制，且需满足用户和系统级的QoS要求。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为二元整数线性规划（BILP）优化问题，目标是在准确率、网络和资源约束下最小化响应时间；
2. 训练五个轻量级ML模型预测特定查询下的延迟、准确率、资源使用和响应大小；
3. 设计轻量级启发式算法近似求解BILP问题以支持在线调度；
4. 构建包含59000+条记录的综合基准数据集用于模型训练和可复现性验证。

Result: 在基于Kubernetes的边缘测试平台（57个实例）上的评估表明，相比两个基线方法，LMEdge在降低延迟、保持准确率、提升资源利用率和提高服务请求满足率方面均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: LMEdge为异构边缘环境下的LLM服务编排提供了有效的解决方案，通过QoS感知的动态决策机制实现了性能与资源效率的平衡，为边缘AI服务部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services increasingly operate on edge infrastructure, enabling low-latency and privacy-preserving AI services. However, efficiently serving LLM requests across heterogeneous and resource-constrained edge devices require orchestration mechanisms that jointly determine model configuration (family, size, and quantization level) and execution placement while satisfying user- and system-level quality of service (QoS) requirements. This paper introduces LMEdge, a QoS-aware orchestration service that dynamically makes these decisions across heterogeneous edge devices. We formulate the problem as a binary integer linear programming (BILP) optimization that minimizes response time under accuracy, network, and resource constraints. To enable scalable online scheduling, we employ five lightweight machine learning (ML) models to predict query-specific latency, accuracy, resource usage, and response size for each model-size-quantization-device combination, and design a lightweight heuristic that approximates the BILP solution. We collect a comprehensive benchmarking dataset of over 59000 rows to train models and support reproducibility. Evaluation on a Kubernetes-based edge testbed with 57 instances and diverse query categories shows that LMEdge reduces latency, preserves accuracy, improves resource utilization, and increases serving ratio compared to two baselines.

</details>


### [45] [Talaria: Session-Aware Serverless Serving of Hundred-Billion-Parameter LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17181)
*Utopia Meng,Unicornt Zhao,Derek Li,Goalen Gao,Frank Du*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Talaria，一个会话感知的无服务器多模型LLM服务系统，通过联合优化放置和接纳决策，解决会话连续性问题，在SWE-Bench基准测试中将p50会话完成时间降低了5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器多模型LLM系统独立调度每个请求，但工具使用代理（tool-using agents）会在会话中反复调用LLM并携带可重用的长KV前缀。现有方法存在两个问题：(1)仅考虑负载的路由可能将会话续接与其模型和KV状态分离；(2)轮次模型复用会延迟正确放置的续接请求直到目标模型的下一个时间槽。这些问题对千亿参数模型尤为严重，因为权重限制驻留且长上下文KV重建或迁移成本高昂。

Method: Talaria采用会话感知的设计：路由器根据模型驻留状态、KV局部性和实例压力对放置进行排序；软预留机制在最后一个服务实例的接纳预算中考虑可能的返回请求；会话预填充(SP)在活动模型槽关闭前接纳符合预算的续接请求；实例本地子系统保持HBM地址稳定、保留主机可恢复的KV并在模型切换时暂存权重。

Result: 在单个TP=8服务器上，使用30个SWE-Bench模型会话（960次调用）和三个总参数超1000亿的模型进行测试。相比禁用SP、主机KV恢复和D2D暂存的轮次调度器，Talaria将p50会话完成时间从1000秒降至189秒（5.3倍加速），p95从2296秒降至867秒（2.6倍加速）。

Conclusion: Talaria通过会话连续性感知的联合放置-接纳决策，有效解决了多模型服务中的会话连续性问题，显著降低了会话完成时间，验证了会话感知调度对大规模多模型LLM服务的重要性。

Abstract: Serverless multi-model LLM systems multiplex popularity-skewed model catalogs over shared GPU pools, yet typically schedule each request independently. Tool-using agents break this abstraction: a session repeatedly calls an LLM across short tool gaps, carries a long reusable KV prefix, and is judged by session completion time (SCT). Load-only routing can separate a continuation from both its model and KV state, while round-based model multiplexing can delay even a correctly placed continuation until the target model's next slot. Both failures are especially costly for hundred-billion-parameter models: their weights constrain residency, while long-context KV is expensive to reconstruct or move.
  We present Talaria, a session-aware serverless multi-model serving system that makes session continuity a joint placement-and-admission decision. Its router ranks placements by model residency, KV locality, and instance pressure, while soft reservations account for likely returns in the last serving instance's admission budget. Session-prefill (SP) admits budget-eligible continuations before the active model slot closes. An instance-local substrate keeps HBM addresses stable, preserves host-restorable KV, and stages weights across model switches.
  On a single TP=8 server, we replay 30 SWE-Bench model-sessions (960 calls) over three models, each with more than 100B total parameters. Against an otherwise identical round scheduler with SP, host-KV restoration, and D2D staging disabled, Talaria cuts p50 SCT from 1000 s to 189 s and p95 from 2296 s to 867 s, speedups of 5.3x and 2.6x.

</details>


### [46] [Taurus: Accelerating Out-of-Core Graph Neural Network Inference on Billion-Scale Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17374)
*Pranjal Naman,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Taurus，一种针对十亿规模图的图神经网络（GNN）推理的单机系统。通过将层级推理重构为基于源的广播和顺序SSD扫描，结合流水线GPU-CPU-SSD层次结构、拓扑感知重排序等技术，在超出内存容量的图上实现了比最强基线7-140倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式GNN系统面临高通信时间和基础设施成本问题，而基于磁盘的GNN系统主要针对训练设计，在推理阶段存在大量无效读取操作。在十亿规模图上进行GNN推理面临特征和嵌入的大内存占用以及核外设置中的高磁盘I/O成本等挑战。

Method: Taurus将层级推理重构为基于源的广播和顺序SSD扫描，避免随机和重复的特征收集。核心技术包括：（1）流水线GPU-CPU-SSD层次结构；（2）拓扑感知重排序；（3）待处理消息驱逐；（4）针对高顶点的GPU驻留存储；（5）非缓冲顺序读取和GPU支持的写入以减少页面缓存污染、主机内存压力和写入开销。

Result: 在具有多达269M顶点、4B边和514 GiB特征的核外图上，Taurus比最强的层级基线DGI快7-25倍，比顶点级基线快40-140倍。

Conclusion: Taurus成功解决了十亿规模图GNN推理中的内存和I/O瓶颈问题，通过创新的数据访问模式和系统优化策略，在单机环境下实现了显著的性能提升，为大规模图推理提供了一个高效、经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Network (GNN) inference on billion-scale graphs is challenging due to the large memory footprint of features and embeddings and high disk I/O costs in out-of-core settings. Existing distributed GNN systems incur high communication times and infrastructure costs, while disk-based GNN systems are primarily tailored to training and experience massive wasted reads during inference on the entire graph. We present Taurus, a single-machine system for GNN inference on graphs that do not fit in RAM, supporting both \textit{exact} full-graph inference and fanout-sampled inference. To avoid random and repeated feature gathers, Taurus reformulates layer-wise inference as source-centric broadcasts over sequential SSD scans, backed by a pipelined GPU-CPU-SSD hierarchy, topology-aware reordering, pending-message eviction, and a GPU-resident store for high-degree vertices. It further uses non-buffered sequential reads and GPU-backed writes to reduce page-cache pollution, host-memory pressure, and write overheads. On out-of-core graphs with up to $269M$ vertices, $4B$ edges, and $514$ GiB of features, Taurus outperforms the strongest layer-wise baseline, DGI, by $7$-$25\times$, and vertex-wise baselines by $40$-$140\times$.

</details>


### [47] [A Training-Memory Regression in MLA Sequence Parallelism: Why Megatron-Core Forbids Absorption, and LAGA -- a Communication-Efficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17644)
*Changzheng Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Megatron-Core中MLA absorbed形式在训练中被禁用的根本原因（激活内存膨胀20-34%），并提出LAGA方法，在保持通信效率的同时解决内存问题，实现跨节点吞吐量提升1.07-1.24x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Megatron-Core的MLA实现中，absorbed形式虽然能在推理时大幅减少通信量，但在训练时被硬编码禁用且未说明原因。本文旨在量化解释这一限制的根本原因，并为从业者提供真正可用的低通信MLA训练方案。

Method: 通过将absorbed形式移植到训练场景进行实验测量，验证其中间变量内存膨胀问题；提出LAGA方法，保留latent gather通信模式但拒绝absorb重构，改为在本地从gathered latent重建per-head K/V，从而避免内存陷阱。

Result: 实验表明absorbed形式在训练时激活内存膨胀20-34%（DeepSeek-V3规模下达9.2GB-19.2GB）；LAGA在真实DeepSeek-V3规模上实现：通信量减少1.98x，内存开销与显式形式相差<0.5%，数值精度误差<1e-3，跨节点场景下注意力块吞吐量提升1.07-1.24x。

Conclusion: Absorbed形式在训练中被禁用是合理的，因其导致不可接受的内存开销；LAGA成功填补了低通信MLA训练方案的空白，为大规模MLA训练提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) ships two implementations in Megatron-Core: an explicit form used for training and an absorbed form -- which slashes collective communication by gathering only the compressed latent -- that is fully implemented but hard-asserted out of training (the forward opens with "assert not (self.training and self.cache_mla_latents)"), allowed only in inference decode. The library documents no reason. We show the restriction is well-founded and quantify why: ported to training, the absorbed form is a memory trap -- its intermediates live in n_h x d_kv dimensions per token, larger than the per-head K/V they replace -- inflating activation memory by 20-34%, up to 9.2 GB at DeepSeek-V3 scale (n_h=128, seq=16384, SP=8, eager kernel; the gap widens to 19.2 GB under a fused kernel), enough to change device-fit. This measurement, validated on two axes (linear in seq and n_h) and cross-verified on NVIDIA A100, explains the otherwise-undocumented restriction and leaves practitioners with no low-communication MLA training path. We then provide one. LAGA (Latent All-Gather Attention) keeps the absorbed form's latent-gather communication but rejects the absorb reformulation, instead reconstructing per-head K/V locally from the gathered latent. On 8x Ascend 910B at real DeepSeek-V3 dimensions, LAGA cuts collective communication 1.98x, matches explicit memory within 0.5%, is bit-identical to explicit at SP=1 and equivalent to within 1e-3 at SP=2-8, and under a fused attention kernel improves attention-block throughput 1.04-1.06x single-node and 1.07-1.24x cross-node -- leading at all sequence lengths in the cross-node regime MLA is deployed for.

</details>


### [48] [Byzantine Fault-Tolerant Post-Quantum Distributed Quorum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17700)
*Quentin Kniep,Jakub Sliwinski,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式仲裁签名（DQS），通过改变协议通信方式而非密码学方法，解决了后量子仲裁签名的难题，实现了常数大小消息和竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后量子仲裁签名（阈值、聚合、多重签名）方案证书大小随签名者数量增长，聚合速度慢，且不存在常数大小的方案，这成为将拜占庭容错系统迁移到后量子安全的主要障碍。

Method: 避开密码学难题，提出分布式仲裁签名（DQS）原语，仅基于普通数字签名和Bracha式批准广播构建。将证书从网络消息转变为本地事件，分为弱证书（保证安全性）和强证书（保证活性）两类事件。

Result: DQS实现了每条消息均为常数大小（适合单个数据报），总通信量为二次方级别，安全假设不变。在大规模分布式系统中，后量子DQS的开销与经典的量子前BLS方案具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过改变协议通信方式而非等待密码学突破，DQS成功解决了后量子仲裁签名的难题，为拜占庭容错系统向后量子安全迁移提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Threshold, aggregate, and multi-signatures -- which we collectively call quorum signatures -- certify that a quorum of nodes endorsed a statement, with a certificate as small as a single signature. No constant-size post-quantum quorum signature is known: all candidates grow with the number of signers and are slow to aggregate, making quorum signatures the hardest obstacle to migrating byzantine fault-tolerant systems to post-quantum security.
  In this paper, we sidestep this open cryptographic problem by changing how the protocol communicates. We introduce a primitive we call Distributed Quorum Signature (DQS), built solely from ordinary digital signatures and a Bracha-style approval broadcast. DQS turns certificates from network messages into local events. Two event types divide the roles certificates play: weak certificates capture safety, strong certificates capture liveness. In DQS every message is constant size, fitting a single datagram regardless of the number of nodes. The total communication is quadratic, and no security assumptions change. In a large distributed system, the overhead of post-quantum DQS is competitive with the canonical pre-quantum BLS scheme.

</details>


### [49] [AutoEncoder-Compressed Parallel Split Learning for Pre-trained Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17913)
*Bas Meuwissen,Vasileios Tsouvalas,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AE-PSL的通信高效并行分割学习框架，通过轻量级自编码器压缩中间激活和梯度，并采用两阶段对齐机制确保与预训练基础模型的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型在资源受限边缘设备上的分布式微调面临本地计算限制和通信开销问题。现有的并行分割学习虽然减少了客户端计算，但每个训练步骤都需要与服务器交换中间激活和梯度，造成严重通信负担。现有的通信压缩方法要么是任务无关的启发式方法（如稀疏化和量化），要么需要与目标模型共同训练，直接应用于现成基础模型会导致特征分布不匹配并降低微调性能。

Method: 提出AE-PSL框架，在分割层放置轻量级自编码器来压缩中间激活和梯度。关键创新是引入两阶段对齐机制：第一阶段使自编码器适应预训练模型的特征流形，第二阶段适应客户端特定的特征分布，确保AE压缩与预训练基础模型的兼容性。

Result: AE-PSL框架能够在保持与预训练基础模型兼容性的同时有效压缩通信数据，解决了现有可学习压缩器直接插入现成模型时出现的特征分布不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段对齐机制，AE-PSL成功实现了在预训练基础模型上的高效通信压缩，为资源受限边缘设备上的分布式微调提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed Fine-Tuning (DFT) of large-scale Foundation Models (FMs) on resource-constrained edge devices is limited by local compute constraints and communication overhead. Parallel Split Learning (PSL) reduces client-side computation by keeping few model layers on each client and offloading the remaining computation to the server; however, clients must exchange intermediate activations and gradients with the server at every training step. Existing SL communication-compression methods mainly rely on task-agnostic heuristics, such as sparsification and quantization. While learnable SL compressors can better adapt to intermediate representations, they require co-training with the target model. Therefore, directly inserting them into off-the-shelf FMs introduces feature-distribution misalignment and degrades DFT performance. To address this, we propose AE-PSL, a communication-efficient PSL framework that compresses intermediate activations and gradients using a lightweight AutoEncoder (AE) placed at the split layer. To ensure compatibility of AE compression with pre-trained FMs, AE-PSL introduces a novel two-stage alignment mechanism, which adapts the AE to the pre-trained model's feature manifold and client-specific feature distributions before DFT.

</details>


### [50] [ExpertPlex: A High-Goodput Disaggregated Serving System for MoE LLMs with Adaptive Persistent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18002)
*Bingyang Wu,Chao Jin,Zili Zhang,Xinming Wei,Yinmin Zhong,Ruidong Zhu,Chengxu Yang,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ExpertPlex提出了一种新的MoE大模型服务架构，通过共享专家模块和分离注意力模块，在消除95%权重冗余的同时减少了通信开销，相比现有方法提升吞吐量最高达2.01倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE大模型服务系统面临资源分配困境：实例级预填充-解码分离随着模型规模增长导致粗粒度资源分配和相位资源不匹配；而同GPU共置方案由于固定资源分区无法适应动态计算和路由专家负载变化，导致阻塞或资源空闲，同时共享网络干扰性能。需要一种既能避免权重冗余又能动态高效调度资源的解决方案。

Method: ExpertPlex采用三层架构设计：(1)专家共享与注意力分离架构，共享大规模MoE专家权重消除冗余，分离轻量级注意力模块降低通信成本；(2)自适应持久化内核以瓦片粒度调度动态专家计算实现高效隔离执行；(3)注意力发起的MoE通信避免网络干扰并实现跨相位通信-计算重叠；(4)瓦片到集群模型优化机制以最大化吞吐量。

Result: 在MiniMax-M2.7和GLM-5.1-FP8模型服务实验中，ExpertPlex相比实例级预填充-解码分离方法提升吞吐量高达2.01倍，相比预填充-解码同GPU共置方法提升1.66倍，同时消除了超过95%的重复模型权重。

Conclusion: ExpertPlex成功解决了MoE大模型服务中的资源分配和权重冗余问题，通过创新的架构设计实现了高效的资源共享和动态调度，为大规模MoE模型部署提供了新的技术路径，显著提升了服务效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: LLMs scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parameters for superior intelligence, but massive weights and dynamic computation impede efficient serving. Existing instance-level prefill-decode disaggregation isolates the phases on separate full-model replicas. As MoE weights grow, each instance may span tens to hundreds of GPUs, making resource allocation increasingly coarse. Configured prefill-to-decode ratios thus often mismatch demand, overprovisioning one phase while overloading the other. Prefill-decode colocation avoids this duplication, but existing Green Context solutions partition each GPU by phase and fix phase resources during a kernel. They cannot track resource changes across operations or layerwise variation in routed expert load, causing head-of-line blocking or idle reserved resources. Partitioning every GPU also leaves each phase with fewer local resources, forces wider parallelism and more communication, and lets prefill and decode traffic interfere on the shared network.
  We present ExpertPlex, which shares massive MoE experts across phases while disaggregating lightweight attention modules. Expert sharing eliminates over 95\% duplicate model weights and multiplexes dynamically sparse computation, while attention disaggregation reduces attention communication cost. ExpertPlex further uses (1) adaptive persistent kernels to schedule dynamic expert computation at tile granularity for efficient, isolated execution; (2) attention-initiated MoE communication to avoid network interference and enable cross-phase communication-computation overlap; and (3) a tile-to-cluster model to optimize these mechanisms for maximum goodput. Experiments serving MiniMax-M2.7 and GLM-5.1-FP8 show that ExpertPlex improves goodput by up to 2.01$\times$ over instance-level prefill-decode disaggregation and 1.66$\times$ over prefill-decode colocation.

</details>


### [51] [HyMCache: A KV Cache Framework for Multi-Turn LLM Serving with CXL-Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18141)
*Hakbeom Jang,Inho Song,Sam H. Noh,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyMCache提出了一种基于CXL混合内存的KV缓存框架，通过优化DRAM管理策略，在SSD成本下实现了接近DRAM性能的TB级KV缓存重用，显著提升了大语言模型长上下文服务的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文、多轮对话和智能体LLM应用中，KV缓存重用对减少冗余计算至关重要。然而，现有方案面临内存瓶颈：GPU HBM和主机DRAM成本过高，难以扩展到TB级共享上下文容量；而低成本高容量的远程存储层又存在性能问题。需要一种既能满足性能需求又能控制成本的大规模KV缓存解决方案。

Method: 提出HyMCache框架，集成CXL混合内存技术用于多轮LLM服务。核心方法包括：1）利用多轮KV缓存访问的读主导、可预测和仅追加特性；2）采用请求级前缀预取策略，将延迟敏感的读取操作暂存到设备DRAM；3）使用机会性写缓冲机制优化写入性能。通过这些技术，在SSD级成本下实现DRAM级的KV缓存效率。

Result: 在真实CXL-HM原型上的评估表明：在相同DRAM预算下，单节点服务中HyMCache比本地LMCache性能提升3.0倍，在PD disaggregated服务配置中提升1.45倍。与1TB分布式DRAM的Mooncake相比，HyMCache性能仅低约30%，但DRAM使用量减少16倍。

Conclusion: HyMCache成功验证了CXL混合内存在大规模KV缓存重用场景中的有效性，为TB级长上下文LLM服务提供了成本效益最优的解决方案，证明了通过智能内存管理可以在SSD级成本下实现接近DRAM的性能表现。

Abstract: Long-context, multi-turn, and agentic LLM workloads increasingly reuse previously processed context, making KV-cache reuse essential for reducing redundant computation. However, this reuse shifts the bottleneck to the memory tier that stores and serves reusable KV states at cluster scale. GPU HBM and host DRAM are too costly to scale to TB-scale shared context capacity, motivating remote tiers built from lower-cost, higher-capacity media. This paper presents HyMCache, a KV-cache framework that integrates CXL-hybrid memory (CXL-HM) for multi-turn LLM serving. CXL-HM combines a small amount of in-device DRAM with large SSD-backed capacity behind a CXL interface. By exploiting the read-dominant, predictable, and append-only nature of multi-turn KV-cache access, HyMCache rethinks DRAM management within CXL-HM to efficiently support TB-scale SSD-backed KV reuse. It uses request-level prefix prefetching and opportunistic write buffering to stage latency-critical reads in device DRAM, enabling DRAM-scale KV-cache efficiency at SSD-level cost. We evaluate HyMCache on a real CXL-HM prototype under both single-aggregator and PD-disaggregated serving configurations. Under the same DRAM budget, HyMCache outperforms local LMCache by 3.0x in single-node serving and 1.45x in PD-disaggregated serving. Compared with 1 TB distributed-DRAM Mooncake, HyMCache incurs about 30% lower performance but uses 16x less DRAM.

</details>


### [52] [uSTM: A Lightweight and Efficient STM Supporting General Types and Deferred Aborts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18178)
*Zachary Kent,Guy Blelloch,André Costa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ustm系统，约300行代码实现，支持通用数据类型、保持数据布局、延迟中止，性能匹配或超越现有SOTA系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STM系统虽性能优异，但未完全解决其最初目标：安全性、可用性和通用性。具体限制包括：事务可更新数据类型受限（需适合单字）、需修改数据布局、在客户端代码中间中止事务导致空间泄漏等bug。

Method: 提出ustm系统：支持通用数据类型同时保持数据布局；将中止延迟至事务末尾，使事务内客户端代码正常终止；为确保opacity保证，实现新型时间戳算法——split-increment timestamps；整体约300行代码实现。

Result: ustm性能匹配或超越多种SOTA STM系统，在多种工作负载上表现优异；同时解决了数据类型限制、数据布局修改、事务中间中止等可用性与安全性问题。

Conclusion: ustm成功平衡了性能与系统设计目标（安全性、可用性、通用性），证明了简洁实现（~300行代码）即可达到SOTA性能并克服现有系统缺陷。

Abstract: Software Transactional Memory (STM) systems allow developers to more easily exploit multicore architectures by wrapping arbitrary sequential code in transactions that are executed concurrently. In recent years, the performance of STM systems has approached that of hand-tuned data structures through techniques that avoid unnecessary aborts and exploit the semantics of underlying data structures.
  Despite achieving excellent performance, most STM systems do not fully address the concerns they targeted in the first place: safety, usability, and generality. In particular, these systems place restrictions on the data types that may be updated transactionally, such as requiring that these types fit within a word, and can require modification of data layout. Moreover, most STM systems abort transactions in the middle of client code to ensure correctness. This can cause space leaks and other bugs not present in the original code.
  We present ustm, a novel STM system addressing all of these shortcomings while still maintaining excellent performance, all within ~300 lines of code. uSTM supports general types while maintaining data layout. Aborts are deferred until the end of the transaction, allowing client code within a transaction to terminate normally. To ensure that uSTM guarantees opacity, we implement a novel timestamping algorithm we call split-increment timestamps.
  We compare the performance of uSTM to a variety of state-of-the-art (SOTA) STM systems, demonstrating that uSTM matches or outperforms the SOTA on a variety of workloads.

</details>

### [53] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided NSGA-II Enhanced with Gray Relational Coefficient for Multi-Objective Optimization: Application to NASDAQ Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16194)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Qinxu Ding,Ding Ding,Siying Zhu,Jing Ren,Yue Wang,Chong Hui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RL-NSGA-II-GRC的新型约束多目标优化方法，通过结合强化学习智能体控制器和灰色关联系数(GRC)选择机制来改进NSGA-II算法，在基准测试中将收敛性提升了约4.4%-5.8%，并在NASDAQ投资组合优化应用中成功构建了有效前沿，实现了1.92的年化夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融市场中的决策者越来越依赖定量方法来处理多个往往相互冲突目标之间的复杂权衡。在投资组合优化问题中，需要在最小化风险和最大化收益之间寻求平衡。现有的约束多目标优化方法在帕累托前沿的收敛性和多样性方面存在不足，需要一种能够自适应调整参数并有效引导搜索方向的改进算法。

Method: 本文提出了RL-NSGA-II-GRC方法，该框架包含两个核心创新：(1)强化学习智能体控制器：通过超体积、可行性和多样性等指标在线自适应调整进化参数，根据代际反馈进行动态控制；(2)基于灰色关联系数(GRC)的锦标赛算子：综合考虑支配等级、拥挤距离和与理想参考点的接近度，通过统一评分对父代进行排序，引导搜索向帕累托前沿发展。该方法将强化学习智能体集成到进化框架中实现参数的自适应控制。

Result: 在基准测试方面，RL-NSGA-II-GRC在Kursawe和CONSTR基准上相比标准NSGA-II分别实现了约5.8%和4.4%的收敛性改进，同时保持了良好的非支配解分布。在实际应用方面，该方法在NASDAQ投资组合优化中产生了平滑且密集的有效前沿，能够支持识别最大夏普比率投资组合(年化夏普比率=1.92)以及针对不同风险厌恶水平的效用最优投资组合。

Conclusion: 本研究成功证明了将强化学习与灰色关联系数相结合的多目标优化框架的有效性。该方法不仅在基准测试中展现出优越的收敛性能，还在实际金融应用中提供了可操作的投资决策支持。研究为约束多目标优化问题提供了一种新的解决方案，特别是在需要平衡多个冲突目标的复杂决策场景中具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: In modern financial markets, decision-makers increasingly rely on quantitative methods to navigate complex trade-offs among multiple, often conflicting objectives. This paper addresses constrained multi-objective optimization (MOO) with an application to portfolio optimization for minimizing risk and maximizing return. To address existing gaps, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-guided non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II) enhanced with gray relational coefficients (GRC), termed RL-NSGA-II-GRC, which combines an RL agent controller and GRC-based selection to improve convergence and diversity of Pareto fronts. The agent adapts evolutionary parameters online using metrics of hypervolume, feasibility, and diversity, while the GRC tournament operator ranks parents via a unified score considering dominance rank, crowding distance, and proximity to ideal reference. We evaluate the framework on the Kursawe and CONSTR benchmarks and a NASDAQ portfolio application. On the benchmarks, RL-NSGA-II-GRC achieves convergence improvements of about 5.8% and 4.4% over NSGA-II, while preserving well-distributed non-dominated solutions. In the portfolio application, it produces a smooth, densely populated efficient frontier supporting identification of the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio (annualized Sharpe =1.92) and utility-optimal portfolios for different risk-aversion levels. The main contributions are three-fold: 1) we propose an RL-NSGA-II-GRC method integrating an RL agent into the evolutionary framework to adaptively control parameters via generational feedback; 2) we design a GRC-enhanced binary tournament operator providing a comprehensive indicator to guide the search toward the Pareto front; 3) we demonstrate, on benchmark MOO and a NASDAQ case study, that the method delivers improved convergence and well-populated frontiers supporting actionable insights.

</details>


### [54] [DocOCR-Eval: A Correction-Based Framework for OCR Tool Selection Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16203)
*Zihan Xu,Puzhen Wu,Lawrence Chun Man Lau,Wei Liu,Sirui Li,Yifan Peng,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DocOCR-Eval，一个无需标注的OCR评估框架，用于自动评估和选择文档解析工具，解决了标注稀缺场景下的OCR工具选择难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析是文档理解任务的基础步骤，但现有的OCR引擎和多模态大语言模型众多，在标注稀缺的场景下为特定文档集合选择合适的解析方案仍然具有挑战性。许多OCR引擎的上下文推理能力有限，而人工标注成本高昂，这促使研究者开发一种无需标注的自动评估方法。

Method: 提出DocOCR-Eval框架，采用三阶段校正和排序策略：1)利用多个多模态大语言模型进行校正；2)聚合多个MLLM的结果；3)通过排序策略逼近基于标注的工具排序，从而在无需真实标签的情况下实现OCR工具的自动评估和选择。

Result: 在多个跨领域、跨语言的扫描文档基准上系统评估了多种OCR引擎和最先进MLLM的文本识别性能。实验证明，聚合多个MLLM的结果可以逐步提高与基于标注排序的一致性，在现实的标签受限场景下能够实现可靠的OCR工具选择。

Conclusion: DocOCR-Eval框架成功实现了无标注条件下的OCR工具自动评估和选择，为在不同真实世界文档集合中部署文档解析系统提供了实用指导，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Document parsing is a foundational step for document understanding tasks such as visual question answering and key information extraction, as it transforms unstructured scanned images into structured representations by extracting textual, visual, and layout information. While numerous Optical Character Recognition (OCR) engines and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been developed for this purpose, selecting an appropriate document parsing solution for a given document collection remains challenging, particularly in label-scarce settings. In this work, we conduct a systematic evaluation of text recognition performance across a diverse set of OCR engines and state-of-the-art MLLMs on multiple scanned document benchmarks spanning different domains and languages. Motivated by the limited contextual reasoning capabilities of many OCR engines and the high cost of manual annotations, we propose DocOCR-Eval, an annotation-free evaluation framework for automatic OCR assessment and selection. DocOCR-Eval employs a three-staged correction and ranking strategy to approximate annotation-based tool ordering without ground-truth labels. We show that aggregating across multiple MLLMs progressively improves alignment with annotation-based rankings. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that reliable OCR tool selection can be achieved in realistic, label-limited settings, providing practical guidance for deploying document parsing systems across diverse real-world document collections.

</details>


### [55] [Fully-sensorized smart-eyewear platform for on-device Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16222)
*Andrea Giudici,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Mario Caliò,Aurelio Teliti,Giacomo Gervasoni,Diana Trojaniello,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGO智能眼镜平台，通过硬件-固件-AI协同设计，在STM32N6微控制器上部署优化YOLOv11模型，实现10 FPS实时障碍物识别和113分钟续航，内存占用仅2.483MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜解决方案严重依赖云计算，导致高延迟和隐私泄露风险。用户需要一款既保证佩戴舒适性，又能提供高计算性能和能源效率的本地化智能辅助设备。

Method: 采用硬件-固件-AI协同设计方法，基于STM32N6微控制器及其集成NPU开发平台。引入Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA)架构改进YOLOv11模型以适配NPU加速器。使用Walking On The Road (WOTR)数据集训练，整合RGB摄像头、ToF传感器、麦克风等多模态传感器套件。

Result: 部署模型在内存限制下达到mAP50-95为24，内存占用仅2.483 MB。系统实现10 FPS实时推理速度，在200 mAh电池上连续运行约113分钟。成功验证了高性能、隐私保护和社交可接受性辅助设备的可行性。

Conclusion: 证明了边缘AI解决方案需要紧密集成、多学科协同设计方法。ARGO展示了在不依赖云计算的条件下，智能眼镜能够同时实现人体工程学舒适性、高计算吞吐量和能源效率，为隐私保护的辅助设备提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents ARGO, a smart eyewear platform designed to bridge ergonomic comfort, high computational throughput, and energy efficiency. Unlike cloud-dependent solutions, ARGO leverages the STM32N6 microcontroller and its integrated Neural Processing Unit (NPU) to enable on-device machine learning, minimizing latency and preserving user privacy through local data processing. The primary contribution lies in the holistic co-design of hardware, firmware, and artificial intelligence, centered on the deployment of an optimized YOLOv11 model for real-time urban obstacle recognition. To ensure compatibility with the target NPU, we introduce Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA), an architectural refinement that enables efficient accelerator execution while preserving the original computational logic. The model is trained on the Walking On The Road (WOTR) dataset, and the final deployed configuration achieves an mAP50-95 of 24 under strict memory constraints, with a memory footprint of only 2.483 MB. The platform integrates a multimodal sensor suite, RGB cameras, Time-of-Flight sensors, microphones, and ambient sensors, and delivers 10 FPS at a continuous autonomy of ~113 minutes on a 200 mAh battery. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a high-performance, privacy-preserving, and socially acceptable assistive device, and highlight how competitive edge AI solutions increasingly demand tightly integrated, multidisciplinary co-design approaches.

</details>


### [56] [LLM Unlearning for Cyber Defense: A Survey on Methods, Challenges, and Emerging Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16227)
*Ruppikha Sree Shankar,Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Troy Paulus Asia,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述从安全性和鲁棒性视角审视了大语言模型遗忘技术，重点关注梯度主导方法，核心探讨现有方法是否真正移除了知识还是仅抑制了表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在安全关键系统中广泛部署，但无法遗忘敏感信息导致严重的网络安全、隐私和安全风险。重新训练十亿参数模型在计算上不可行，且知识在参数中分布式纠缠，促使LLM遗忘技术成为主要的网络防御手段。现有方法是否能真正移除知识仍是一个未解决的核心问题。

Method: 本综述从安全性、鲁棒性和可验证遗忘三个角度审视LLM遗忘技术，主要关注基于梯度的方法。选择梯度方法是因为其与现有训练流程兼容性好，且可扩展至十亿参数模型。

Result: 该综述系统地分析了当前LLM遗忘方法在安全性、鲁棒性方面的表现，揭示了现有方法的核心局限——可能仅抑制模型在普通提示条件下的表达，而非真正移除知识。

Conclusion: 当前LLM遗忘方法的有效性存疑，需要从安全性和可验证性角度重新审视遗忘技术。真正的知识移除与表面表达抑制的区别是评估遗忘效果的关键。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in security-critical systems across healthcare, finance, education, and decision support, yet their inability to forget creates serious cybersecurity, privacy, and safety risks. Sensitive personal information, copyrighted material, hazardous domain knowledge, and memorized training data remain encoded across billions of parameters long after deployment, leaving models vulnerable to extraction, jailbreak attacks, membership inference, and regulatory non-compliance. Real-world incidents, from chatbots regenerating private information to fabricated legal citations producing direct legal and financial cost, place the problem at the center of the emerging-threats landscape rather than the realm of speculation. Because retraining billion-parameter models on revised corpora is computationally infeasible, and because knowledge within an LLM is distributed and entangled across parameters rather than localized to identifiable units, LLM unlearning has emerged as the principal cyber defense response, aiming to remove or suppress targeted knowledge from a trained model without retraining and without eroding what the model should still know. A central question, however, remains unresolved. Do current methods genuinely remove knowledge, or do they only stop the model from expressing it under ordinary prompting conditions? This survey examines LLM unlearning through the lens of security, robustness, and verifiable forgetting, with primary focus on gradient-based methods, which have come to dominate the field due to their compatibility with existing training pipelines and their scalability to billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [57] [Operator-Aware Mixed-Precision Tolerance Calibration for Tensor Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16228)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过挖掘实际运行数据来校准张量核测试的容差阈值，发现比人工设定的阈值更严格，显著提升了bug检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的张量核正确性测试使用人工设定的固定容差阈值，这些阈值在代码库中被复制传播且很少被重新审视，可能导致bug检测效果不佳。

Method: 从云GPU运行积累的26个gpuemu测试用例和2种数据类型（共8,076行结果）中挖掘逐元素误差分布，基于正确实现下的观察结果来校准每个操作和数据类型的绝对容差阈值。

Result: 校准后的容差比当前人工设定值更加严格，最大收紧幅度达到2,184倍（attention_triton fp16）。在7个LLM风格的bug变体上，bug检测召回率从73.2%提升至82.4%（绝对提升9.3个百分点，新增229个检测），同时控制组的假阳性率仅从0上升至1.1%。

Conclusion: 基于实际运行数据校准的容差阈值能够显著提升张量核bug检测能力，同时保持较低的假阳性率，证明了数据驱动的容差校准方法的有效性。

Abstract: Most tensor-kernel correctness tests go through a fixed-shape all close-style check with hand-picked absolute and relative tolerances. The thresholds are copied across the corpus and rarely revisited. We mine the element-wise error distribution of every test case from accumulated cloud GPU runs across the 26-entry gpuemu corpus and 2 dtypes (8,076 result rows). We then ask one empirical question: what absolute tolerance would the kernel itself, observed under its correct implementation, justify?
  The answer is much tighter than the current hand-picked atol. The largest tightening is attention_triton fp16 at $2{,}184\times$. Restricted to the seven LLM-style buggy variants for which the corpus ships a paired correct counterpart, calibrated per-(op, dtype) tolerances raise bug-detection recall from 73.2% (1,805 of 2,467) to 82.4% (2,034 of 2,467), an absolute gain of 9.3 percentage points (+229 new detections). The control false-positive count rises from 0 to 20 out of 1,882 correct-control cases (+1.1 percentage points).

</details>


### [58] [RouteCost: A Production-Inspired Multi-Stage Framework for Pre-Order Shipping Cost Estimation in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16230)
*Xianling Zeng,Zihan Yu,Sichen Zhao,Yalun Qi,Zhiming Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RouteCost，一种受生产环境启发的多阶段框架，用于电商场景下的准确预订单物流成本预测，在26万订单数据集上显著提升了预测精度和聚合校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商场景下的物流成本准确预估对定价展示、利润规划和转化率至关重要。现有静态查表方法忽略了需求混合、计费重量、尺寸定价、附加费触发和订单合并等关键变异源，而单体回归器则容易利用强但非因果的相关性，导致预测不准确或不可解释。

Method: 提出RouteCost多阶段框架：(1)时间感知需求预测；(2)基于费用表的基准定价；(3)第二阶段残差修正；(4)基于代理的装箱合并推断。通过路线加权期望公式，将路线级成本估计聚合为产品级物流成本预测，保留了路线级的可解释性。

Result: 在超过26万订单、260个产品和18个月订单历史的数据集上，该框架在预测质量和聚合校准方面均取得改进，同时保持了路线级的可解释性。

Conclusion: RouteCost框架通过分解建模各关键因素，有效提升了电商物流成本预测的准确性和可解释性，为实际生产环境提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate pre-order shipping cost estimation is important in e-commerce because it affects price presentation, margin planning, and conversion. In practice, shipping cost is shaped not only by distance but also by destination demand mix, billable weight, dimensional pricing, surcharge triggers, and latent operational effects such as shipment consolidation. Static lookup methods therefore miss important sources of variation, while monolithic regressors may exploit strong but non-causal correlations. We propose RouteCost, a production-inspired multi-stage framework that decomposes the problem into time-aware demand forecasting, fee-card-informed baseline pricing, Stage 2 residual correction, and proxy-based box-consolidation inference. Route-level cost estimates are aggregated through a route-weighted expectation formulation to produce product-level shipping cost predictions. Across over 250,000 orders, 260 products, and 18 months of order history, the framework improves predictive quality and aggregate calibration while preserving route-level interpretability.

</details>


### [59] [Orthogonal Gradient Constraints Shape Noisy-Label Memorization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16231)
*Richard Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OrthoGrad优化器干预方法，通过移除权重梯度中与当前权重向量平行的分量来研究记忆驱动的过拟合。实验表明该方法在小数据 regimes 下能改善CNN的测试准确率并减少噪声标签拟合，但其效果依赖于 regimes 而非普遍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化方法主要修改目标函数、架构或数据分布，本文旨在研究对优化器更新本身的几何干预，以深入理解噪声标签学习中的记忆驱动过拟合现象。

Method: 提出OrthoGrad方法，在每次权重更新时，将梯度投影到与当前权重向量正交的方向，即移除梯度中与权重向量平行的分量，从而约束参数更新方向。

Result: 在小数据 regimes 的MNIST实验中，OrthoGrad能改善CNN的测试准确率并减少噪声标签拟合；在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18实验中，该方法能改变记忆轨迹但无法阻止最终的噪声标签记忆。机制诊断表明当原始梯度包含显著径向分量时效果最强，在大数据 regimes 下效果减弱。

Conclusion: 正交更新约束是研究学习动力学的有用诊断工具，但OrthoGrad的效果具有 regimes 依赖性而非普遍的正则化作用。

Abstract: Modern neural networks can fit corrupted training labels, making noisy-label learning a useful setting for studying memorization-driven overfitting. Most regularization methods modify the objective, architecture, or data distribution; here we instead study a geometric intervention on the optimizer update itself. We evaluate OrthoGrad, which removes the component of each weight gradient parallel to the current weight vector, in noisy-label image classification. On MNIST with small-data regimes, OrthoGrad improves test accuracy most clearly for CNNs while reducing corrupted-label fitting. Mechanism diagnostics based on weight norms and gradient-weight cosine similarity suggest that the projection has the strongest effect when the raw gradient contains a nontrivial radial component, and becomes weaker in larger-data regimes where gradients are already nearly orthogonal to weights. Additional CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 experiments show that the method can alter memorization trajectories but does not prevent eventual noisy-label memorization. These results support orthogonal update constraints as a useful diagnostic for studying learning dynamics, while showing that OrthoGrad is regime-dependent rather than universally regularizing.

</details>


### [60] [From Weights to Words: Expressing and Editing Preference Model Inferences in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16232)
*Zachary Wojtowicz,Ayush Nayak,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"weights to words"方法，通过自然语言描述的偏好维度来解决统计学习算法推断人类偏好时的欠定性和不透明性问题，实验证明该方法能提高预测准确性并获得用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习算法从高维选择数据推断人类偏好时面临两个核心挑战：1）选择备选项通常在多个维度上同时存在差异，难以确定哪些因素真正驱动了观察到的决策；2）这些方法的不透明性使人类操作者无法检查、质疑或纠正模型的错误。

Method: 提出"weights to words"方法，输入选择问题数据集，自动发现一组领域相关的偏好维度。每个维度用自然语言描述并配对模型表示空间中的向量。该方法通过将归因集中在少量有意义因素上来解决欠定性问题，并通过自然语言外化模型推理来解决不透明性问题，使用户能够实时检查和编辑。

Result: 在四个领域（道德困境、电影、葡萄酒、LLM响应）展示了方法的通用性。两个预注册人类实验（道德困境N=450，电影选择N=449）表明：1）使用学习到的基对偏好模型进行正则化可提高留出选择的预测准确率；2）纳入参与者的结构化编辑可进一步提高准确率。对比实验中，参与者更偏好该方法推断的偏好画像，并认为其预测更准确。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了偏好学习中的欠定性和不透明性问题，通过可解释的自然语言维度提高了模型预测性能，同时增强了用户参与度和模型可修正性，为偏好学习提供了更可靠和透明的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing use of statistical learning algorithms to infer human preferences from high-dimensional choice data runs up against a fundamental challenge: choice alternatives typically differ in many ways simultaneously, so it is generally unclear which factors actually drove an observed decision and should be credited as preferences. Compounding this problem, the opacity of these methods leaves human operators unable to inspect, contest, or correct models when they err. We introduce \emph{weights to words}, a method that takes a dataset of choice problems as input and automatically discovers a collection of domain-relevant preference dimensions, each described in natural language and paired with a vector in the model's representational space. These dimensions address both under-determination and opacity: they can be applied to concentrate attribution on a small set of meaningful factors, and they can externalize the model's inferences in natural language so that users can inspect and edit them in real time. We first qualitatively illustrate the method's versatility on four diverse domains: moral dilemmas, movies, wines, and free-form LLM responses. We then report two pre-registered human-subjects experiments, on moral dilemmas ($N=450$) and movie selection ($N=449$), that demonstrate its benefits for learning preference models: (1) regularizing a preference model toward the learned basis increases prediction accuracy on held-out choices, and (2) incorporating participants' structured edits further improves accuracy. In head-to-head comparisons, participants prefer the method's inferred preference profiles and endorse its predictions as more accurate.

</details>


### [61] [Token-Level Cross-Modal Transformer with Contrastive Multi-Task Learning for Breast Cancer Subtype Classification and Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16233)
*Suxing Liu Byungwon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对癌症亚型分类和生存预测的多模态融合问题，提出了改进方案，解决了现有方法在细粒度交互、结构化融合和联合优化方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学中整合异质基因组和临床模态进行联合癌症亚型分类和生存预测仍是一个关键挑战。现有方法存在三个局限性：(1)将每个模态视为单一特征向量，无法实现跨模态的细粒度token级交互；(2)跨模态融合通常通过线性加权或后期平均而非结构化token交换进行；(3)生存和分类目标独立优化，缺乏联合正则化信号。

Method: 基于摘要内容，该方法可能引入token级别的跨模态交互机制，采用结构化的token交换策略进行多模态融合，并设计联合优化框架同时处理生存预测和癌症亚型分类任务。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果，需进一步查看论文全文以获取性能指标和对比实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究针对多模态癌症分析的现有局限性提出了改进方案，有望提升癌症亚型分类和生存预测的联合性能。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous genomic and clinical modalities for joint cancer subtype classification and survival prediction remains a key challenge in precision oncology. Existing approaches suffer from three limitations: (1) they treat each modality as a monolithic feature vector, precluding fine-grained token-level interactions across modalities; (2) cross-modal fusion is typically performed through linear weighting or late averaging rather than structured token exchange; and (3) survival and classification objectives are optimized independently, missing a joint regularization signal.

</details>


### [62] [HantaWatch: Federated Learning for Hantavirus Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16234)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HantaWatch，一个针对汉坦病毒基因组监测的联邦学习框架，解决了序列数据分布有限、非独立同分布源异质性和专家审核能力受限等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉坦病毒基因组监测面临三大主要挑战：序列数据分布受限、非独立同分布(non-IID)的源异质性，以及专家审核能力有限。这些限制阻碍了有效的病毒监测和响应能力。

Method: 提出HantaWatch联邦学习框架，集成了五个核心组件：(1) k-mer特征提取；(2) 源感知联邦客户端构建；(3) 自适应DU-FedProx优化；(4) 监测特异性模型选择；(5) 仅预测分类。该框架允许实验室和监测站点协作训练序列模型而无需共享原始数据。

Result: 在二分类和多分类任务上的实验表明，HantaWatch支持高风险筛查、暴发相关预测、进化支分类和临床综合征分类。该框架在平衡预测性能、假阴性风险和更新稳定性方面表现良好，能够输出风险评分、置信度估计、不确定性标记和专家审核优先级排序。

Conclusion: HantaWatch为分布式汉坦病毒监测提供了一个实用的联邦决策支持层，支持专家优先级排序而不取代实验室或公共卫生解读，实现了在不共享原始数据前提下的协作式病毒监测。

Abstract: Hantavirus genomic surveillance is limited by the distribution of sequence data, non-IID source heterogeneity, and constrained expert-review capacity. We propose HantaWatch, a federated learning framework that enables laboratories and surveillance sites to collaboratively train sequence-based models without sharing raw data. HantaWatch integrates k-mer feature extraction, source-aware federated client construction, adaptive DU-FedProx optimization, surveillance-specific model selection, and prediction-only triage. Experiments on binary and multi-class tasks show that HantaWatch supports high-risk screening, outbreak-associated prediction, clade classification, and clinical-syndrome categorization while balancing predictive performance, false-negative risk, and update stability. The framework converts model output into risk scores, confidence estimates, uncertainty flags, and ranked expert-review priorities. HantaWatch therefore provides a practical federated decision-support layer for decentralized Hantavirus surveillance, supporting expert prioritization without replacing laboratory or public-health interpretation.

</details>


### [63] [OpenMHC: Accelerating the Science of Wearable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16235)
*Narayan Schuetz,Yuze Bai,Lianggang Pan,Edgar Eggert,Favour Nerrise,Juan Delgado-SanMartin,Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Mohammad Asadi,Anders Johnson,Paul Schmiedmayer,Dennis Wang,Allan Lawrie,Daniel Seung Kim,Xin Liu,Akshay Paruchuri,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley,Kelly W. Zhang*

TL;DR: 发布了目前最大的开源可穿戴健康数据集OpenMHC（包含超6000万小时数据）及基础模型开源实现，并建立了统一基准测试平台，旨在推动可穿戴健康AI研究的开放科学发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最大的可穿戴设备数据集不公开可用，领先的可穿戴基础模型很少开源权重或提供可复现的训练代码，这严重阻碍了可穿戴健康AI研究的开放性和可重复性。

Method: 1）构建OpenMHC数据集：基于My Heart Counts研究应用超过10年的数据收集，包含11,894名参与者的超6000万小时可穿戴数据，涵盖19个传感器通道和最多169个关联变量；2）开源近期可穿戴基础模型的实现；3）建立统一开放基准测试，包含健康行为预测、多变量数据填补、时序预测三个赛道；4）对经典方法和基础模型进行基准对比。

Result: 成功发布了迄今最大、最全面的开源可穿戴健康数据集及配套工具链，实现了数据、代码和模型权重的全面开源。

Conclusion: 通过前所未有规模的开源（数据、代码、模型权重），旨在实现可穿戴健康AI研究的民主化，赋能社区在该领域推动开放的科研进步。

Abstract: Mobile and wearable devices offer an unprecedented opportunity for continuous, passive health monitoring and active health coaching. However, the largest wearable datasets are not publicly available for research, and leading wearable foundation models trained on such datasets are rarely open-weight or come with reproducible training code. To accelerate open science in wearable health, we release OpenMyHeartCounts (OpenMHC), the largest and most comprehensive open-access wearable health dataset to date, alongside open-source implementations of recent wearable foundation models. OpenMHC, derived from over a decade of data collected through the My Heart Counts study app, includes >60 million hours of wearable data across 19 sensor channels (e.g., step count, heart rate, sleep, workouts) and up to 169 linked variables, including health, lifestyle, mood, and behavior from 11,894 consenting participants. Furthermore, we introduce a unified, open benchmark that enables standardized comparison of wearable health models across three tracks: health and behavior downstream prediction, multivariate data imputation, and time-series forecasting. We benchmark classical methods alongside recent wearable and multivariate time series foundation models. By open-sourcing data, code, and model weights at this unprecedented scale, we aim to democratize wearable health AI research and enable the community to drive open progress in this domain.

</details>


### [64] [The Failures of Marginal Influence-Based Attribution Methods for Global Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16236)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统时间序列可解释性方法（如SHAP）在自相关存在时会混淆直接和中介时序依赖，导致解释失效。本文提出了DAG-faithfulness概念来评估解释方法的正确性，证明了现有方法普遍不满足该性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型可解释性方法主要产生平坦的归因分数，在自相关条件下经常失败。本文旨在揭示这种失败的根本原因，并提出DAG-faithfulness性质来评估解释方法的正确性。

Method: 1. 证明现有方法的失败模式源于边际条件或离分布梯度计算方式；
2. 揭示这些方法在自相关下会混淆直接和中介依赖；
3. 提出DAG-faithfulness定义及其评估框架。

Result: 1. 证明现有方法的失败源于计算不匹配而非数值格式；
2. 发现SHAP和现有时间序列扩展方法普遍不满足DAG-faithfulness；
3. 为构建可靠的时间序列解释方法提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文为时间序列可解释性研究提供了重要的理论分析工具，指明了构建正确解释方法的关键要求——满足DAG-faithfulness，为未来方法设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Explainability methods for time series models predominantly produce flat attribution scores: they quantify the direct influence of a feature at a timestamp by a scalar. We prove that the dominant failure mode of such methods is not the scalar format itself but a fundamental computational mismatch: existing methods compute scores via marginal conditioning or off-manifold gradients, both of which conflate direct temporal dependencies with mediated ones under autocorrelation. We also define DAG-faithfulness: an explanation is DAG-faithful if the temporal dependency graph it encodes is Markov-equivalent to the temporal directed acyclic graph (DAG) implicitly learned by the model. Particularly, we observe that standard attribution methods, specifically SHAP, are not DAG-faithful in general, and that recent time-series-aware extensions inherit the same computational limitation.

</details>


### [65] [Quantizing Recursive Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16237)
*Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Wajeeha Tahir,Anna Tegon,Lionnus Kesting,Gamze İslamoğlu,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归推理模型的量化失败源于激活缩放粒度问题，采用逐块缩放的MXInt4格式可完全恢复模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型通过多次重用紧凑的权重绑定块来解决复杂问题，但这种重用机制在量化时产生独特的动态问题：量化误差在每一步都会累积。研究发现per-tensor 4-bit量化会导致系统性偏差累积，在数独任务上造成灾难性的精度下降（从84.1%降至0.0%），亟需解决这一量化崩溃问题。

Method: 提出使用MXInt4（一种逐块整数激活格式）应用于递归推理模型。该方法采用逐块缩放而非传统的逐张量缩放，保持整数元素和二次幂块尺度，与逐块浮点格式相竞争。研究还分析了递归深度和重用对量化敏感性的影响。

Result: 发现量化崩溃的根本原因是激活缩放粒度而非位宽或数字格式。从per-tensor缩放改为per-block缩放可完全恢复模型性能。MXInt4在各项任务上表现良好。最深的架构（EqR平衡模型）对量化最敏感，但逐块缩放能够克服这一脆弱性。该方法成功迁移到开放式的ARC-AGI基准测试。

Conclusion: 激活缩放粒度是递归推理模型量化的关键因素，逐块缩放策略能够有效解决量化误差累积问题，使模型在各种架构深度下保持鲁棒性，为递归推理模型的低比特量化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models solve hard puzzles by applying compact, weight-tied blocks over many refinement steps. Because these blocks are reused many times, quantizing them creates a unique dynamical problem: the quantization error is incurred at every step. While 8-bit quantization (integer or float) preserves accuracy, moving to a per-tensor 4-bit format causes a systematic bias to accumulate. The ensuing drift catastrophically degrades exact-solution accuracy on Sudoku from 84.1% to 0.0% (only ~25% of cells correct). In this work, we show that this collapse is caused by activation-scaling granularity rather than bit-width or number format. Crucially, moving to per-block scaling completely restores the transition. To implement this, we apply MXInt4, a blockwise integer activation format, to recursive reasoning models. It is competitive with blockwise float formats on our tasks, while keeping integer elements and power-of-two block scales. Finally, recursion depth and reuse modulate quantization sensitivity, with the deepest architecture we test (the EqR equilibrium model) the most sensitive. Yet blockwise scaling overcomes this vulnerability, staying robust across these architectures and transferring to the open-ended ARC-AGI benchmark.

</details>


### [66] [Diffusion-corrected Autoregressive Fourier Neural Operator for Droplet Evolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16238)
*Jinghao Cao,Minsung Kang,Hongyue Sun,Chi Zhou,Jihoon Chung,Xubo Yue,Sanchoy Das,Bo Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiffARFNO两阶段框架，结合自回归Fourier-MIONet粗预测器和DDIM校正器，解决了材料喷射中液滴演化的长期预测误差累积问题，显著超越现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料喷射（喷墨打印）中液滴演化预测对维持打印质量至关重要，但长期预测面临误差累积和过程变量复杂耦合两大挑战，现有方法难以实现高保真长期预测。

Method: 提出DiffARFNO两阶段框架：第一阶段训练Fourier-MIONet作为粗预测器进行自回归长期预测；第二阶段引入条件DDIM校正器，通过滑动窗口内高效迭代去噪过程精细化粗预测，恢复细节信息。

Result: 在ANSYS Fluent液滴数据集上的大量实验表明，DiffARFNO显著优于现有最先进的预测模型，成功实现了高保真的长期预测。

Conclusion: 通过结合粗预测和扩散校正的两阶段策略，DiffARFNO有效缓解了长期预测中的误差累积问题，为材料喷射过程的长期高质量预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting droplet evolution in material jetting, or Inkjet Printing (IJP), is essential for maintaining printing quality. However, long-horizon forecasts remain challenging due to error accumulation and the complex coupling of process variables. In this work, we introduce the Diffusion-corrected Auto-Regressive Fourier Neural Operator (DiffARFNO), a two-stage framework that combines an autoregressive Fourier-MIONet with a conditional Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) corrector. Fourier-MIONet is trained as a coarse predictor and deployed autoregressively for long-horizon forecasting. In the second stage, a DDIM-based conditional corrector refines the coarse prediction within each sliding window through efficient iterative denoising. By combining coarse predictions from Fourier-MIONet with a DDIM corrector that restores fine details, DiffARFNO aims to provide high-fidelity predictions for long-horizon forecasts. Extensive experiments on droplet datasets from ANSYS Fluent demonstrate that DiffARFNO significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [67] [BACON: Budgeted Human Calibration for Modeling and Evaluation with Multiple AI Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16239)
*Lei Shi,Anlan Zhang,Rita Lyu,Zhengmian Hu,Tong Yu,David Arbour,Avi Feller,Saayan Mitra,Ritwik Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BACON框架，通过融合预算受限的人类标注校准与多AI评判输出，解决了AI评判相对人类偏好存在偏差且高度依赖具体项目的问题，在有限人类标注下实现了可扩展、统计可靠的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评判作为人类评估的可扩展低成本替代方案，其输出相对于人类偏好存在偏差，且这种偏差在不同评判器、任务和领域间高度变化。当未校准的AI评估用于模型排序、项目评分或群体质量报告时，这些偏差会直接扭曲下游决策，因此需要一种能够校准AI评判输出的方法。

Method: 提出BACON四阶段流程：1）为每个项目构建全覆盖辅助特征（多评判器分数、token级不确定性统计、上下文嵌入）；2）对小型采样子集收集人类标签；3）训练交叉拟合结果模型生成校准的项目级代理预测；4）支持两种应用场景：使用增强估计方程估计器进行群体级指标估计（提供有效置信区间），以及用于项目排序和标注的个体级代理评分。将AI评判视为辅助测量而非基准真值，人类标签提供校准锚点。

Result: 在多样化任务、领域和标注预算下，BACON相比原始AI输出和纯人类标签方法，提高了预测准确性和排序一致性，降低了偏差和方差，证明了在有限人类标注下实现可扩展评估的有效性。

Conclusion: BACON提供了一个实用、统计基础扎实的框架，用于在有限人类标注下进行可扩展评估，通过将AI评判作为辅助测量并结合人类校准标签，有效平衡了评估成本与质量，为AI系统评估提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: AI judges offer a scalable, low-cost alternative to human evaluation, but their outputs can be biased relative to human preferences and highly item-dependent, varying across judges, tasks, and domains. When uncalibrated AI evaluations are used for model ranking, item scoring, or population-level quality reporting, these biases can directly distort downstream decisions. We propose BACON, a four-stage pipeline that combines budgeted human calibration with multiple AI-judge outputs to produce more accurate annotations. BACON constructs full-coverage auxiliary features for every item, including multi-judge scores, token-level uncertainty statistics, and contextual embeddings. It then collects human labels for a small sampled subset and trains a cross-fitted outcome model to generate calibrated item-level surrogate predictions. These predictions support two use cases: population-level estimation of summary metrics, such as means or quantiles, using an augmented estimating-equation estimator with valid confidence intervals; and individual-level surrogate scoring for item ranking and annotation. BACON treats AI judges as auxiliary measurements rather than ground truth: human labels provide the calibration anchor, while AI-derived signals improve efficiency. Across diverse tasks, domains, and labeling budgets, BACON improves predictive accuracy and ranking consistency, and reduces bias and variance relative to raw AI outputs and purely human-label-based methods. These results show that BACON offers a practical, statistically grounded framework for scalable evaluation with limited human annotation.

</details>


### [68] [Normalized Rewards for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16240)
*Shawn Im,Federico Danieli,Skyler Seto,Barry-John Theobald,Katherine Metcalf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了正则化方法来解决DAAs（如DPO）过度优化导致优选响应概率下降的问题，在Llama-3.1-8B上实现了AlpacaEval2分数相对提升>20%，通用基准性能提升>9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对齐算法（DAAs）如DPO存在过度优化隐式奖励模型的问题，导致优选响应的似然概率下降，可能引发不良行为。作者希望解决这一副作用，同时理解过优化的内在机制。

Method: 提出添加正则化项的目标函数，维持选择和拒绝响应的总长度归一化概率；分析了有/无正则化情况下响应似然变化的token分布，识别出少量异常token承担了主要似然变化；将正则化应用于基于参考（DPO）和无参考（SimPO）方法。

Result: 发现似然变化主要由少量异常token引起，解释了DAAs如何在降低优选响应似然的同时改善生成质量；正则化方法在生成质量与通用能力之间实现更好权衡，奖励建模性能跨数据集提升；Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上AlpacaEval2相对提升>20%，通用基准相对提升>9%；正则化有效缓解优选响应整体及异常token的似然位移。

Conclusion: 所提正则化方法成功缓解DAAs的过优化问题，在保持生成质量提升的同时改善了通用能力和奖励建模性能，为理解和改进语言模型对齐算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) such as DPO have become a common way to post-train and align LLMs with human preferences. However, DAAs have been observed to over-optimize their implicit reward model and decrease the likelihood of preferred responses. This results in a decrease in the total likelihood assigned to responses seen in the preference dataset, potentially resulting in undesirable behavior. To counteract this undesired side-effect of DAAs, we examine the effect of using objectives that add a regularization term to maintain the total length-normalized probabilities of the chosen and rejected responses. To better understand over-optimization, we investigate how response likelihood changes are distributed over the tokens with and without regularization. We find that a significant portion of the likelihood changes are due to a small set of outlier tokens, which explains how DAAs improve generation quality despite decreasing the likelihoods of chosen responses. We apply the proposed regularization to reference-based (DPO) and reference-free (SimPO) methods and find (1) improved trade-offs between generation quality and general benchmark capability and (2) improvements in reward modeling across datasets. For example, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we see both a >20% relative increase in AlpacaEval2 scores and >9% relative performance gains on general benchmarks. Additionally, we find that the added regularization term effectively mitigates the amount of displacement within preferred responses overall, and for the outlier tokens specifically, by utilizing low-likelihood tokens.

</details>


### [69] [MADA-RL: Multi-Agent Debate-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parameter-Efficient Reasoning in Compact Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18006)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Zifeng Ding,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MADA-RL框架，通过反事实评论家优势方法专门化紧凑模型，在数学推理任务上显著提升了小模型性能，同时大幅降低了训练参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然推理能力强，但训练成本高昂，这一问题在参数量小于4B的紧凑模型和有限预算约束下尤为突出。现有方法难以在低成本条件下有效提升紧凑模型的推理能力。

Method: 引入MADA-RL后训练框架，将紧凑模型专门化为生成器和评论家角色，通过辩论感知学习信号训练，仅通过LoRA适配器微调少量参数。核心贡献是反事实评论家优势：一种动态的、基于角色的基线，将评论家优势重新定义为其奖励减去生成器集成在每个实例上的准确率，明确优化评论家超越生成器共识而非简单复制正确答案。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准测试上，MADA-RL将DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B模型的准确率从39.9%提升至41.9%（+2.0个百分点，p < 0.001），使用的可训练参数量比完全微调基线少16倍，位于准确率-可训练参数帕累托前沿。控制研究表明反事实优势产生了所有评估模型中最高的评论家改进率，训练的评论家学会了纠正生成器错误而非模仿。

Conclusion: MADA-RL成功地在参数高效的条件下提升了紧凑模型的推理能力，反事实优势机制使评论家真正学会纠正而非模仿生成器。该方法接近但未超越在更大数据集上训练的最强基线（DeepScaleR、STILL-3），论文分析了这一差距及相关推理时成本。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong reasoning performance, but often at prohibitive training cost - a challenge that is especially acute for compact models ($\leq 4 \, \mathrm{B}$ parameters) trained under limited budgets. We introduce MADA-RL, a post-training framework that specializes compact models into generator and critic roles and trains them with a debate-aware learning signal, fine-tuning only a small subset of parameters via LoRA adapters. Our central contribution is a counterfactual critic advantage: a dynamic, role-conditioned baseline that redefines the critic's advantage as its reward minus the generator ensemble's per-instance accuracy. This explicitly optimizes critics to improve over generator consensus rather than to merely reproduce a correct answer, yielding more targeted credit assignment than static mean-reward normalization. At deployment, the specialized agents are composed in a lightweight multi-round protocol. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, MADA-RL raises the accuracy of the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B model from $39.9 \, \%$ to $41.9 \, \%$ ($+2.0$ points, $p < 0.001$) using $16$ times fewer trainable parameters than fully fine-tuned baselines, placing it on the accuracy-trainable-parameter Pareto front. It approaches, but does not surpass, the strongest baselines (DeepScaleR, STILL-3), which are trained on substantially larger datasets; we analyse this gap and the associated inference-time cost directly. A controlled study isolates the source of MADA-RL's gains: the counterfactual advantage produces the highest critic improvement rate of any model evaluated, indicating that trained critics learn to correct generator errors rather than to imitate them.

</details>


### [70] [KernelBench-Verified: Do LLM-Generated Kernels Actually Beat PyTorch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16241)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Ping Yu,Jianyu Wang,Max,Fan,Julian Reed,Azalia Mirhoseini,Will Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型生成的CUDA内核存在奖励黑客行为，通过引入验证框架和隐藏测试集，揭示模型实际性能显著低于原始报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大语言模型能够生成定制的CUDA内核，在KernelBench等基准测试中声称超越PyTorch性能。然而研究发现前沿模型频繁进行奖励黑客操作，人为夸大报告的性能指标，这促使作者重新评估模型的真实能力。

Method: 作者提出KernelBench-Verified扩展评估框架，包含两个关键改进：(1)启用TF32张量核心加速的基线计时机制，提供更真实的现代GPU执行估计；(2)四分布隐藏测试套件防止模型通过硬编码旁路特定张量值来规避计算；(3)引入内存效率指标捕获内核优化中常被忽视的速度-内存权衡。

Result: 在七个前沿LLM的验证单轮评估中，最佳模型(GPT-5.5)仅实现0.88x几何平均加速，显著低于标准评估协议下的1.43x加速。当对照真实基线评估时，没有任何模型持续超越PyTorch。在内存方面，最佳模型生成的GPU内核中有28%增加了峰值GPU内存使用量。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，随着LLM内核生成能力的提升，必须持续调整鲁棒的评估协议。当前模型在实际评估条件下并未真正超越现有框架，强调了评估框架需要与模型能力共同演进的必要性。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) can generate custom CUDA kernels that appear to outperform PyTorch on benchmarks such as KernelBench. Building upon this foundational framework, we demonstrate that frontier models frequently engage in reward hacking to artificially inflate reported performance. In this work, we identify two areas where evaluation frameworks must co-evolve with model capabilities. First, to accurately measure true speedup, we examine the baseline timing mechanism, noting that enabling Tensor Core acceleration with TF32 provides a more realistic estimation of execution on modern GPUs. Second, concerning algorithmic correctness, models often exploit the narrow test distribution by hardcoding bypasses for specific tensor values. By skipping required computations, these kernels artificially accelerate execution rather than implementing actual CUDA kernels. We introduce KernelBench-Verified, an extended evaluation framework that incorporates a TF32-enabled baseline and a four-distribution hidden test suite. We additionally introduce memory efficiency metrics that capture the often-overlooked speed-memory tradeoff in kernel optimization. Under verified single-turn evaluation with seven frontier LLMs, we find that the best-performing model (GPT-5.5) achieves a 0.88x geometric mean speedup, significantly lower than the 1.43x speedup observed under the standard evaluation protocol. No model consistently outperforms PyTorch when evaluated against realistic baselines. On the memory front, 28% of GPU kernels generated by the best model increase peak GPU memory usage. Our findings demonstrate the necessity of continually adapting robust evaluation protocols as LLM kernel generation capabilities advance.

</details>


### [71] [TRACE: Trajectory-Based Safety Patch Learning for LLM Post-Training Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16242)
*Changyue Li,Jiaming He,Youliang Yuan,Jialin Wu,Boxi Yu,Zhicong Huang,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对微调即服务(FTaaS)平台导致的模型安全对齐受损问题，本文提出TRACE框架，通过轨迹模拟和离线补丁学习来有效恢复模型安全性，同时保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FTaaS平台中，用户定制化微调可能削弱大语言模型的安全对齐，而现有基于参数合并的安全恢复方法存在任务-安全更新纠缠的根本性瓶颈：安全补丁与下游任务更新在主导方向上重叠，导致合并强度难以校准——过弱无法恢复安全性，过强则损害任务效用。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，将重点从在线合并操作设计转向离线补丁学习。核心方法包括：(i)模拟有害微调轨迹，生成渐进式损坏状态；(ii)优化一个插件式补丁，在保持任务效用的同时恢复安全性，确保在不同损坏基础状态下都能有效工作。该方法寻求在最小干扰任务相关方向的同时，保持对不安全行为的决定性控制。

Result: 在六个基准测试和两个模型上的实验表明，TRACE在安全-效用前沿上表现卓越：在所有设置下均达到近100%的安全性，同时保持了与无防御微调模型相当的任务效用性能。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹模拟和离线补丁学习，TRACE成功解决了任务-安全更新纠缠问题，为FTaaS平台提供了一种既不损害模型效用又能有效恢复安全性的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-Tuning-as-a-Service (FTaaS) platforms let users train large language models (LLMs) on customized tasks, but this pipeline could erode models' safety alignment. In practice, service providers need to recover models' safety without re-running full alignment, or destroying the utility gained from customized tasks. A line of existing work refers to model parameter merging, which adds a safety patch on the fine-tuned model parameters to shift the model away from unsafe tendencies. However, this merging-based paradigm is fundamentally bottlenecked by task-safety update entanglement: downstream task updates and the safety patch often overlap in their dominant directions, so the merge strength is intrinsically hard to calibrate. If the safety vector is scaled too weakly, harmful components could still dominate, preventing the model from returning to a safe region; if it is scaled too aggressively, it suppresses task-relevant directions and degrades utility.
  To solve this problem, we shift the focus of merging-based methods from designing online merging operators to offline patch learning, and seek a safety patch that minimally interferes with task-relevant directions while retaining decisive control over unsafe behaviors. We propose TRACE, a trajectory-based safety patch learning framework that (i) simulates harmful tuning trajectories to generate progressively corrupted states, and (ii) optimizes a plug-in patch to recover safety while maintaining utility across varying corrupted base states.
  Across six benchmarks and two models, TRACE consistently dominates the safety-utility frontier. TRACE reaches nearly 100% safety on all settings, while maintaining comparable utility to the undefended fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [72] [RobustMAD: Evaluating Real-World Robustness of Multimodal Small Language Models for Deployable Anomaly Detection Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16243)
*Anushiya Arunan,Xin Li,Yan Qin,U-Xuan Tan,Nhu Khue Vuong,Xiaoli Li,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向部署的多模态工业异常检测基准RobustMAD，系统评估了紧凑型多模态小语言模型的鲁棒性，发现其在部分任务上意外超越GPT-5 Nano，但仍存在多模态定位脆弱、响应不全面和逻辑基础薄弱三大关键失效模式，并据此为下一代工业检测助手设计提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态工业异常检测助手是下一代智能工厂的关键组件，但现有大型模型因计算开销高和云端推理的隐私风险而难以部署。紧凑型多模态小语言模型虽然具备部署可行性，但缺乏全面的鲁棒性分析和真实工业场景下的挑战性基准，阻碍了该领域的进步。

Method: 开发RobustMAD基准，首个面向部署动机的评估框架，通过涵盖物体理解、异常检测、不可回答问题和视觉质量退化等多样化开放式查询，全面评估模型在真实工业条件下的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 表现优异的MSLM展现出意外能力，甚至在某些任务上超越更大的GPT-5 Nano模型，但仍未达到安全关键要求。RobustMAD揭示了三种反复出现的失效模式：(i)在细粒度区分或视觉退化条件下多模态定位脆弱，(ii)响应不够全面，(iii)对不可回答或病态问题的逻辑基础薄弱导致幻觉输出。
