For air-quality classification, models achieved high balanced accuracy when PM2.5 was included, but performance decreased substantially when PM2.5 was removed, indicating strong dependence on pollutant-related information. SHAP analysis showed that, without PM2.5, socioeconomic and meteorological variables such as GDP per capita, precipitation, and healthcare access became more influential. Subgroup analysis showed similar aggregate regression error across income groups, but PM2.5 contributed more strongly to predictions in lower-middle-income countries. These results show that model accuracy alone is not sufficient for climate-health prediction. Interpretable models can help identify dominant pollution-related signals, test whether results depend on key pollutant variables, and show whether prediction patterns differ across socioeconomic groups.

</details>


### [142] [ChemFusion: A Multimodal Cross-Attention Network for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17033)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ChemFusion混合神经网络，通过交叉注意力机制融合电子描述符与3D原子坐标，有效解决了过渡金属催化反应预测中的表示瓶颈，并在交叉偶联反应预测中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属催化反应结果预测极具挑战性，核心瓶颈在于如何有效融合广泛的电子描述符与反应位点局域的三维几何结构，传统方法存在表示鸿沟。

Method: 开发了ChemFusion混合神经网络，利用交叉注意力机制融合传统电子特征与显式3D原子坐标，使全局电子态能够动态关注分子点云中的特定空间约束，无需池化操作。

Result: 在多样化交叉偶联反应库的基准测试中，该方法的预测性能显著优于传统单模态框架；注意力矩阵分析表明模型能自主学习识别并惩罚限制性空间位阻。

Conclusion: 空间感知网络能够有效处理标准统计模型难以捕捉的复杂反应位阻问题，提供了物理可解释性，为化学反应预测开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Forecasting the outcomes of transition-metal-catalyzed reactions is notoriously complex due to the interplay of diverse physical and chemical variables. A persistent computational bottleneck has been effectively merging broad electronic descriptors with the localized, three-dimensional geometry of the reactive site. To bridge this representation gap, we present ChemFusion, a hybrid neural network that fuses conventional electronic features with explicit 3D atomic coordinates. Using a cross-attention mechanism, the model enables global electronic states to dynamically attend to specific spatial constraints within un-pooled molecular point clouds. When benchmarked against a diverse library of cross-couplings, this approach delivers exceptional predictive performance, decisively surpassing traditional single-modality frameworks. Importantly, extracting the attention matrices reveals that the architecture autonomously learns to identify and penalize restrictive steric hindrances. This provides a physically grounded interpretability, demonstrating that spatially aware networks can navigate complex reaction sterics that standard statistical models typically miss.

</details>


### [143] [Apeliotes: A Diffusion-Based Modeling Framework for km-scale Multi-Level Atmospheric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17037)
*Evangelia Rafaela Frastali,Achyut Paudel,Maryam Golbazi,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Apeliotes，一个基于机器学习的公里级高分辨率天气预报框架，通过结合预训练的全球天气基础模型和区域生成扩散模型，实现了对多种气象变量和多层大气场的精准预测，在风速和温度预测上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率天气数据在许多地区仍然有限，而传统基于动力学的降尺度方法计算成本高、难以跨地区/变量/预测场景扩展，这推动了开发基于机器学习、可随机生成多变量高分辨率气象场的降尺度系统。

Method: 提出了Apeliotes框架，基于全球再分析大气数据、预训练的全球天气基础模型以及区域训练的生成扩散模型，直接生成公里级高分辨率天气变量及多层大气场（这些场在现有全球大气数据中不可直接获取）。

Result: 模型在垂直风廓线预测中误差小于3%，10米风速预测相关系数达0.91（NRMSE=0.42），2米温度预测相关系数达0.99（NRMSE=0.17），展现了极具竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: Apeliotes框架成功实现了高分辨率天气预报，能够准确提供公里级气象变量和多层大气场，克服了传统动力降尺度方法的计算成本与扩展性限制，为高分辨率气象数据生成提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-resolution atmospheric data are required to resolve mesoscale and localized meteorological structures, however such datasets remain limited in many regions of the world. Existing high-resolution weather products are typically produced through dynamical downscaling, which is computationally expensive and difficult to scale across locations, variables, and forecast scenarios. These limitations motivate machine-learning-based downscaling systems that can generate multiple weather variables stochastically while producing new high-resolution fields directly. In this paper we present Apeliotes, a framework for high-resolution weather forecasting. Built on the global re-analysis atmospheric data, a pre-trained global weather foundation model, and a regionally trained generative diffusion model, Apeliotes not only provides accurate kilometer-scale weather variables, but also multi-level atmospheric fields which are not directly available in the existing global atmospheric data. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Apeliotes achieves highly competitive performance. The model predicts vertical wind profile with less than 3\% error between truth and predicted fields, achieving correlations of 0.91 for 10-m wind speed and 0.99 for 2-m temperature, with NRMSE values of 0.42 and 0.17, respectively.

</details>


### [144] [Solver-Hard Is Not Model-Hard: A Hardness-Controlled Diagnostic for LLM Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17047)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在近匹配子句密度条件下，LLM约束推理器的判决准确性与理论证明难度及求解器代理难度存在分离现象，且在特定模型中观察到对表面结构的高度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM约束推理评估常在随机SAT相变附近进行，混淆了实例密度与求解器难度。作者希望区分理论证明难度与求解器经验难度，并检验LLM是否能在近匹配密度条件下正确传递推理能力。

Method: 通过在相近规模区间内近匹配子句密度和匹配最大子句宽度，比较理论证明难度不同的公式族（扩展型Tseitin与阶梯型Tseitin、鸽巢锚点），并引入密度不匹配对照组。使用求解器特定代理（Glucose平均冲突数）衡量经验难度，评估LLM准确性与代理的相关性，并设计证明保留的标签重排实验测试模型表面敏感性。

Result: 理论和求解器代理难度在公式族间显著分离（代理差异高达51倍），但三个模型的近匹配密度准确率差范围仅为-32到+20点，汇总差为+1.7点（p=0.74），且正确率与冲突数关联方向错误（r=+0.15）。证明保留的标签重排对一个模型在所有五个聚类中均大幅降低准确率（平均-93点），而另一模型则无影响。16k推理模型在证明易解的匹配公式上花费更多，并在求解器最易的UNSAT族上耗尽预算；32k C1的准确率差消失。

Conclusion: LLM的判决准确性与观察到的token花费在特定条件下与理论证明难度及求解器代理难度发生分离，揭示了模型表面敏感性和局限性，但该结论仅限于判决准确性和观察到的token花费，不涉及证书求解、精确证明长度或分配效率。

Abstract: LLM constraint reasoners are often evaluated near the random-SAT phase transition, confounding density and solver hardness. We test instance-level transfer while near-matching clause density. At aligned size bins, with near-matched density and matched maximum clause width, we compare proof-hard expander-Tseitin and proof-easy ladder-Tseitin formulas, pigeonhole anchors, and density-mismatched controls. Theory separates their resolution hardness; a solver-specific Glucose mean-conflict proxy differs by up to $51\times$, and five other solvers preserve the direction. Across three included models (243 instances each; a fourth is excluded for abstention), the near-matched-density accuracy gaps range from $-32$ to $+20$ points, with a pooled gap of $+1.7$ points ($p=0.74$) and a wrong-signed correctness-versus-conflict association ($r=+0.15$). A proof-preserving relabeling lowers accuracy in all five clusters for one model (mean $-93$ points) but not another, exposing model-surface sensitivity. In a preregistered extension, provider-reported completion-token spend does not consistently increase with the proxy after accounting for formula length and censoring. At 16k, the reasoning model spends more on proof-easy matched formulas and exhausts its budget on the solver-easiest UNSAT family; the 32k C1 gap is absent. These scoped dissociations concern verdict accuracy and observed token spend, not certificate solving, exact proof length, or allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [145] [What does a Bayes-filtered transformer believe? A predictive Monte Carlo approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17060)
*Afiq Abdillah Effiezal Aswadi,Haotong Ma,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了预测蒙特卡洛(PMC)方法，用于解释贝叶斯过滤变换器(BFT)中隐含的先验和后验分布，解决了现有方法在预测空间比较中的脆弱性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯过滤变换器解释方法通过比较预测分布来推断模型内部化的先验和后验，但这种方法存在脆弱性：不同的后验分布可能产生相同的后验均值预测，导致无法准确识别模型实际实现的算法或计算过程。

Method: 提出预测蒙特卡洛(PMC)作为BFT的一般性可解释性工具。PMC仅使用下一词元生成能力，通过采样近似获得隐含的潜在任务先验和后验分布，直接在潜在空间中回答可解释性问题，而非在预测空间中进行间接比较。

Result: 将PMC应用于三个涵盖0-马尔可夫和1-马尔可夫可交换性的风格化任务族。实验结果表明，先前在这些设置中报告的现象在潜在空间中仍然可见，验证了PMC方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PMC提供了一种直接在潜在空间解释BFT的通用工具，克服了现有预测空间比较方法的局限性，能够准确揭示训练模型实际内部化的先验和后验分布。

Abstract: A Bayes-filtered transformer (BFT) is a transformer trained on sequences that are generated in two steps: first a latent task is drawn from a prior, then observations are drawn conditional on that task. Trained under autoregressive log loss, the BFT's next-token prediction, in the idealized limit, is the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution (PPD) induced by that prior and that conditional law. In practice the trained BFT is only an approximation of this ideal PPD, raising an interpretive question: what prior and posterior over the latent task has the trained BFT actually internalized? Existing work answers this question by comparing the trained BFT's predictions against the predictions of various "reference" posteriors, each standing in for a different candidate algorithm or computation the BFT might be implementing. This prediction-space comparison is fragile: different posteriors can share the same posterior-mean predictions. We use predictive Monte Carlo (PMC) as a general interpretability tool for any BFT: using only next-token generation, PMC returns an approximation to the implicit prior and posterior over the latent task, answering the interpretive question directly in latent space. We apply PMC to three stylized task families spanning 0-Markov and 1-Markov exchangeability. The phenomena previously reported in these settings remain visible in latent space. Code is available at https://github.com/afiq-aswadi/bft-pmc

</details>


### [146] [Persistent Sparse Autoencoders: Learning Feature Timescales in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17117)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出持久稀疏自编码器（Persistent SAEs），通过学习特征持久系数捕获序列中的持久信息，实验证明其能学习不同时间尺度的特征，为语言模型解释和监控提供新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器（SAEs）独立编码每个token，无法暴露序列中持续存在的信息。缺乏对跨序列持久信息的建模能力限制了SAEs在语言模型解释和监控中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出持久稀疏自编码器（Persistent SAEs），在标准SAEs基础上为每个特征学习一个持久系数（persistence coefficient），使模型能够学习哪些特征应该持久以及持久多长时间，从而捕获特征在不同时间尺度上的持续性。

Result: 1) 保持竞争性的重建质量；2) 学习到特征时间尺度谱系：快速特征作为局部可解释检测器，慢速特征将主题级信息集中在持久状态中；3) 在提示注入监控案例研究中，慢速特征能保持检测信号并在长上下文中保持因果有效性。

Conclusion: Persistent SAEs通过持久语义表示为解释和监控语言模型开辟了新机会，能够有效捕获和利用序列中的持久信息。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language model activations into sparse features, but standard SAEs encode each token independently and do not expose information that persists across a sequence. We introduce Persistent Sparse Autoencoders (Persistent SAEs), which extend standard SAEs by learning a persistence coefficient for each feature, allowing the model to learn which features should persist and for how long. Our experiments show that they retain competitive reconstruction quality while learning a spectrum of feature timescales: fast features behave as locally interpretable detectors, whereas slow features concentrate topic-level information in a persistent state. Moreover, as shown in a prompt-injection monitoring case study, slow features preserve detection signals and remain causally effective over long contexts. These results suggest that Persistent SAEs open up new opportunities for interpreting and monitoring language models through persistent semantic representations.

</details>


### [147] [Robust Assamese Speech Recognition through Controlled Fine-Tuning of Whisper Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17164)
*Ganapati Das,Dwipen Laskar,Hasin Afzal Ahmed,Sanjib Kr Kalita,Kshirod Sarmah,Hem Chandra Das,Manjula Kalita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言阿萨姆语，通过微调预训练Whisper模型并采用硬件优化训练策略，显著提升了ASR性能，在错误率和语义评估上均大幅优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对形态丰富且标注数据稀缺的低资源语言（如阿萨姆语），预训练的Whisper模型在其语音识别任务上表现不佳，亟需提升该类语言的ASR性能。

Method: 基于Mozilla Common Voice 24.0-Assamese语料库，对Whisper模型进行控制性微调；在资源受限环境下实现了硬件感知的优化训练流程，采用混合精度训练和梯度累积技术，并在T4 GPU上进行训练。

Result: 微调模型在多项指标上显著优于零样本基线：WER、CER、MER和WIL分别降至43.17%、13.18%、43%和64.81%，相对提升78.26%、93.10%、57.0%和35.19%；语义评估BLEU和METEOR得分分别为30.81和0.5262；幻觉率和实时因子（RTF）分别降低96.70%和32.38%。

Conclusion: 所提出的微调Whisper模型在低资源阿萨姆语ASR任务上取得了显著性能提升，验证了针对低资源语言进行模型微调与硬件优化训练的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Developing Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) for morphologically rich, low-resource languages such as Assamese is challenging due to insufficient annotated speech data. The pretrained Whisper model performs poorly on Assamese speech recognition tasks. This paper presents a controlled, fine-tuned Whisper-based Assamese ASR system trained on the Mozilla Common Voice 24.0-Assamese corpus. A hardware-aware optimized training pipeline is implemented for resource-constrained environments, employing mixed-precision training and gradient accumulation on Tesla 4 Graphics Processing Units (T4 GPUs). The proposed fine-tuned model significantly outperformed the Zero-shot baseline, yielding Word Error Rate (WER), Character Error Rate (CER), Match Error Rate (MER), and Word Infomation Loss (WIL) of 43.17\%, 13.18\%, 43\%, and 64.81\%, respectively, achieving significant relative improvements of 78.26\%, 93.10\%, 57.0\%, and 35.19\% over the baseline. Semantic evaluation of the fine-tuned model also demonstrates notable improvement over a zero baseline, attaining Bilingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and Metric for Evaluation of Translation with Explicit ORdering (METEOR) scores of 30.81 and 0.5262, respectively. Additionally, the predicted hallucination rate and Real-Time Factor (RTF) are substantially improved by 96.70\% and 32.38\%, compared to the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining and Tuning Transformer-based LLMs in Arithmetic Tasks with Human Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17166)
*Luyu Qiu,Jianing Li,Hwanhee Kim,Xiaoyong Wei,Yueyuan Zheng,Janet Hsiao,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现基于Transformer的大语言模型在整数算术任务中表现出与人类学习者相似的学习模式，并通过应用人类教育策略显著提升了模型性能，为理解LLMs的认知过程提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的大语言模型在多种NLP任务中表现优异，但在基础算术等简单问题上表现不佳，引发了对模型可靠性、安全性和伦理部署的担忧。研究者希望探索是否可以借鉴人类学习方法来改善LLMs的算术能力。

Method: 将算术任务分解为明确定义的子任务，进行损失收敛顺序分析和消融研究；借鉴人类学习策略，应用问题解决策略和认知赋能方法来训练vanilla Transformer模型；采用可解释AI（XAI）方法进行可视化验证和解释性分析。

Result: 发现LLMs展现出与人类学习者相似的学习模式：简单子任务的学习速度快于复杂子任务；成功通过应用人类学习策略提高了LLMs的算术准确率；通过准确性提升实验、可视化验证和解释性分析全面验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的LLMs可能在算术学习中与人类学习者共享认知过程，这一发现通过可解释AI验证得到支持，有助于增强对LLMs在关键和高风险应用中的信任，为LLMs的教育训练方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) continue to achieve state-of-the-art performance across various natural language processing tasks. However, their subpar performance on seemingly elementary problems, such as basic arithmetic, raises concerns about model reliability, safety, and ethical deployment. In this study, we demonstrate that the performance of a vanilla Transformer model trained on integer arithmetic tasks can be improved using methods effective for human learners. We begin by decomposing the arithmetic task into well-defined subtasks and conducting loss convergence order analysis together with ablation studies for each subtask. Our findings reveal that LLMs exhibit learning patterns similar to those of human learners, with a faster learning speed for simpler subtasks compared to more complex ones. In addition, we successfully improved the accuracy of LLMs by applying problem-solving strategies and cognitive empowerment methods shown to enhance the performance of human learners. This suggests that transformer-based LLMs may share cognitive processes with human learners in arithmetic. Lastly, we provide a comprehensive demonstration of our method's effectiveness, including significant accuracy improvement experiments, visualization verification, and explanation-based analysis to illuminate the intricacies of LLMs in arithmetic learning. In general, this work explores the potential similarities between transformer-based LLMs and human learners, supported by explainable AI (XAI) verifications, ultimately fostering trust in LLMs for critical and high-stakes applications.

</details>


### [149] [DADIR: Density-Aware Data-level Imbalanced Regression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17178)
*Shermin Shahbazi,Hossein Mohammadi,Mohsen Afsharchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DADIR框架，通过密度感知的自适应分区、密度正则化条件变分自编码器和潜在空间数据平衡三个组件，有效解决了不平衡回归问题，在少数类区域表现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡回归方法依赖于固定的目标分区或合成样本生成，未能联合考虑密度变化和局部特征空间结构，导致在处理连续目标变量和异构密度分布时效果不佳。

Method: 提出DADIR框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）密度感知自适应分区（DAAP），根据密度变化递归划分目标空间；（2）密度正则化条件变分自编码器（DR-CVAE），在学习潜在特征的同时保留稀疏区域表示；（3）潜在空间数据平衡，结合特征级聚类和过采样生成结构一致的合成样本。

Result: 在多种不平衡回归数据集上的实验表明，该方法在预测性能上有持续改进，特别是在代表性不足的区域表现突出，同时也提高了整体准确性。

Conclusion: DADIR能够更有效地识别少数类区域，保留稀疏区域信息，并生成逼真的合成数据，生成的平衡数据集可直接用于现有回归模型而无需修改其架构或学习目标。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning addresses predictive modeling problems with underrepresented regions of the data distribution. Although widely studied in classification, imbalanced regression remains challenging because of continuous target variables and heterogeneous density distributions. Existing data-level methods often rely on fixed target partitioning or synthetic sample generation without jointly considering density variations and local feature-space structure. We propose DADIR, a Density-Aware Data-level Imbalanced Regression framework that exploits density information throughout the balancing process. DADIR comprises three components: (1) Density-Aware Adaptive Partitioning (DAAP), which recursively partitions the target space according to density variations; (2) a Density-Regularized Conditional Variational Autoencoder (DR-CVAE), which preserves sparse-region representations while learning latent features; and (3) latent-space data balancing, which combines feature-level clustering with oversampling to generate structurally consistent synthetic samples. Together, these components identify minority regions more effectively, preserve sparse-region information, and generate realistic synthetic data. The resulting balanced dataset can be used directly with existing regression models without modifying their architecture or learning objective. Experiments on diverse imbalanced regression datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in predictive performance, particularly in underrepresented regions, while also improving overall accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [DynImmune-BERT: Dynamic Immune Repertoire Modeling with Neural ODE Driven Continuous Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17244)
*Rong Fu,Yongtai Liu,Xiaowen Ma,Haoyu Zhao,Shuo Yin,Yiqing Lyu,Long Zhang,Wangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynImmune-BERT，一种连续时间T细胞受体库模型，用于患者水平免疫状态预测。相比静态模型，该模型能够更好地捕捉纵向样本中的克隆动态变化，在有时间序列数据时表现优于静态编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态T细胞受体库语言模型通常将样本视为序列的集合，难以充分表示采样间隔、测序深度和克隆存在模式等关键信息，无法有效捕捉免疫扰动后克隆扩张、收缩、消失和重现等动态变化特征。

Method: 提出DynImmune-BERT连续时间模型，结合以下技术：(1)深度自适应中心对数比初始化；(2)克隆存在性门控神经ODE动力学；(3)有界邻域自注意力机制；(4)基于事件的状态重启；(5)混合迁移目标函数监督主导和稀有克隆质量；(6)低秩元适配器初始化重现的克隆型，保持参数量独立于观察到的克隆数量。

Result: 评估结果表明：(1)在有纵向受体库结构可用时，事件感知的时间建模可以补充强大的静态编码器；(2)小规模外部队列和协议差异需要谨慎解释；(3)模型提供了校准和阈值诊断，并可视化潜在克隆轨迹和注意力邻域。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在纵向T细胞受体库数据中引入时间动态建模的有效性，为免疫状态预测提供了新的方法学框架，但也强调了在小样本外部验证和跨协议应用时需要谨慎解释结果。

Abstract: Longitudinal T cell receptor repertoires contain signals of clonal expansion, contraction, disappearance, and reappearance after immune perturbation. Static repertoire language models usually summarize a sample as a bag of sequences, so the sampling interval, sequencing depth, and clone presence pattern are only weakly represented. This paper presents DynImmune-BERT, a continuous time repertoire model for patient level immune status prediction. The method combines depth adaptive centered log ratio initialization, clone presence gated Neural ordinary differential equation dynamics, bounded neighborhood self attention, event based state restart, and a hybrid transport objective that supervises dominant and rare clone mass. A low rank meta adapter initializes reappearing clonotypes while keeping the parameter count independent of the number of observed clones. The evaluation separates literature reported baselines from internally controlled temporal comparisons, reports uncertainty for small external cohorts, adds calibration and threshold diagnostics, and visualizes latent clone trajectories and attention neighborhoods. The results indicate that event aware temporal modeling can complement strong static encoders when longitudinal repertoire structure is available, while small external cohorts and protocol differences require cautious interpretation.

</details>


### [151] [Distilled Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17247)
*Chen Wang,Zhaochun Li,Jionghao Bai,Yining Zhang,Hexuan Deng,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出蒸馏强化学习（Distilled RL）方法，结合教师监督和强化学习目标，解决了现有强化学习信用分配困难和在线蒸馏知识迁移受限的问题，在模型后训练中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法存在局限性：强化学习依赖粗粒度结果监督，导致信用分配困难且难以获取新知识；在线蒸馏（OPD）无条件匹配教师logits，面临知识迁移困境——相似教师提供有限新知识，差异大的教师提供无效指导，限制了跨家族蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出蒸馏强化学习（Distilled RL）框架，将教师监督融入强化学习目标以提供细粒度指导，选择性迁移新知识，避免无条件模仿。包含三个核心组件：反向重要性采样与裁剪、负样本重置、序列级几何归一化。

Result: 通过简洁可解释的案例研究证明Distilled RL能有效从教师模型迁移先前不可用的新知识到学生模型。大规模实验表明，在家族内蒸馏和跨家族蒸馏两种设置下，Distilled RL在pass@1和pass@k指标上均显著优于标准强化学习和OPD方法。

Conclusion: Distilled RL成功整合了教师监督与强化学习优势，实现了细粒度指导和选择性知识迁移，突破了传统蒸馏方法的限制，为LLM后训练提供了一种更有效的知识迁移范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training is essential for improving reasoning, adaptation, and alignment. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, RL relies on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in difficult credit assignment and limited capability to acquire new knowledge. OPD, meanwhile, unconditionally matches teacher logits through KL divergence, which creates a dilemma: similar teachers provide little new knowledge, while substantially different teachers often yield ineffective guidance, largely restricting OPD to within-family distillation. We propose Distilled Reinforcement Learning (Distilled RL), which integrates teacher supervision into the RL objective to provide fine-grained guidance, selectively transfer new knowledge and avoid unconditional imitation. Distilled RL contains three components: reverse importance sampling with clipping, negative sample reset, and sequence-level geometric normalization. Through a concise and interpretable case study, we demonstrate that Distilled RL can effectively transfer previously unavailable knowledge from a teacher model to a student model. Extensive experiments across both within-family and cross-family distillation settings show that Distilled RL substantially outperforms standard RL and OPD in terms of both pass@1 and pass@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL.

</details>


### [152] [Node4All: Learning Node Representation Beyond Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17272)
*Dooho Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Node4All，一个无需针对特定数据集进行训练和调参的通用节点表示学习框架，在25个基准数据集上取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的节点表示学习方法大多依赖于针对特定数据集的训练和超参数调优，缺乏跨数据集的泛化能力。设计能够跨不同图数据集泛化的可复用图模型具有挑战性。

Method: 提出Node4All框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 通道图变换器(CGT)，使单一固定参数化能够处理任意图数据集；(2) 基于合成图序列的自监督学习方法，实现跨数据集泛化。

Result: 在25个基准数据集的节点分类任务上，与21个基线方法对比，Node4All无需针对特定数据集优化，统一应用于所有数据集即可达到排名第5的竞争力表现；在单样本学习和上下文学习设置下超越了最近的图基础模型。

Conclusion: Node4All实现了跨任意图数据集的可复用性，同时在实践中保持有效性，证明了通用节点表示学习的可行性，为图基础模型研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Node representation learning has advanced rapidly, yet most existing methods rely on per-dataset training and hyperparameter tuning. This dataset-specific optimization comes from the difficulty of designing reusable graph models that generalize across diverse graph datasets. In this work, we introduce Node4All, a node representation learner applicable to arbitrary graph datasets without any dataset-specific optimization.
  Node4All is built on two complementary ideas. At the architectural level, we introduce the Channel Graph Transformer (CGT), which enables a single fixed parameterization to process arbitrary graph datasets. At the learning level, we propose a self-supervised learning based on a series of synthetic graphs. Together, these components enable generalization beyond individual datasets, which is infeasible with existing architectures and learning frameworks. We extensively evaluate Node4All on node classification across 25 benchmarks against 21 baselines, covering both supervised and self-supervised methods. Despite all baselines being trained and optimized for each dataset, a single Node4All, applied uniformly across the datasets, achieves a competitive ranking of 5th among 21 baselines. Moreover, Node4All supports one-shot and in-context learning with an appropriate predictor and outperforms recent graph foundation models (GFMs) in these settings. These results demonstrate that Node4All not only achieves reusability across arbitrary graph datasets, but also remains an effective solution in practice. Code and model checkpoints are available in https://github.com/dooho00/node4all.

</details>


### [153] [AIGB-R1: Self-Evolving Generative Auto-Bidding via Hierarchical Planner-Executor Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17281)
*Yuejia Dou,Hesong Wang,Xinyu Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhilin Zhang,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIGB-R1框架，利用大语言模型的推理能力增强AI生成竞价，通过分层架构和自演化机制解决了数值精度有限、幻觉和推理延迟等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI生成竞价(AIGB)方法受限于离线数据集的模式覆盖有限和对任务状态理解不足，难以有效探索最优策略。虽然大语言模型(LLMs)具有先验世界知识和推理能力，但直接应用于自动竞价任务面临数值精度有限、幻觉和推理延迟等固有挑战。

Method: 提出AIGB-R1分层自演化自动竞价框架，包含高层规划器模块(宏观策略规划)和低层执行器模块(细粒度决策)；设计经验驱动的自演化循环实现自主策略探索优化；采用离线预训练和后训练对齐的两阶段流程，构建交互式竞价仿真环境；提出解耦群组相对策略优化(D-GRPO)通过优势解耦实现端到端优化。

Result: 在大规模公开数据集上的实验结果表明AIGB-R1框架的有效性，能够有效增强AI生成竞价性能。

Conclusion: AIGB-R1成功地将大语言模型的推理能力融入自动竞价系统，通过分层架构和自演化机制克服了直接应用LLMs的技术障碍，为在线广告竞价优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Auto-bidding plays an essential role in online advertising, automatically adjusting bids for advertisers to optimize their commercial goals. The emerging AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) paradigm widely adopts generative modeling to optimize bidding strategies, yet suffers from the limited mode coverage of offline datasets and inadequate task-state understanding, hindering effective exploration of optimal strategies. Large Language Models (LLMs), with prior world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, offer a promising approach to overcome these limitations. However, directly applying LLMs to auto-bidding tasks faces inherent challenges in limited numerical precision, hallucinations, and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose AIGB-R1, a hierarchical self-evolving auto-bidding framework aiming to enhance AI-Generated Bidding via LLMs' Reasoning capabilities, comprising a high-level Planner module for macro-level strategy planning and a low-level Executor module for fine-grained decision-making. Building upon this, we design an experience-driven self-evolving loop, enabling autonomous strategy exploration and optimization from accumulated experience. We adopt a two-stage pipeline of offline pre-training and post-training alignment, and build an interactive bidding simulation environment for strategy rollout. Furthermore, we propose Decoupled Group Relative Policy Optimization (D-GRPO) to achieve end-to-end optimization via advantage decoupling. Experimental results on a large-scale public dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of AIGB-R1.

</details>


### [154] [An Iterative Geometric Approach to Optimizing Separating Hyperplanes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17282)
*Akos Hajnal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何方法的硬间隔SVM求解算法，利用初始分离超平面作为起点，通过局部优化逐步提升间隔直至收敛到全局最优，在部分大规模数据集上展现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于线性可分数据集，硬间隔SVM需要求解最大间隔分离超平面。传统方法直接求解凸二次优化问题，当初始分离超平面已知时，研究者探索是否可以利用这一信息来更高效地达到唯一最优解。

Method: 提出一种几何迭代方法：从初始分离超平面出发，在每次迭代中仅考虑当前活跃集这一局部信息，求解该子集的最优分离超平面来重新对齐超平面。通过一系列小型子问题替代原始凸二次优化问题，逐步改进超平面对齐同时保持分离性并持续增大间隔。

Result: 初步实验评估表明，当初始分离超平面可用时，该方法在大规模数据集上具有竞争力，某些情况下甚至优于直接求解优化问题的最先进方法。理论分析证明了算法的正确性和收敛性。

Conclusion: 利用初始分离超平面可以有效加速硬间隔SVM的求解，所提出的局部优化方法在保持分离的前提下逐步收敛到全局最优，为大规模数据集上的SVM训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Given a binary-labeled linearly separable dataset, and the objective is to compute the maximum-margin separating hyperplane, also known as the hard-margin Support Vector Machine (SVM) classifier. This paper investigates whether, if given an initial separating hyperplane, can it be exploited to reach this unique optimum more efficiently. We present a geometric approach that gradually improves the alignment of the hyperplane, starting from an initial separating hyperplane, while preserving separation and continuously increasing its margin until convergence to the global optimum. At each iteration, the method considers only local information, namely the current active set, and aims to re-align the hyperplane according to the optimal separating hyperplane of this reduced subset. Consequently, the original convex quadratic optimization problem is addressed through a sequence of smaller subproblems. The paper presents the algorithm in detail, together with a preliminary experimental evaluation and several theoretical findings. The results suggest that, when an initial separating hyperplane is available, the proposed method can be competitive on larger datasets and, in some cases, can outperform state-of-the-art approaches that solve the optimization problem directly.

</details>


### [155] [Lookahead Branching for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17290)
*Liam Davis,Duo Zhou,Huan Zhang,Guy Katz,Clark Barrett,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将前瞻分支策略集成到神经网络验证的分支定界方法中，显著提升验证速度并增加高达57%的可解实例数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证在确保AI系统安全性方面至关重要，但现有方法在处理复杂网络时效率受限。分支定界方法中的分支决策质量对验证效率有显著影响。前瞻策略在组合优化中已证明有效，但在神经网络验证中的应用尚待探索，因此有必要系统研究其在神经网络验证中的潜力与实现方式。

Method: 作者提出一种通用框架，将前瞻策略集成到任意基于分支定界的神经网络验证器中。具体方法包括：首先建立前瞻分支的通用算法模板；其次揭示现有最优分支启发式方法FSB可视为前瞻策略的特例；进一步利用前瞻过程生成额外引理以加速验证；最后在Marabou和$α$-$β$-CROWN两个代表性验证器上实现该策略，并开源代码。

Result: 实验结果表明，前瞻策略带来验证时间的持续加速，并使可解实例数增加高达57%。在两个代表性验证器（Marabou和$α$-$β$-CROWN）上的实验均验证了方法的有效性。此外，前瞻过程生成的额外引理进一步提升了验证效率。

Conclusion: 前瞻分支策略为神经网络验证提供了一种通用且有效的加速方法，不仅可以优化分支决策质量，还能产生有益的辅助引理。该方法易于集成到现有验证器中，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the effect of lookahead branching strategies in neural network verification. We present a general recipe to integrate lookahead into any branch-and-bound verifier and demonstrate how one of the current state-of-the-art branching heuristics, FSB, can be viewed as a special instantiation of the lookahead branching strategy. We also describe how, in addition to improving the quality of branching decisions, lookahead can generate additional lemmas that accelerate verification. We instantiate the method in two representative branch-and-bound-based verifiers (Marabou and $α$-$β$-CROWN), and demonstrate that lookahead leads to consistent speedups in verification time and up to $57\%$ more solved instances. Code is available at https://github.com/ai-ar-research/lookahead-branching.

</details>


### [156] [DRNOISE: Benchmarking Deep Research Agents in Misleading Evidence Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17291)
*Jun Nie,Zhiqin Yang,Zhenheng Tang,Yonggang Zhang,Xiaowen Chu,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRNOISE基准测试，用于评估深度研究代理在误导性证据下的表现。研究发现，即使是表现优秀的代理，在面对单一虚假文档干扰时，准确率也会下降66-88个百分点。主要失效模式是“验证惯性”，即代理往往在完成证据链验证前就接受直接给出答案的文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法无法深入了解深度研究代理在面对看似普通但实际上是虚假文档时是否能保持良好的证据标准。在开放网络环境中，相关记录往往与冗余摘要、过时报告和误导性文档共存，而现有评估缺乏对代理在此类环境下的可靠性评估。

Method: 引入DRNOISE基准测试，包含100个任务，涵盖十类证据操作。每个任务有一个唯一的标准答案，由两条相互印证的间接记录链支持；配对的噪声条件添加一个看似合理的文档，直接陈述一个冲突的答案。通过对不同代理在清洁任务和噪声条件下的表现进行对比分析。

Result: 单一干扰文档导致原本在清洁任务上表现良好的代理准确率下降66-88个百分点。通过轨迹分析识别出主要失效模式为“验证惯性”：代理经常检索到真实记录，但在完成和协调证据链之前就停止验证，转而接受直接给出答案形式的文档。通用的验证提示可以减少但无法完全消除这一差距。

Conclusion: 深度研究代理在开放网络部署中面临普通页面中看似合理的虚假信息的挑战。可靠的研究不仅需要检索和引用，更需要主动地将直接主张与记录级证据进行协调验证。当前代理存在显著的验证惯性问题，需要改进验证机制以提升可靠性。

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly operate over the open web, where relevant records coexist with redundant summaries, outdated reports, and misleading documents. Existing evaluations offer limited insight into whether agents preserve sound evidential standards when an ordinary-looking false document is deliberately seeded into a searchable environment and offers a direct shortcut to a conflicting answer. We introduce DRNOISE, a 100-task benchmark for answer recovery under misleading evidence. Each task has a unique gold answer supported by two corroborating indirect record chains; the paired noisy condition adds one plausible document that states a conflicting answer directly. The benchmark spans ten families of evidence operations. Across agents with strong clean-task performance, this single intervention causes 66-88 percentage-point accuracy drops. Trace analyses identify verification inertia as the dominant failure mode: agents often retrieve truthful records but stop before completing and reconciling the evidence chain, instead deferring to the answer-like document. Generic verification prompts reduce but do not close this gap. The setting is especially relevant to open-web deployment, where plausible falsehoods arrive through ordinary-looking pages rather than explicit attacks. Reliable deep research therefore requires more than retrieval and citation; it requires active reconciliation of direct claims with record-level evidence.

</details>


### [157] [WAR: Workload-Aware Rollouts for Synchronous Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17299)
*Ryan Xu,Atlas Zhao,David Bao,Frank Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WAR系统，通过联合优化解码和调度来加速同步智能体强化学习，在低负载时使用无模型推测解码，高负载时采用缓存感知调度，实现1.4x-1.6x的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野轨迹生成已成为智能体强化学习的主要系统瓶颈。由于智能体与环境进行多轮交互，轨迹迅速增长到数万个token，使得同步RL训练越来越受限于轨迹生成速度。

Method: WAR基于关键观察：最优的轨迹优化策略取决于运行时负载。(1)低负载时，启用无模型推测解码(SuffixDecoding)，复用已完成轨迹的后缀模式作为推测草稿，无需额外草稿模型且避免GPU争用；(2)高负载时，将优化重点转向缓存感知调度，基于缓存局部性、轨迹进度和服务器负载将请求分配到轨迹副本，减少冗余KV缓存重计算。

Result: WAR在低负载下将长上下文智能体轨迹吞吐量提升1.4倍，高负载下最高提升1.6倍。在不改变底层RL算法的情况下，在不同负载场景下实现稳定的吞吐量改进。

Conclusion: WAR消除了同步智能体RL中的主要轨迹生成瓶颈，为可扩展的长上下文智能体训练提供了实用路径。通过结合解码级后缀复用和系统级轨迹调度，有效解决了长视野轨迹生成的系统瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon rollout generation has become the dominant systems bottleneck in agentic reinforcement learning (RL). As agents interact with environments over many turns, trajectories rapidly grow to tens of thousands of tokens, making synchronous RL training increasingly constrained by rollout. We propose WAR, a workload-aware rollout system that substantially accelerates synchronous agentic RL by jointly optimizing decoding and scheduling. WAR is built on a key observation: the optimal rollout optimization strategy depends on runtime load: (1) Under low load, WAR enables model-free speculative decoding with SuffixDecoding, which reuses suffix patterns from previously completed trajectories as speculative drafts for future rollouts. Unlike model-based drafters, SuffixDecoding introduces no additional draft model and avoids GPU contention with rollout generation. (2) Under high load, where saturated batched decoding leaves limited room for speculative speedup, WAR shifts the optimization focus to cache-aware scheduling. A global scheduler places requests across rollout replicas based on cache locality, trajectory progress and server load, reducing redundant KV-cache recomputation and mitigating load imbalance. By combining decoding-level suffix reuse with system-level rollout scheduling, WAR delivers robust throughput improvements across workload regimes without changing the underlying RL algorithm. WAR improves long-context agentic rollout throughput by 1.4x under low load and up to 1.6x under high load. These results show that WAR removes a major rollout bottleneck in synchronous agentic RL and provides a practical path toward scalable long-context agent training.

</details>


### [158] [Rationalizing Boltzmann Rationality: An Axiomatic Characterization of Entropy-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17316)
*Silviu Pitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，通过区分“机会”与“选择”两类随机性并施加IIA与单调性约束，唯一性地推导出Boltzmann策略、熵正则化表示及软贝尔曼方程，从而为softmax策略提供规范性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中softmax策略虽被广泛采用，但现有文献缺乏从第一性原理对其唯一性的推导；软贝尔曼方程中的熵奖励违反了支撑MDP奖励结构的独立性公理，这一基本张力长期未解。

Method: 通过区分环境随机性（机会）与策略随机性（选择），将VNM独立性限制于环境彩票；在策略与价值函数的选择节点施加无关选择独立性（IIA）与单调性约束，从公理化角度推导。

Result: 唯一确定Boltzmann策略形式、熵正则化表示及软贝尔曼方程；证明软/硬贝尔曼方程的选择归结为设计决策——智能体是否重视自身的选择能力；获得回报单调性与广义折扣下收敛性等强化学习专用推论。

Conclusion: 通过融合经济学与信息理论两条独立线索，为softmax策略提供规范性基础；当IIA适合作为智能体设计准则时，熵正则化成为自然选择，否则应采用硬贝尔曼方程。

Abstract: The softmax policy $π(a \mid s) \propto \exp(βQ(s,a))$ is the default model of stochastic choice in reinforcement learning (RL). Various justifications based on robustness, exploration, and optimization have been offered in the RL literature, but none uniquely derives the softmax form from first principles. This leaves a basic tension unresolved: the entropy bonus in the soft Bellman equation violates the Independence axiom that underwrites the Markov decision process (MDP) reward structure. We dissolve this tension by distinguishing two kinds of randomness: chance and choice. By restricting von Neumann-Morgenstern (VNM) Independence to environmental lotteries over base prospects, we show that imposing independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA) and monotonicity on the policy and value functions at choice nodes uniquely determines the Boltzmann policy, the entropy-regularized representation, and the soft Bellman equation. The choice between the soft and hard Bellman equations thus reduces to a design decision: whether the agent values its own ability to choose. We develop RL-specific consequences, including return monotonicity and convergence under generalized discounting, and synthesize the independent lines from economics and information theory that arrive at the same structure, offering a normative assessment of when IIA is appropriate for agent design.

</details>


### [159] [TAPAS: Throughput-adaptive Perception for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17317)
*Aman Vyas,Vasista Kodumagulla,Zain Taufique,Pasi Liljeberg,Anil Kanduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPAS，一种面向移动/边缘平台的吞吐量自适应感知策略，通过场景复杂度感知和动态模型到集群映射，实现运行时智能资源分配，在保证吞吐量的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知策略假设固定FPS和静态模型到集群映射，导致在动态环境变化时出现吞吐量过度/不足供给或不必要能耗的问题。需要解决场景复杂度感知以估计合适FPS目标和动态模型到集群映射以最小能耗提供所需吞吐量的挑战。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）结合奖励推理模型（RRM）和GRU（门控循环单元）智能体，在异构移动/边缘平台上编排感知任务，实现基于变化FPS目标的运行时智能资源分配。

Result: 在Jetson Orin NX上的KITTI数据集测试序列中，TAPAS达到93-100%吞吐量满足率并节省76%能耗；在未见过的nuScenes数据集上，保持97%吞吐量满足率，能耗比SOTA方法降低64%。

Conclusion: TAPAS成功实现了吞吐量自适应的感知策略，能够在保证高吞吐量满足率的同时显著降低能耗，并在未见数据集上表现出强鲁棒性，证明了场景复杂度感知和动态模型到集群映射方法的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems rely on a perception module to navigate through dynamic environments. In real-world scenarios, the perception module's throughput requirements vary at runtime due to changes in scene complexity. However, existing perception strategies assume a fixed FPS and static model-to-cluster mapping, resulting in either over/under provision of throughput requirements or unnecessary energy consumption across diverse scenes. Addressing this challenge requires tightly coupled \textit{scene complexity awareness} to estimate an appropriate FPS target and \textit{dynamic model-to-cluster mapping} to deliver the required throughput at minimum energy. We propose a throughput-adaptive perception strategy for mobile/edge platforms, enabling intelligent runtime resource allocation based on varying FPS targets. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) with RRM (Reward Reasoning Model) and a GRU (Gated Recurrent Unit) agent to orchestrate perception tasks across heterogeneous mobile/edge platforms. We evaluate TAPAS on Jetson Orin NX across KITTI and unseen nuScenes. On the \textit{KITTI} dataset's test sequences, TAPAS achieves 93-100% throughput met rate while saving energy by 76%. On the unseen \textit{nuScenes} dataset, TAPAS maintains 97% throughput met rate with 64% lower energy compared to \textit{SOTA} approaches, proving its robustness.

</details>


### [160] [Rethinking the Suitability of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms Under Practical Transfer Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17326)
*Hany Hamed,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究指出在迁移强化学习中，仅比较样本效率是不够的，PPO等样本效率较低的算法可能因并行训练而具有更高的实际效率；同时，域随机化对同策略、异策略和基于模型等不同学习范式具有类似的鲁棒性提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习算法评估主要关注样本效率，但在迁移强化学习设置中，这一标准不足以指导实际应用。研究者希望探索两个新的评估维度：实际效率（实际计算时间而非交互次数）和动态失配下的鲁棒性（不同学习范式对域随机化的响应），以帮助实践者做出更好的算法选择。

Method: 在相同的迁移协议下，对代表三种不同强化学习范式的算法进行控制实验对比：同策略的PPO、异策略的SAC和基于模型与规划的TD-MPC2。研究从两个维度评估：(1) 比较实际训练时间与样本效率的差异；(2) 分析域随机化覆盖范围对不同算法学习鲁棒策略的影响。

Result: 发现样本效率较低的PPO算法通过大规模并行训练，能够比样本效率更高的SAC和TD-MPC2更快地产生有效策略，验证了大规模并行训练范式的实际优势。同时，域随机化对三种代表不同范式的算法产生了相似的影响，都能帮助学习到更鲁棒的策略。这是首次在相同迁移协议下对PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2受域随机化影响进行控制对比研究。

Conclusion: 在迁移强化学习中评估算法时，不仅应考虑样本效率，还需重视实际训练时间和算法产生可用策略的能力等实际因素。域随机化作为一种技术手段，对不同学习范式的算法具有普适的鲁棒性提升效果。这一发现强调了多维评估框架对强化学习实践的重要性。

Abstract: Transfer-oriented reinforcement learning requires evaluating algorithms along dimensions that go beyond standard sample efficiency. We focus on two dimensions: practical efficiency, which asks whether conclusions about algorithm suitability change under wall-clock rather than interaction-based budgets, and robustness under dynamics mismatch, which asks how different learning paradigms respond to variability in the training distribution induced by domain randomization. We provide two insights to reinforcement-learning practitioners. First, comparing the sample efficiency of different algorithms is often an insufficient criterion in transfer-oriented settings. The wall-clock time required to train a decent policy is an important consideration for practitioners, and we find that the sample-inefficient PPO algorithm can produce a performant policy faster than relatively more sample-efficient algorithms such as SAC and TD-MPC2, validating the common understanding of massively parallel training paradigms. Second, domain randomization can help different kinds of algorithms learn robust policies. In particular, although PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 represent different RL paradigms - on-policy, off-policy, and model-based learning and planning, respectively - we find that domain randomization affects all three algorithms in a similar way. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first controlled comparison of the effect of domain-randomization coverage on PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 under the same transfer protocol. Taken together, these two insights highlight the importance of evaluating RL algorithms not only by sample efficiency, but also by practical considerations such as training time and the algorithms' ability to produce usable policies.

</details>


### [161] [When Drift Detectors cry Wolf: False Alarm Rates in continuous ML Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17336)
*Raj Shekhar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD和对抗验证五种漂移检测器在持续监控下的假阳性行为。PSI在小样本下假警频繁但样本量超过200后趋于稳定；KS/MMD/LSDD在低数据场景更可靠；Bonferroni校正可降低假阳性但牺牲检测敏感性，揭示了稳定性-敏感性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移检测研究多关注合成偏移下的检测准确性，而忽视了持续监控场景下的假警问题。在生产环境中，模型需要跨时间和多特征进行持续监控，即使是微小的假阳性率也会累积成频繁告警，导致告警疲劳，因此亟需研究检测器的假阳性行为。

Method: 对五种常用漂移检测器（PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD、对抗验证）进行实证分析，系统评估它们在持续监控条件下的假阳性行为。通过改变批量大小观察各检测器的表现差异，并应用Bonferroni校正来评估其对假阳性率和真阳性敏感性的影响。

Result: PSI对小批量大小高度敏感，小样本下假警频繁，但批量超过约200样本后行为显著稳定和改善。KS、MMD、LSDD在各批量大小下保持相对波动，但在低数据场景比PSI更可靠。Bonferroni校正虽降低假阳性率，但常以降低真阳性敏感性为代价，强化了漂移检测中稳定性-敏感性权衡的已知特性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了持续监控条件下多种漂移检测器假阳性行为的系统比较，识别了各检测器家族的权衡特性，为生产ML系统中漂移检测器的选择和校准提供了实用指南，强调了在部署时需根据具体数据量需求平衡稳定性与敏感性。

Abstract: Drift detection is a core component of production machine learning monitoring systems, where detectors are used to compare incoming data with a reference distribution and trigger alerts when changes occur. However, these detectors are often evaluated in research settings that emphasize detection accuracy under synthetic shifts, while overlooking false alarms under continuous monitoring. In production environments, models are monitored repeatedly over time and across many features, and even small false positive rates can accumulate into frequent alerts, leading to alarm fatigue. We empirically analyze false positive behavior across five commonly used drift detectors: PSI, KS, MMD, LSDD, and adversarial validation. Consistent with existing literature, PSI exhibits strong sensitivity to batch size, producing frequent false alarms at small sample sizes; however, we further observe that its behavior stabilizes and improves substantially once batch sizes exceed approximately 200 samples. In contrast, KS, MMD, and LSDD display persistent fluctuations across batch sizes, while remaining comparatively more reliable than PSI in low-data regimes. Applying a Bonferroni correction reduces false positive rates, but often at the cost of reduced true positive sensitivity, reinforcing the well-known stability - sensitivity trade-off in drift detection. This work provides a systematic comparison of false positive behavior across multiple drift detectors under continuous monitoring conditions. We identify tradeoffs across detector families and provide practical guidelines for selecting and calibrating drift detectors in production ML systems.

</details>


### [162] [A multiverse-consensus pipeline for reproducible feature selection in untargeted LC-MS metabolomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17345)
*Mohammed Saeed Al-Huraibi,Ihsan Yozgat,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多宇宙分析（multiverse analysis）引入非靶向LC-MS代谢组学的特征选择，通过对比多种预处理流程，揭示了单一流程结果的不稳定性，并提出了基于共识的特征筛选方法以提高结果稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非靶向LC-MS代谢组学中，预处理步骤存在众多可行的选择，但研究者通常只采用单一流程并报告所得特征清单。这种做法使得结果对未尝试过的选择的依赖程度变得不可见，隐藏了分析中的自由度。为了揭示并量化这种不确定性，需要一种能够系统地探索不同预处理选择对结果影响的方法。

Method: 作者提出了一种可审计、配置驱动的分析流程：(i) 应用十阶段质量控制过滤级联，记录每个特征的筛选命运；(ii) 以多宇宙分析框架运行下游分析，涵盖四种对比性的预处理哲学与四种特征排序方法的组合，并采用Bootstrap稳定性选择和标签置换检验进行评估。只有在多条路径中重现的特征才能进入分层共识集。通过Jaccard指数评估不同管道间的一致性，并使用管道级标签置换测试检验假发现。

Result: 在五个乳腺癌细胞系的演示数据集（检测到30,370个特征）上，四个单一管道各自返回4-20个特征，成对一致性低至Jaccard=0.05。多宇宙共识保留了15个特征（在≥2/4路径中重现），其中仅一个特征在所有四种路径中重现。两个共享归一化和漂移校正方法的路径主导了共识集。管道级标签置换测试在50次零置换中未发现假发现。

Conclusion: 通过仅报告预处理稳健的特征，并提供完整的保留/剔除审计轨迹，可以将隐藏的分析自由度转化为明确、可检视的输出。这提高了代谢组学特征选择的透明度和可信度。作者也指出了方法的局限性，包括单批次设计以及对独立验证的需求。

Abstract: Background: Untargeted LC-MS metabolomics requires a long chain of preprocessing decisions, each with several equally defensible options. Analysts typically commit to one pipeline and report the resulting feature shortlist. How strongly that shortlist depends on choices that were never varied stays invisible. Results: We adapt multiverse analysis to untargeted metabolomics feature selection. We present an auditable, configuration-driven pipeline that (i) applies a ten-stage quality-control filter cascade in which every feature's fate is logged, and (ii) runs the downstream analysis as a multiverse over four contrasting preprocessing philosophies, each combined with four feature-ranking methods under bootstrap stability selection and label-permutation testing. Only features recurring across paths enter a tiered consensus. On a demonstration dataset of five breast-cancer cell lines (30,370 detected features), the four single pipelines individually returned shortlists of 4-20 features whose pairwise agreement was as low as Jaccard = 0.05. The multiverse consensus retained 15 features (>=2/4 paths), of which one recurred across all four, although two paths (sharing normalization and drift-correction methods) dominate the consensus. A pipeline-wide label-permutation test found no false discoveries in 50 null permutations. Conclusions: Reporting only preprocessing-robust features, with a complete kept/dropped audit trail, converts hidden analytical degrees of freedom into an explicit, inspectable output. We discuss scope and limitations, including single-batch design and the need for independent validation.

</details>


### [163] [Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17377)
*Jiawen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的参数高效微调方法——ChebyMA，该方法通过在可学习坐标上评估的Chebyshev多项式基的多曲面叠加来调整权重矩阵，在多个CV和NLP任务上展现出优异的参数-准确率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法在表达能力和理论保证方面存在局限，传统线性投影难以有效捕捉高维复杂特征的几何结构；需要一种既有扎实理论基础又具有高度表达能力的参数高效适配方法，以实现更好的参数-性能权衡。

Method: 用Chebyshev多项式基在可学习坐标上评估并通过可训练系数矩阵组合的多曲面叠加来替代标准线性投影；从函数逼近理论角度建立逼近表达性定理，证明单曲面ChebyMA在Frobenius范数下重构误差的收敛性；借助Kolmogorov n-width理论分析多曲面叠加（S>1）在解耦高维复杂特征方面的表达优势。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100（计算机视觉）和AG News/SST-2（自然语言处理）数据集上，ChebyMA相比全参数微调、LoRA、TLoRA和StelLA等方法持续获得更优的参数-准确率帕累托前沿，在所有测试数据集上显著超越其他对比方法。

Conclusion: ChebyMA通过将权重矩阵适配转化为连续函数逼近问题，实现了理论保证与实证效果的统一；其纯向量化计算特性确保了计算效率，多曲面叠加机制有效提升了模型对复杂特征的表达能力，为参数高效微调提供了新的理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: The paper presents a new parameter-efficient adaptation method called ChebyMA (Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation). ChebyMA adopts weight matrices through a multi-surface superposition of Chebyshev polynomial bases evaluated on learnable coordinates and combined via trainable coefficient matrices, replacing standard linear projections with highly expressive continuous function approximation. Theoretically, we establish an Approximation Expressivity Theorem, proving from the perspective of function approximation theory that single-manifold ChebyMA guarantees convergence in Frobenius norm error of reconstruction. Besides, drawing on Kolmogorov $n$-width intuition, we demonstrate the expressive advantages of multi-manifold superposition ($S > 1$) in decoupling high-dimensional complex features. Experimental results on Computer Vision CIFAR datasets(CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100)\cite{CIFAR} and Natural Language Processing (AG News, SST-2) datasets demonstrate that ChebyMA consistently achieves a superior parameter-accuracy Pareto front compared to standard full-parameter fine-tuning, LoRA\cite{LoRA}, TLoRA\cite{TLoRA}, and StelLA\cite{StelLA}. ChebyMA significantly outperforms other tested methods in tested datasets, validating its solid theoretical foundation for generality with purely vectorized computations.

</details>


### [164] [CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17398)
*Ruogu Chen,Weihua Xiao,Ramesh Karri,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoEvoP&R，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，用于自动演化解析式布局目标函数，解决了现有布局阶段代理指标与下游布线和时序质量不匹配的问题，显著提升了布线后线长、拥塞和时序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解析式布局器依赖可微分目标函数（如HPWL和单元密度惩罚）来引导布局，但这些布局阶段的代理指标与下游布线及时序质量存在偏差。先前的工作通过人工设计项或学习的黑盒代理来减少这种差距，但前者需要专家反复调参，后者难以解释、调试和部署。因此需要一种自动化、可解释的方法来优化布局目标函数。

Method: 提出了CoEvoP&R框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）自动演化解析式布局目标函数。每一代中，提示词结合受限的目标接口、基线上下文和历史候选，以及来自布局、时序代理和布线工具的布线相关反馈。LLM生成可读的可微分目标函数，嵌入并验证于DREAMPlace中，通过时序代理和实际布线器评估，并存储反馈以指导后续迭代。

Result: 在8个ChiP-Bench Nangate45设计和3个随机种子上，CoEvoP&R相比原生DREAMPlace将布线后线长和拥塞分别降低16.9%和36.7%，最差负松弛改善0.70 ns，总负松弛幅度减少912 ns。在8个ICCAD 2015 Superblue设计上，布线后线长和拥塞分别降低5.4%和23.2%。

Conclusion: CoEvoP&R成功利用LLM自动演化可解释的解析式布局目标函数，有效弥合了布局阶段代理指标与下游布线及时序质量的差距，在多个基准测试上取得了显著的性能提升，为解析式布局提供了新的自动化优化范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Analytical placers rely on differentiable objective functions to guide placement, typically combining intermediate surrogate metrics such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) and cell-density penalties. However, these placement-stage surrogates remain misaligned with downstream routed and timing quality. Prior work reduces this gap with human-designed terms or learned black-box surrogates, but the former requires expert retuning and the latter is difficult to explain, debug, or deploy in analytical placement flows. CoEvoP&R addresses these limitations with a large language model (LLM)-based framework that automatically evolves analytical placement objectives. At each generation, the prompt combines the restricted objective interface, baseline context, and archived prior candidates with routing-related feedback from placement, timing proxy, and routing tools. The LLM proposes readable differentiable objectives, which are embedded and validated in DREAMPlace, evaluated through a timing proxy and an actual router, and stored with their feedback to guide later generations. Across eight ChiP-Bench Nangate45 designs and three seeds, CoEvoP&R reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 16.9% and 36.7%, with gains of 0.70 ns in worst negative slack and a 912 ns reduction in total negative slack magnitude over native DREAMPlace. Across eight ICCAD 2015 Superblue designs, it reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 5.4% and 23.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git.

</details>


### [165] [CORAL: Learning Amyloid Fibril Ligand Docking with Cooperative Binding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17412)
*Yasheng Sun,Bohan Li,Youqi Tao,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CORAL，一种基于强化学习的生成式对接框架，专门针对淀粉样纤维蛋白独特的交叉β凹槽几何结构进行配体姿态预测，通过引入配体间协同堆叠能量显著提升了对接性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经退行性疾病（如阿尔茨海默症和帕金森症）的一个特征是蛋白质异常聚集形成淀粉样纤维，能够选择性结合这些纤维的小分子在诊断、成像探针和治疗方面具有重要价值。然而预测配体与纤维靶点的结合面临两大根本挑战：(1)淀粉样蛋白-配体复合物的共晶结构极其稀缺，即使有冷冻电镜的最新进展，也仅有少数被结构表征，使得监督训练对接模型对该靶点类别不切实际；(2)淀粉样纤维呈现出与球状蛋白质根本不同的结合模式——配体插入纵向交叉β凹槽并沿纤维轴协同堆叠，这是现有对接模型所未设计的几何结构。

Method: 提出了CORAL（COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking，协同淀粉样配体对接），这是一个强化学习框架，训练生成式对接模型产生专门针对交叉β凹槽几何结构的配体姿态分布。该方法的奖励函数显式地将协同配体-配体堆叠能量与蛋白质-配体对接亲和力结合，直接捕捉淀粉样纤维独特的结合几何结构。此外，研究者还构建了一个精心策划的淀粉样蛋白-配体复合物评估集，由领域专家验证的模型生成姿态组成。

Result: 在实验解析的结构和策划的评估集上的实验表明，CORAL在姿态质量和结合亲和力相关性方面均优于现有的对接基线方法，验证了该方法处理淀粉样纤维独特结合模式的有效性。

Conclusion: CORAL成功解决了淀粉样纤维配体对接的关键挑战，通过强化学习框架和协同堆叠能量的引入，为淀粉样纤维相关疾病的诊断和治疗药物开发提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: A hallmark of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's and Parkinson's is the aberrant aggregation of proteins into amyloid fibrils, and small molecules that selectively bind to these fibrils hold promise as diagnostics, imaging probes, and therapeutics. Predicting how such ligands bind to fibril targets, however, presents two fundamental challenges. First, resolved co-crystal structures of amyloid-ligand complexes are exceptionally scarce; even with recent advances in cryo-EM only a handful have been structurally characterized, making supervised training of docking models impractical for this target class. Second, amyloid fibrils present a binding mode fundamentally different from globular proteins: ligands intercalate into longitudinal cross-$β$ grooves and stack cooperatively along the fibril axis, a geometry that existing docking models are not designed to capture. To address these challenges, we present CORAL (COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking), a reinforcement learning framework that trains a generative docking model to produce ligand pose distributions tailored to the cross-$β$ groove geometry. Our reward explicitly incorporates cooperative ligand-ligand stacking energy alongside protein-ligand docking affinity, directly capturing the distinctive binding geometry of amyloid fibrils. We further introduce a curated evaluation set of amyloid-ligand complexes constructed from model-generated poses validated by domain experts. Experiments on both experimentally resolved structures and this evaluation set demonstrate improved pose quality and binding affinity correlation over existing docking baselines.

</details>


### [166] [Grounded verification of chemical and materials reasoning: detection is the bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层验证器，通过检测可验证的声明、对照权威数据库检查，并在门控修正循环中修复错误，显著降低了大语言模型在化学领域的虚构错误，同时大幅减少了检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在化学领域（分子式、空间群、形成能等）存在虚构现象，尤其在长尾实体上表现明显，且模型的置信度不可靠。传统的全面检索方法成本高昂，因此需要一种更高效的验证和修复机制。

Method: 提出分层验证器：提取可检查的声明，对照权威数据库和物理定律进行检查，并通过门控修正循环进行修复。该方法专注于确定性、数据库 grounded 的验证，避免了全面检索的覆盖成本。

Result: 在4个模型和528个条件约束提示上，门控修正将分子式错误从22%降至4%，检索次数比全面增强减少3.2倍，超越对话式oracle。修复成功率在触发标记时达80-97%。当验证器范围覆盖交付物时，最终答案准确率从83%提升至90%。在长尾错误区域效果显著（如同位素半衰期从11%降至0%）。

Conclusion: 验证的关键瓶颈在于检测而非修复。数据库 grounded 的方法仅在验证器范围能够触及交付物时才能改善最终答案，且提升主要出现在存在可提取长尾错误的场景，对接近完美的物理常数等场景无显著改善。

Abstract: Large language models confabulate chemical objects (molecular formulas, space groups, formation energies) in fluent reasoning traces, concentrated on long-tail entities where confidence is least trustworthy. Deterministic, database-grounded verification can catch and repair such errors without the coverage cost of blanket retrieval; the binding constraint, we find, is detection, not repair. Our tiered verifier extracts each checkable claim, checks it against authoritative databases and physics, and feeds the reference into a gated correction loop. Across four models and 528 condition-pinned prompts, gated correction cuts committed-formula error from 22% to 4% at $3.2\times$ fewer retrievals than blanket augmentation, beating a conversational oracle. Repair succeeds wherever a flag fires (80--97%); the bottleneck is in-loop detection recall. Grounding improves the final answer only when the verifier's scope reaches the deliverable (83% to 90%), and the lift appears only where extractable long-tail error exists: absent on near-ceiling physical constants, large on isotope half-lives (11% to 0%).

</details>


### [167] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了KATA框架，通过球面打包原理解决线性注意力在关联召回任务上的性能退化问题，实现了常数时间推理并在长序列处理上显著超越FlashAttention-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力虽然能够实现常数时间的循环推理，但在关联召回任务上性能严重退化。现有方法无法有效处理需要精确检索和召回信息的任务，限制了线性注意力在实际应用中的表现。

Method: 1. 将注意力召回问题形式化为球面打包问题
2. 通过自对偶齐次锥认证非负注意力权重，从第一性原理导出特征映射
3. 采用秩一半正定（PSD）特征实现容量-干扰平衡
4. 引入参数无关的凸输出门
5. 实现两种操作模式：类FlashAttention的前向传播和精确O(T)分块状态形式
6. 使用融合Triton核函数实现

Result: 1. 类FlashAttention前向传播达到约1.6倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量
2. 精确O(T)分块状态形式在131k标记处达到约11倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量
3. 在MQAR和重复键覆盖任务上超越Gated DeltaNet
4. 在16倍分布外长度上保持0.985的MQAR准确率
5. 仅需约四分之一的KV缓存条目即可接近softmax性能

Conclusion: KATA成功解决了线性注意力在关联召回任务上的关键缺陷，通过严谨的数学框架实现了高效的长序列建模。实验表明该方法在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了召回能力，为线性注意力的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。研究还揭示了位置嵌入、delta规则和衰减门与特征几何之间的交互关系。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [168] [Decoder-Preserving Sparse Autoencoders: Which Readouts Survive Sparse Compression?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17425)
*Aniket Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解码器保留稀疏自编码器(DPSAE)，通过引入矩阵值失真度量来解决SAE重建误差和稀疏性相同但保留信号不同的问题，在GPT-2上降低了10.6-11.4%的失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)将模型激活压缩为稀疏编码时，相同的重建误差和稀疏性可能保留不同的线性可解码信号，这种模糊性尚未被正式定义和解决。

Method: 将模糊性形式化为最优岭预测算子之间的矩阵值失真，通过将该失真与重建损失结合来训练解码器保留SAE(DPSAE)，并引入任务先验来控制模式保留。

Result: 在GPT-2 small第8层上，DPSAE在三次配对运行中将保持失真降低10.6-11.4%同时匹配重建NMSE；检查点通过自然文本输出-KL非劣效性测试，但在Pythia配对中对少数稀疏特征的探测无改善。

Conclusion: 重建质量并不决定哪些重拟合的线性读出能在稀疏压缩中存活，读出保留与学习更清晰的基准概念或保留每个冻结模型行为是不同的，这些发现对SAE应用有重要启示。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) compress model activations into sparse codes, but equal reconstruction error and sparsity can preserve different linearly decodable signals. We formalize this ambiguity as a matrix-valued distortion between optimal ridge-prediction operators and train decoder-preserving SAEs by combining this distortion with reconstruction loss. In a rank relaxation, an isotropic task prior saturates per-mode omission costs without changing PCA's ordering, whereas a structured prior can change which modes are retained. A controlled sparse experiment shows that a declared prior protects held-out combinations from its task subspace. On GPT-2 small block 8, DPSAE reduces held-out decoder distortion by 10.6--11.4% across three paired runs while matching reconstruction NMSE. The same checkpoints pass an average natural-text output-KL noninferiority test, but one matched Pythia pair shows no improvement in probes restricted to a few sparse features. These results show that reconstruction quality does not determine which refitted linear readouts survive sparse compression, and that readout preservation is distinct from learning cleaner benchmark concepts or preserving every frozen-model behavior.

</details>


### [169] [Abliteration Is Not a Scalpel: Off-Target Effects of Refusal Removal on Decision Disposition Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17427)
*Aleksander Fafuła*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现 abliteration（权重中删除拒绝方向）不仅删除拒绝行为，还会导致模型决策风格系统性改变：更乐观、更长自我辩护、更少不确定性词汇表达，且不同模型家族对置信度的影响相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: abliteration 作为流行的“无审查”模型标准方法，其对模型行为的副作用尚未得到充分评估，需要通过无拒绝行为的任务分离纯副作用。

Method: 使用21,600个华沙证券交易所股票的上下涨跌决策任务，通过冻结管道控制变量，仅模型可变；严格控制来源（官方BF16检查点、单一abliteration作者、相同服务栈、字节相同冻结提示），比较Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507两个MoE家族的基础版与abliterated版。

Result: 三个效应在两个家族中均复现：abliterated模型系统性更乐观（Gemma +12.2pp，Qwen +7.4pp）、自我辩护更长、被迫自我批评时使用更少明确不确定性词汇。第四个效应符号相反：同一操作使Gemma-abliterated置信度降低而Qwen-abliterated置信度升高（家族置信区间不重叠）。能力协变量排除指令遵循退化驱动，无分支显示经济技能；来源审计发现两个独立污染通道。

Conclusion: abliteration 不是干净的手术，会产生可测量的决策风格副作用；部署“无审查”模型作为代理等于部署一个可测量的不同决策者，而非仅删除拒绝的基础模型。工具链伪影在社区修改检查点研究中可能是常态。

Abstract: Abliteration - deleting a model's refusal direction from its weights - is the standard recipe behind popular "uncensored" open-weight models. We show the surgery is not clean. As a disposition probe we use 21,600 decisions under uncertainty - weekly up/down calls on 60 Warsaw Stock Exchange equities over 18 weeks, replayed through a frozen pipeline so the decision-layer model is the only variable. The task elicits no refusals at all, so any between-arm delta is pure side effect. Holding provenance constant (official BF16 checkpoints, a single abliteration author, an identical serving stack, one byte-identical frozen prompt), we compare base and abliterated arms of two Mixture-of-Experts families, Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507. Three effects replicate across both families (weeks-clustered bootstrap CIs excluding zero): abliterated models are systematically more optimistic (+12.2 pp Gemma, +7.4 pp Qwen; the confirmed preregistered endpoint), justify themselves at greater length, and use fewer explicit uncertainty words in forced self-critiques (both exploratory). A fourth effect reverses sign: the same operation makes Gemma-abliterated less confident and Qwen-abliterated more (family CIs non-overlapping) - one weight surgery, opposite shifts in expressed confidence. Capability covariates rule out instruction-following degradation as the driver, and no arm shows economic skill: the apparent edge of abliterated arms is regime beta, not alpha. Our provenance audit also caught two independent contamination channels - a mismatched-quantizer pilot pair and a stale community chat template that silently mangled the rendered prompt - suggesting toolchain artifacts are the rule in studies of community-modified checkpoints. Whoever deploys an "uncensored" model as an agent is deploying a measurably different decision-maker, not the base model minus refusals.

</details>


### [170] [Calibrated Alzheimer's Conversion Risk in Mild Cognitive Impairment: Persistent Homology of Clinical Trajectories with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17442)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次将持久同调应用于纵向临床轨迹点云，构建了首个提供个体层面不确定性估计的MCI-to-AD转换预测模型，在泄漏审计后实现了竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCI向阿尔茨海默病（AD）转换的预测模型缺乏个体层面的不确定性估计，且很少进行透明的数据泄漏审计。准确预测转换对于试验富集和护理规划至关重要。

Method: 研究分析了ADNI中741名MCI受试者，纠正了五种泄漏源。将Vietoris-Rips持久同调与子水平集代理、轨迹斜率和工程特征（共76个）结合，通过堆叠集成和5折交叉验证进行评估，并引入分裂一致性（split-conformal）方法提供个体风险保证。

Result: 加入TDA特征的Cox和随机生存森林模型的C-index分别提升至0.799和0.826（分别提升+0.045和+0.014）。主要嵌套AUC为0.840，外部验证AUC为0.879。H0持久熵是SHAP分析的首要特征，并与APOE4剂量显著相关。交叉一致性覆盖率达到90.4%±2.2%。

Conclusion: 研究提出H0持久熵作为认知衰退的拓扑生物标志物，证明了经泄漏审计和一致性校准的流程可实现竞争性准确度，并提供了该任务前所未有的个体层面不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Background. Predicting conversion from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to Alzheimer's disease (AD) is central to trial enrichment and care planning, yet existing models provide no individual-level uncertainty estimates and rarely include transparent leakage audits. We introduce the first application of persistent homology to longitudinal clinical trajectory point clouds for this task, and the first split-conformal individual risk guarantee for any AD-conversion model. Methods. We analysed 741 MCI subjects (240 converters, 32.4%) from ADNI with a uniform 4-year follow-up cap. Five leakage sources were corrected; without them a naive pipeline achieved AUC=0.934, inflated by +0.075. Vietoris-Rips persistent homology and sublevel-set proxies were combined with trajectory slopes and engineered features (76 total) in a stacking ensemble evaluated by 5-fold cross-validation. Results. Cox and Random Survival Forest models with TDA features achieved concordance C=0.799 and C=0.826 versus C=0.753 and C=0.812 without (+0.045 and +0.014). The primary nested AUC is 0.840 (same-fold bound 0.866); external AUC was 0.879 on a zero-overlap ADNI-2/GO/3 cohort. H0 persistence entropy was the top SHAP feature and significantly associated with APOE4 dosage (Spearman r=-0.191, p<0.0001, Bonferroni-corrected). Cross-conformal coverage was 90.4%+-2.2% (target 90%); empirical external coverage 96.9%. Maximum fairness gap in false-negative rate across seven subgroups was 0.092. Conclusions. We propose H0 persistence entropy as a topological biomarker of cognitive decline and demonstrate that a leakage-audited, conformally calibrated pipeline reaches competitive accuracy with individual-level uncertainty quantification not previously available for this task.

</details>


### [171] [Residual-Guided Multi-Resolution Refinement of Foundation Models: A Case Study in Drought Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17507)
*Wentao Gao,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Thuc Duy Le,Jixue Liu,Yanchang Zhao,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RGMR框架，通过残差引导的多分辨率细化方法，在不更新模型参数的情况下，显著提升了时间序列基础模型在区域气候预测任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域气候预测对时间序列基础模型提出了独特挑战。现有模型通常通过单次推理处理时间模式，而专家气候学家则采用多尺度时间分析和基于系统误差诊断的迭代细化方法。这种专家方法与现有模型推理方式之间存在差距，需要一种能够模仿专家分析流程的方法来提升气候预测性能。

Method: 提出了RGMR（残差引导的多分辨率细化）框架，这是一种推理时框架，能够适应预训练的基础模型执行结构化的从粗到细细化过程进行气候预测，而无需更新主干网络参数。该框架架构无关，可应用于不同的时间序列基础模型主干。在干旱预测任务中，使用标准化降水蒸散指数（SPEI）进行评估。

Result: RGMR在三个南澳大利亚站点和三个南澳大利亚以外的额外区域上，跨越三个不同的TSFM主干网络（TimesFM、TimeGPT、TabPFN）均表现一致，持续降低了测试集MSE。应用于TimesFM时，该框架在三个南澳大利亚站点上将一个月提前期的SPEI MSE降低了高达18.9%（平均降低约18.7%）。

Conclusion: RGMR为在区域气候预测工作流程中部署冻结的时间序列基础模型提供了一条实用途径。该方法无需更新模型参数即可显著提升预测性能，展现了在实际气候预测应用中的实用价值。

Abstract: Regional climate prediction presents unique challenges for time series foundation models, which typically process temporal patterns through single-pass inference. Expert climatologists, in contrast, employ multi-scale temporal analysis and iterative refinement based on systematic error diagnosis. We present RGMR (Residual-Guided Multi-Resolution Refinement), an inference-time framework that adapts pre-trained foundation models to perform structured coarse-to-fine refinement for climate forecasting without updating backbone parameters. Applied to drought forecasting using the Standardized Precipitation Evapotranspiration Index (SPEI), RGMR is architecture-agnostic across the three TSFM backbones evaluated per site (TimesFM, TimeGPT, TabPFN) and consistently lowers test-set MSE on three South Australian sites and three additional regions outside South Australia. Applied to TimesFM, the wrapper reduces one-month-ahead SPEI MSE by up to 18.9\% across the three South Australian sites (mean reduction $\approx$18.7\%). Overall, RGMR provides a practical route for deploying frozen TSFMs in regional climate forecasting workflows.

</details>


### [172] [Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning: Towards Task-Specific Zero-Shot Models in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17508)
*Sazan Mahbub,Caleb Ellington,Zhiyuan Li,Yixin Yang,Souvik Kundu,Ben Lengerich,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL是一个概率元学习框架，通过从自然语言任务描述和已学习预测器的记忆中合成系数空间结构，实现零样本生成任务特定的可解释模型，在长尾临床程序预测任务中零样本和极小样本情况下分别达到73.4%和73.2%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中预测任务呈长尾分布、新临床目标频繁出现，模型需要保持可检查、不确定性感知并兼容人类监督，现有方法难以在缺乏标注数据时生成可靠且可解释的预测器。

Method: RAIL检索相关源任务，通过系数空间传递结构，在原始诊断特征空间生成新预测器；其概率化公式对检索、模型系数和预测提供不确定性估计，支持可靠性感知部署。

Result: 在长尾临床程序预测任务中，RAIL在无监督任务特定模型的零样本设置下达到73.4%准确率，在仅有2-4个样本的极小样本设置下保持73.2%准确率，而监督基线接近随机水平；临床信息增强的任务表示进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: RAIL为构建可扩展的临床预测系统提供了一条路径，能在适应新任务的同时保持可解释性和可靠性，支持不确定性标记和临床审查。

Abstract: We introduce Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning (RAIL), a probabilistic meta-learning framework for zero-shot generation of task-specific interpretable models that synthesizes coefficient-space structure from natural-language task descriptions and a memory of previously learned task-specific predictors. RAIL retrieves related source tasks, transfers structure through coefficient space, and generates a new predictor in the original diagnostic-feature space, enabling zero-shot and few-shot clinical procedure prediction with feature-level explanations. Its probabilistic formulation provides uncertainty over retrieval, model coefficients, and predictions, supporting reliability-aware deployment: uncertain predictions or unstable explanations can be flagged for additional clinical review rather than treated as automatic decisions. This makes RAIL particularly suited for healthcare settings, where prediction tasks are highly long-tailed, new clinical targets arise frequently, and models must remain inspectable, uncertainty-aware, and compatible with human oversight. Across long-tailed clinical procedure prediction tasks, RAIL maintains reliable performance across data-availability regimes: it achieves 73.4% accuracy in the held-out zero-shot settings, where no supervised task-specific model can be trained, and remains near 73.2% accuracy in the extreme few-shot regime with only 2-4 examples, where supervised task-specific models perform close to chance. RAIL further benefits from clinically informed task representations and yields retrieval, uncertainty, and coefficient-level diagnostics that make model behavior more transparent. These results suggest a path toward scalable clinical prediction systems that can adapt to new tasks while preserving interpretability and reliability.

</details>


### [173] [Lightweight Wrappers for Adapting Time Series Foundation Models to Regional Drought Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17511)
*Wentao Gao,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Thuc Duy Le,Jixue Liu,Yanchang Zhao,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级黑盒适配框架，通过推理时集成而非权重更新来增强冻结的时间序列基础模型，在区域气候预测中实现了最高26%的MSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型时间序列基础模型在零样本预测方面表现优异，但在区域气候预测应用中面临实际挑战：模型权重常为私有、本地训练记录有限、计算资源受限，使得传统微调方法不可行。

Method: 提出轻量级黑盒适配框架，包含两个即插即用模块：(1) SMR²（平稳性感知多分辨率残差），将输入分解为多分辨率时间视图，学习特定步长的残差修正来捕获区域动态，自适应集成形成单一预测；(2) MBB（移动块Bootstrap），通过块重采样保持时间依赖性，对时间相干的残差扰动进行集成以稳定点预测。两模块均采用bagging原理：构建输入或残差的多样视图，用相同的冻结骨干模型预测，然后聚合。

Result: 在澳大利亚南部多个站点的SPEI指数预测任务上验证，框架在多个骨干模型上持续提升预测性能，相比冻结骨干模型实现最高26%的MSE降低。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了资源受限场景下对冻结TSFM的有效适配，无需访问模型参数或进行微调，为区域气候预测系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large \emph{Time Series Foundation Models} (TSFMs) demonstrate strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities across diverse domains. However, their application to regional climate forecasting faces practical challenges: model weights are often proprietary, local training records are limited, and computational budgets are constrained, making traditional fine-tuning approaches infeasible. To address these constraints, we introduce a lightweight, black-box adaptation framework (requiring no access to backbone parameters and no backbone fine-tuning) that enhances frozen TSFMs at inference time through two plug-and-play wrappers: \textbf{SMR\textsuperscript{2}} (Stationarity aware multi-resolution Residual), which decomposes the input into multi-resolution temporal views, learns stride specific residual corrections that capture regional dynamics, then adaptively ensembles them into a single forecast, and \textbf{MBB} (Moving Block Bootstrap), which preserves temporal dependencies through block resampling and ensembles over temporally coherent residual perturbations to stabilize the point forecast. Both wrappers instantiate the same bagging style principle: they build diverse views of the input or its residuals, forecast each with the same frozen backbone, and aggregate, so all adaptation comes from inference time ensembling rather than any weight update. Evaluated on one month ahead Standardized Precipitation Evapotranspiration Index (SPEI) prediction across multiple sites in South Australia, our framework consistently improves forecasting performance across several backbone models, demonstrating up to 26\% mean squared error (MSE) reduction over the corresponding frozen backbone while enabling practical deployment in resource constrained regional forecasting systems.

</details>


### [174] [After the Euclidean Highway: Hyperbolic Expert AI as the Next Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17513)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Munho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HySAT方法，仅在损失层应用双曲损失函数，避免了在可训练适配器上应用双曲几何导致的训练崩溃，成功训练了6个专家级小语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家领域具有树状结构，而欧氏空间Transformer无法有效表示父子结构（随深度指数级衰减）。此前研究尝试在网络上应用双曲几何，但未解决"在何处应用曲率"这一关键问题，且发现将双曲几何应用于可训练适配器会导致严重的训练崩溃。

Method: 提出HySAT（Hyperbolic Structure-Aware Training）方法：仅在损失层应用双曲损失函数，而非在模型架构或适配器层面引入双曲几何。构建了6个专家级SLM（基于Llama 3.1和EXAONE 3.5），采用四种适配器策略，使用18.0M样本语料库，进行了约317K优化器步数的训练。

Result: HySAT方法成功避免了训练崩溃（零NaN，而对比方法产生17次崩溃），完成了~220 GPU-hours的训练。四臂消融实验成功分离出保持的流形不变量。通过三个命题和一个引理从理论上证明了仅在损失层放置的稳定性。四个模型已实际部署（包括一个面向消费者的线上模型），两个开源。数据在Zenodo公开（CC-BY-4.0）。

Conclusion: 双曲几何的应用位置至关重要：可训练适配器上的双曲几何会导致训练崩溃，而仅在损失层的双曲损失则能稳定训练。这为专家领域的树状结构建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Expert domains are trees; the Euclidean transformer is not, diluting parent-child structure exponentially at depth. The hyperbolic turn left one question unasked: not how much of a network to curve, but where curvature may touch the gradient. Placement is a law, not a knob: the same geometry on a trainable adapter collapses training (seventeen training collapses, ~220 GPU-hours), yet at the loss layer alone it trains without one -- this is HySAT (Hyperbolic Structure-Aware Training), hyperbolic losses at the loss layer only. Across six expert SLMs we constructed and deployed (Llama 3.1 and EXAONE 3.5; four adapter strategies; 18.0M-sample corpus; zero NaN over ~317K optimizer steps), a matched four-arm ablation isolates the preserved manifold invariant, and three propositions and a lemma prove why loss-only placement is stable where adapter-on-manifold is not. Four models are operationally deployed (one live, consumer-facing), two open-weight, with per-step traces and a seventeen-incident failure ledger on Zenodo (CC-BY-4.0).

</details>


### [175] [One-step lowest-variance selection in a Gaussian random-field model motivated by masked diffusion: Total correlation and a square root collision threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17522)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了置信度引导并行解掩码中的单步选择问题，建立了随机几何基线模型，发现在保守的次平方根区域内条件高斯全相关系数概率趋于零，而在平方根尺度下保持非负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到掩码离散扩散中置信度引导并行解掩码机制的启发，作者希望理解单步选择过程中低分数位置的几何结构如何影响因子化并行解码的依赖成本。

Method: 构建了一个简化的高斯随机场模型，使用局部依赖的非负分数场表示位置不确定性，调度器选择分数最小的K个位置，通过距离依赖的高斯相关模型度量选定位置间的依赖关系，形成可处理的框架。

Result: 在保守的次平方根区域内，选定块的条件高斯全相关系数概率趋于零；在平方根尺度下，该系数保持非负且具有正的渐近概率，并存在严格正的期望下界。合成实验支持了预测的有限样本行为。

Conclusion: 研究为理解预算大小、分数依赖性和空间相关性如何共同影响掩码离散扩散中基于置信度的单步选择提供了严格的随机几何基线。

Abstract: Motivated by confidence-guided parallel unmasking in masked discrete diffusion, we study a single selection step in a stylized Gaussian random-field model. A locally dependent nonnegative score field represents position wise uncertainty, and the scheduler selects the K positions with the smallest scores. Dependence among the selected positions is measured through a distance-dependent Gaussian correlation model. This separation provides a tractable framework for quantifying how the geometry of low-score locations affects the dependence cost of factorized parallel decoding. We establish two complementary results. In a conservative sub-square-root regime, the conditional Gaussian total correlation of the selected block vanishes in probability. At the square-root scale, it remains non-negligible with positive asymptotic probability and admits a strictly positive expectation lower bound. Synthetic experiments support the predicted finite-size behavior. These results provide a rigorous stochastic-geometry baseline for understanding how budget size, score dependence, and spatial correlation jointly shape one-step confidence-based selection in masked discrete diffusion.

</details>


### [176] [FailureAtlas: A Taxonomy of Failure Modes in Multi-Provider LLM Serving Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17525)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了\fa{}框架，这是一个针对多提供商LLM网关故障的两轴分类法，发现了最严重的故障是"静默型"故障——它们返回HTTP 200状态码，通过所有标准健康检查，但会破坏应用状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多提供商LLM网关已成为关键的生产基础设施，但该架构层的故障模式缺乏系统性文档记录，相关故障案例分散在问题跟踪器和事后分析报告中，缺乏统一的分类框架来理解和分析这些故障。

Method: 提出了\fa{}两轴分类法：(1) 按故障起源层分类（网络/传输层、流式/协议层、状态/会话层、模型行为层、治理/成本层）；(2) 按可检测性分类（响亮型 vs 静默型）。通过公开bug报告和一手压力测试验证了五个目录条目，并进行了机制性根因分析，其中三个条目包含独立复现脚本。

Result: 发现了最严重的运维故障是静默型故障：它们返回HTTP 200状态码，通过所有标准健康检查，但会以需要语义级可观测性才能检测的方式破坏应用状态。在\cb{}评估活动中首次发现了两个静默故障案例：导致历史记录丢失的并发竞态条件，以及损坏工具调用载荷的流式索引冲突。

Conclusion: LLM网关的静默故障构成了严重的运维风险，传统的健康检查机制无法有效检测这些故障，需要开发语义级别的可观测性工具来识别和预防此类故障，这对LLM生产系统的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-provider LLM gateways reverse proxies that route, load-balance, and rate-limit requests across foundation-model APIs have become critical production infrastructure. Yet the failure modes specific to this architectural layer remain undocumented, scattered across issue trackers and post-mortems with no unifying framework.
