Evaluated on WikiText 2 using a compact transformer, L1 augmented attention achieves up to a 14.5% reduction in perplexity over the original transformer baseline and outperforms an RBF L2 kernel. Analysis of norm variance and learned L1 weights reveals distinct geometric roles across layers and strong head level specialization. These results demonstrate that enriching attention with L1 geometry provides a principled and effective improvement to similarity computation in modern language models, with practical benefits for both accuracy and parallel efficiency.

</details>


### [212] [Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18043)
*Zeyuan Song,Zheyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应Mamba神经算子(AMO)，通过构建Takenaka-Malmquist系统，将再生核集成到状态空间模型中，用于求解任意几何和网格上的偏微分方程，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学和工程应用中，准确求解任意几何和各种网格上的偏微分方程是一个重要任务，现有方法在处理复杂几何和网格时存在局限性，需要一种新的可解释神经算子框架。

Method: 提出自适应Mamba神经算子(AMO)，通过构建Takenaka-Malmquist系统，将再生核集成到状态空间模型(SSMs)中，而非使用SSMs的核积分公式。该方法与自适应傅里叶分解(AFD)理论相契合，能够逼近各种几何和网格上的PDE解流形。

Result: 在流体物理、固体物理和金融领域的多个挑战性基准PDE问题上进行了测试，涵盖点云、结构化网格、规则网格和不规则区域。AMO在相对L^2误差方面始终优于现有最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 本工作提出了一种新的设计可解释神经算子框架的范式，为在任意几何和网格上求解偏微分方程提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately solving partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries and a variety of meshes is an important task in science and engineering applications. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators (AMO), which integrates reproducing kernels for state-space models (SSMs) rather than the kernel integral formulation of SSMs. This is achieved by constructing Takenaka-Malmquist systems for the PDEs. AMO offers new representations that align well with the adaptive Fourier decomposition (AFD) theory and can approximate the solution manifold of PDEs on a wide range of geometries and meshes. In several challenging benchmark PDE problems in the fields of fluid physics, solid physics, and finance on point clouds, structured meshes, regular grids, and irregular domains, AMO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art solvers in terms of relative $L^2$ error. Overall, this work presents a new paradigm for designing explainable neural operator frameworks.

</details>


### [213] [SEE: Structure-aware Exploring \& Exploiting for Long-horizon GUI Agent Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18046)
*Zhuohang Fan,Beichen Zhang,Yuanfa Li,Changqiao Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SEE框架，通过构建UI转换图并规划生成多样化长轨迹数据，解决了GUI智能体数据收集困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI智能体数据收集依赖昂贵的人工演示或策略框架，容易过度采样常见流程，而遗漏罕见转换和复杂多步过程，缺乏高覆盖率、长视野的交互轨迹数据。

Method: 提出SEE两阶段数据合成框架：第一阶段通过构建显式UI转换图（覆盖界面和元素）进行高效探索；第二阶段通过规划和受控采样生成多样化多步轨迹，显式防止虚假循环并实现长视野组合。

Result: 在多个真实应用上生成平均长度14.8步且无虚假循环的轨迹；使用SEE微调的智能体在任务成功率和对新界面的泛化能力上均有提升。

Conclusion: SEE框架能够高效生成可复现、可解释且高质量的长轨迹数据，有效提升GUI智能体的性能与泛化能力，并将开源合成代码与数据集。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by vision-language models hold promise for automating real-world mobile tasks. However, progress is limited by the lack of high-coverage, long-horizon interaction trajectories collected from element-rich and rapidly evolving apps. Existing pipelines often rely on costly human demonstrations or on-policy framework, which tends to over-sample common flows while missing rare transitions and complex multi-step procedures. To address this problem, we propose SEE, a two-stage data synthesis framework consisting of (i) an efficient exploration stage that builds an explicit UI transition graph over screens and elements, and (ii) a graph-based synthesis stage that composes diverse multi-step trajectories via planning and controlled sampling. This design yields reproducible and explainable data generation, while explicitly preventing spurious cycles and enabling long-horizon composition. Across multiple real-world apps, SEE produces trajectories with an average length of 14.8 steps while avoiding spurious loops, and agents fine-tuned on SEE achieve improved task success and generalization to unseen screens. We will publicly release our synthesis code and dataset.

</details>


### [214] [SGN: A Similarity-based Generative Network for Data Generation under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18072)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Xincheng Chen,Yuncheng Wu,Zhaojing Luo,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参数更新的可重用生成框架SGN，通过在潜在空间利用目标域少量标注样本进行相似性组合，实现跨域数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在源域训练后，面对分布偏移的目标域时生成的样本往往质量不佳。现有的目标域自适应方法虽然能缓解此问题，但通常需要额外的优化过程和特定于域的参数调整，增加了应用成本和复杂性。

Method: 提出基于相似性的生成网络（SGN），采用编码器-解码器架构，在潜在空间学习由标签诱导的成对相似性结构。在生成阶段，利用目标域的小规模标注代表性样本集，将其编码后在已学习的潜在空间中进行组合，使生成样本继承目标域特征并保持类别一致性，无需针对新目标域进行参数更新。

Result: 理论分析了所提相似性结构的可实现性及维度要求。在图像和表格数据集上的实验表明，SGN在源域到目标域分布偏移场景下的目标引导数据增强任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: SGN作为一种可重用框架，通过单次源域训练即可泛化至新目标域，在无需参数更新的情况下有效提升了目标域数据增强的质量与一致性，为跨域生成任务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models trained on a source domain often produce samples that are poorly aligned with shifted target domains, limiting their effectiveness for target-domain data augmentation. Although target-specific adaptation can reduce this mismatch, it typically requires additional optimization and domain-specific parameters. We propose a Similarity-based Generative Network (SGN), a reusable framework that is trained once on labeled source data and applied to new target domains without parameter updates. SGN learns a latent space structured by label-induced pairwise similarities while preserving reconstructive information through an encoder-decoder architecture. At generation time, a small labeled representative set from the target domain is encoded and combined in the learned latent space, allowing the generated samples to inherit target-specific characteristics while maintaining class consistency. We further analyze the realizability and dimensionality requirements of the proposed similarity structure. Experiments on image and tabular datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SGN for target-guided data augmentation under source-to-target distribution shifts.

</details>


### [215] [Sobek: Streaming Equivariant Tensor Product Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18074)
*Vladimir Chorošajev,Cédric Bény*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流式计算方法来消除等变图神经网络中边级别中间张量的内存开销，在保持性能的同时将内存使用降低99%，并将可处理的工作负载规模提升了两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变图神经网络实现需要为每条边生成特定的权重、消息和伴随张量，导致张量积的工作空间和内存流量随图规模和算子宽度快速增长，限制了可处理的工作负载规模，并阻碍大规模问题充分利用GPU计算能力。

Method: 通过重新关联径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合操作，将边局部的乘积结果直接消耗到有界的接收端状态中，从而消除边级别中间张量的存储需求。这种流式计算公式保持了完全连接的多重性混合，并适用于前向传播、反向传播和双重反向传播三个计算阶段。基于此方法实现了Sobek生成式CUDA后端。

Result: 在两种算子族和所有三种微分阶数的75个容量匹配对比中，Sobek在所有情况下都实现了1.2倍至49.7倍的加速，峰值内存分配降低高达99%，并且能够处理比OpenEquivariance大两个数量级的工作负载，同时保持接近峰值的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 边规模的张量积工作空间是传统执行调度方式的产物，而非等变卷积算子本身的固有特性。通过优化执行调度，可以显著降低内存开销并提升计算效率。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks repeatedly apply edge-conditioned tensor-product convolutions over graph edges. Conventional implementations materialize edge-specific weights, messages, and adjoints, causing tensor-product workspace and memory traffic to grow rapidly with graph size and operator width. This limits feasible workloads and can prevent larger problems from fully utilizing the GPU.
  We show that these edge-sized intermediates are artifacts of the execution schedule, not requirements of the equivariant operator. By reassociating radial projection, spherical-harmonic coupling, and graph aggregation, edge-local products can be consumed directly into bounded receiver-side state. The resulting streaming formulation preserves fully connected multiplicity mixing and extends through forward, backward, and double backward.
  We implement this formulation in Sobek, a generated-CUDA backend, and evaluate it across edge-scaling regimes and varied feature structures. Across two operator families and all three differentiation orders, Sobek is faster in all 75 capacity-matched comparisons, with speedups ranging from $1.2\times$ to $49.7\times$, and reduces peak allocated memory by up to 99\%. It also executes workloads up to two orders of magnitude beyond OpenEquivariance's capacity while retaining near-peak throughput. These results show that edge-scaled tensor-product workspace is a property of the conventional schedule, not of equivariant convolution itself.

</details>


### [216] [Generalised Bellman recurrence and three dualities in sequential decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18077)
*Fernando E. Rosas,David Hyland,Daniel Polani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从三个条件（动力学通过充分统计量分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合与前两者兼容）推导出Bellman方程的数学基础，揭示了三个对偶性，统一了强化学习、控制和决策理论中的相关方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Bellman方程为何具有其特定形式，探索最优值函数递归性质背后的数学原理，理解强化学习、控制和决策理论中不同方法的统一基础。

Method: 从三个基本条件出发推导Bellman方程：（1）动力学通过充分统计量分解；（2）回报递归分解；（3）不确定性聚合与前两者兼容。当三个条件在共同状态上成立时，Bellman方程从它们的一致性中产生；当某个条件失效时，通过状态增强或变形回报/动力学来恢复可处理性。

Result: 证明了Bellman方程从三个条件的相互一致性中自然产生，揭示了三个对偶性：概率与回报之间、回报与聚合之间、聚合与概率之间的对偶性，这些对偶性源于单一构造，统一了强化学习、控制和决策理论中独立发展的方法。

Conclusion: 提供了Bellman方程的理论基础框架，阐明了其成立的数学条件，为理解和改进强化学习算法提供了理论依据，同时建立了不同领域方法之间的理论联系。

Abstract: What gives the Bellman equation its form? We show that the recursive properties of optimal value functions follow from three conditions: that the dynamics decomposes through sufficient statistics, that the return decomposes recursively, and that the aggregation of uncertainty is compatible with both. When all three conditions hold on a common state, the Bellman equation arises from their mutual consistency; when one fails, tractability can often be recovered by augmenting the state or by deforming return or dynamics. The same conditions are shown to give rise to three dualities: one between probability and return, one between return and aggregation, and one between aggregation and probability. Our framework reveals these dualities as arising from a single construction, unifying methods developed separately across reinforcement learning, control, and decision theory.

</details>


### [217] [SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Eric Schniedermeyer,Artem Burchanow,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SelectInfer框架，通过神经元级别的选择性加载和计算优化，在保持任务性能的同时显著降低了LLM在边缘设备上的内存占用和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在NLP任务中表现出色，但高昂的计算和内存需求使其难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。现有的粗粒度剪枝或量化方法往往会损害模型精度或需要重新训练和微调。

Method: 提出了SelectInfer神经元级优化框架：1）通过离线LLM分析器识别任务特定和通用神经元；2）选择性加载：仅加载离线阶段识别的重要神经元子集以减少内存占用；3）选择性计算：在运行时动态计算最相关的神经元。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，SelectInfer在保持任务性能的同时，实现了显著的内存占用和计算量减少。

Conclusion: SelectInfer为在边缘设备上部署LLM提供了实用的解决方案，证明了神经元级优化在平衡性能和效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, but their high computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. Existing approaches to model compression and optimization often rely on coarse-grained pruning or quantization, which can compromise accuracy or require re-training and fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce SelectInfer, a neuron-level optimization framework that enables efficient LLM inference on edge devices through selective neuron loading and computation. By profiling and identifying both task-specific and general-purpose neurons using an offline LLM profiler, SelectInfer implements two key optimizations: selective loading, which reduces memory footprint by selectively loading a subset of neurons that were identified to be most important during the offline stage, and selective computation, which dynamically computes only the most relevant neurons at runtime. Evaluation across multiple datasets shows that SelectInfer achieves significant reductions in memory footprint and computation while preserving task performance, making it a practical step towards enabling LLM deployment on edge devices

</details>


### [218] [Enhancing Rubric-based RL via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18082)
*Mingxuan Xia,Yuhang Yang,Chao Ye,Shuai Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Siqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CriPO方法，通过在线自蒸馏增强基于评分标准的强化学习，同时解决了未探索标准(UC)和被抑制标准(SC)两种失败模式，无需引入训练-推理不匹配，在医学和科学基准上性能更强且优化步骤减少约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的强化学习存在探索受限问题：未探索标准(UC)无法获得优化信号；现有方法通过外部引导解决UC但引入训练-推理不匹配。更重要的是，作者发现了一种被忽视的失败模式——被抑制标准(SC)，即某些rollout满足的标准在优化过程中因标量奖励聚合而被分配非正聚合优势，导致学习信号丢失。分析显示SC广泛存在：超过57%的样本在整个训练过程中表现出这种失败模式，平均每个样本有1.8个SC。

Method: 提出Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization (CriPO)，通过在线策略自蒸馏增强基于评分标准的RL。对于UC，CriPO构建标准注入自教师，计算局部前向KL损失，将缺失行为注入策略；对于SC，CriPO采用反事实自教师定位负优势rollout中的标准相关token，将其token级优势翻转为正值，保留原本会被抑制的有用模式。该方法无需引入训练-推理不匹配。

Result: 在医学和科学基准测试中，CriPO持续优于基于评分标准的RL基线方法，实现更强的最终性能，且优化步骤减少约2倍。SC失败模式的普遍性得到验证：超过57%的样本在整个训练过程中表现出该失败模式，平均每个样本有1.8个SC。

Conclusion: CriPO成功解决了基于评分标准的RL中的两种根本性失败模式（UC和SC），无需引入训练-推理不匹配问题，显著提升了模型性能和训练效率，为开放性任务中的LLM优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL has recently shown promise in improving LLMs on open-ended tasks. A widely recognized limitation of rubric-based RL is limited exploration: criteria that no rollout manages to satisfy (Unexplored Criteria, UC) receive no optimization signal. Recent methods address this by incorporating rubric information as external guidance during rollout, yet they introduce a train-inference mismatch: the policy is optimized on rollouts produced under external guidance while this guidance is absent at inference time, causing error accumulation through autoregressive decoding. Moreover, these exploration-focused approaches overlook a fundamentally different failure mode that we term Suppressed Criteria (SC) -- criteria that are satisfied by some rollouts yet whose learning signals are lost during optimization because scalar reward aggregation assigns them non-positive aggregate advantages. Our analysis reveals that SC are remarkably prevalent: over 57% of samples exhibit this failure mode throughout training, with an average of 1.8 SC per sample. To simultaneously address both UC and SC without introducing training-inference mismatch, we propose Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization (CriPO), which enhances rubric-based RL via on-policy self-distillation. For UC, CriPO constructs a criterion-injection self-teacher and computes a localized forward-KL loss to inject missing behaviors into the policy. For SC, CriPO employs a counterfactual self-teacher to locate criterion-relevant tokens in negative-advantage rollouts and flips their token-level advantages to positive values, preserving useful patterns that would otherwise be suppressed. Experiments on medicine and science benchmarks demonstrate that CriPO consistently outperforms rubric-based RL, achieving stronger final performance with approximately $2\times$ fewer optimization steps.

</details>


### [219] [The Label Complexity of Class-Conditional Coverage under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18088)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移下分位数预测的类别条件有效性问题，证明在协变量和标签联合偏移时无法仅用无标签目标数据实现有效且高效的类别条件覆盖，量化了恢复类别有效性所需的目标标签数量，并在骨架动作识别基准上验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准识别系统评估通常存在分布偏移，导致分位数预测在边际覆盖率保持正常时，类别条件覆盖率却会出现显著下降；例如在跨主体骨架动作基准上，边际覆盖率保持90%，但最差动作类别覆盖率仅70%，60个类别中有10个类别覆盖率低于80%。这种类别条件覆盖失败是隐性的，需要研究如何恢复类别条件有效性及其代价。

Method: 理论分析：证明协变量和标签联合偏移时，目标类别条件分数分布无法从源标签和无标签目标样本识别，因此无标签方法无法同时实现有效且高效的类别条件覆盖。量化分析：推导类别有效性所需的目标标签数量下界和上界。实验验证：在骨架动作识别基准和自然图像损坏基准上进行三类不同严重程度的分布偏移实验，评估边际覆盖率与类别条件覆盖率的关系。

Result: 仅使用源标签进行类别条件校准可在分布偏移保持边际覆盖时恢复大部分类别条件覆盖差距；当边际覆盖本身失效时，该方法也停止有效。使用分类器伪标签的方法最多仅能带来小的常数倍效率提升，但覆盖率会崩溃。恢复类别有效性需要每类少量目标标签；同时保证有效性和类别效率所需标签数量随效率容忍度的逆平方和类别数的对数增长。

Conclusion: 在分布偏移场景下，仅依靠无标签目标数据无法实现既有效又高效的类别条件覆盖；需要少量目标标签恢复类别有效性。仅使用源标签的类别条件校准方法在边际覆盖有效时可以显著改善类别条件覆盖，其有效性边界由分布偏移的严重程度决定，这一规律在不同模态数据上均成立。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of many recognition systems contains distribution shift by construction, since benchmarks place disjoint conditions in the training and test splits. Under such a shift, split conformal prediction keeps marginal coverage near the nominal level while per-class coverage fails silently: on a real cross-subject skeleton benchmark, marginal coverage stays near ninety percent, the worst action class is covered about seventy percent of the time, and ten of the sixty classes fall below eighty percent coverage.
  We characterize the cost of restoring per-class validity. First, an impossibility: once the shift acts jointly on the covariates and the labels, the target class-conditional score law is unidentified from source labels and an unlabeled target sample, so no label-free method attains per-class coverage that is at once valid and efficient. Second, we make the cost precise: per-class validity alone needs only a handful of target labels per class, while the label count necessary and sufficient for validity together with per-class efficiency grows as the inverse square of the efficiency tolerance and the logarithm of the number of classes, with matching upper and lower bounds. Third, within the evaluated prediction-powered inference family, even the most favorable use of the classifier's own pseudo-labels on an unbounded unlabeled target pool improves efficiency by at most a small constant factor where coverage collapses.
  Skeleton action recognition is our real-data case study. A per-class calibration using source labels alone recovers a substantial share of the per-class gap while the shift preserves marginal coverage, and stops helping exactly when marginal coverage itself breaks. Three real shifts of increasing severity trace this boundary, and the same collapse and recovery appears on a natural-image corruption benchmark, beyond any single modality.

</details>


### [220] [LLM-as-a-Coach: Experiential Learning for Non-Verifiable Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18110)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Guanheng Chen,He Zhu,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出体验式学习（EL）方法，将LLM评估模型从裁判转变为教练，通过将反馈转化为可迁移的经验知识来指导策略学习，在开放式任务上表现优于基于标量奖励的强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在开放式任务中将基于评分标准的评估压缩为标量奖励，丢弃了丰富的文本反馈，且无法区分具有不同质量特征的响应。这导致监督信号带宽有限，难以保持高质量响应之间的细粒度偏好差异。

Method: 提出体验式学习（EL）框架：1）将LLM评估模型重新设计为教练角色；2）教练将对每个策略响应的评估提炼为可迁移的经验知识；3）通过策略内上下文蒸馏将经验知识内化到策略模型中。这种方法提供了比标量奖励更高带宽的反馈通道。

Result: 在两种策略家族上，使用策略本身或专有模型的反馈，EL在留出测试集和未见的开放式任务上均优于基于评分标准的强化学习。EL在训练分布之外具有更好的泛化能力，并能缓解奖励黑客问题。

Conclusion: 经验知识作为一种更丰富、更具泛化性的学习信号，适用于非可验证任务的模型后训练，为开放式任务的学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on open-ended tasks compresses an LLM's rubric-based evaluation into a scalar reward, discarding rich textual feedback and conflating responses with distinct quality profiles. We propose Experiential Learning (EL), which repurposes the feedback model from an LLM-as-a-Judge into an LLM-as-a-Coach. The coach distills its assessment of each on-policy response into transferable experiential knowledge, which conditions a teacher model and is internalized by the policy through on-policy context distillation. Compared with scalar rewards, this higher-bandwidth feedback channel provides dense supervision and preserves fine-grained preferences among high-quality responses. Across two policy families, with feedback from the policy itself or a proprietary model, EL consistently outperforms rubric-based RL on held-out and unseen open-ended tasks. Notably, EL generalizes better beyond the training distribution, and mitigates reward hacking. These findings establish experiential knowledge as a richer and more generalizable learning signal for post-training on non-verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [221] [Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections for Parameter-Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18130)
*Valentijn Oldenburg,Floris de Kam,Bente Zuijdam,Lieve Eberson,Nicky van Zutphen,Stef de Wildt,Ivo Verhoeven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于流形约束的超连接（mHC）作为新型参数高效微调方法，通过在冻结的OLMo-2骨干上添加可学习的残差路由模块实现微调。研究发现mHC单独使用不如LoRA稳定，但在相同参数预算下，mHC+LoRA组合能改善语言建模损失并在特定任务上有性能提升，揭示了残差路由作为独立PEFT新轴的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法主要针对权重或激活值进行适配，但忽略了Transformer中的残差连接这一关键组件。残差连接作为Transformer架构的核心部分，在微调场景下尚未被充分探索作为参数高效微调的新途径。本文旨在探索残差连接的泛化形式作为新型PEFT方法的可行性。

Method: 提出流形约束的超连接（mHC）方法，将残差连接泛化为可学习的残差路由模块。具体实现为：在冻结的OLMo-2模型骨干上包裹可学习的残差路由模块，通过流形约束对路由矩阵进行正则化。研究同时探索了将残差混合矩阵固定为身份矩阵的变体，以及mHC与LoRA的组合方案，在1B和7B两个规模上进行实验验证。

Result: 主要发现包括：1）mHC能够成功微调冻结的Transformer模型；2）在微调场景下，将残差混合矩阵固定为身份矩阵往往能提升性能，这与预训练设置有本质不同；3）作为独立PEFT方法，mHC性能不如LoRA稳定；4）在相同可训练参数预算下，mHC+LoRA组合能改善语言建模损失，并在特定基准任务上获得性能提升（1B和7B规模均适用）。

Conclusion: 残差路由作为参数高效微调的新维度具有独特价值和潜力。虽然mHC单独使用效果不稳定，但其与LoRA的组合展示了互补优势，证明了在Transformer微调中探索残差连接变体的可行性。该工作为PEFT领域开辟了新的研究方向，残差路由可作为独立于权重和激活值适配的第三条PEFT路径。

Abstract: Most parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) methods adapt weights or activations, thus leaving one of the key Transformer components unchanged: residual connections. This paper investigates Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), a generalisation of residual connections, as a novel PEFT approach, wrapping frozen OLMo-2 backbones with learned residual routing modules. We find that mHC can finetune frozen Transformers, but that its role differs fundamentally from the original pre-training setting: in finetuning, fixing the residual mixing matrix to identity often improves performance. As a standalone PEFT method, mHC does not consistently outperform LoRA. However, at matched trainable parameter budgets, mHC+LoRA combinations improve language-modelling loss and show task-dependent benchmark gains at both 1B and 7B scale. Overall, our results identify residual routing as a distinct and promising novel PEFT axis.

</details>


### [222] [Do Language Models Dream of Binding Molecules? Benchmarking LLMs under Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18144)
*Thomas MacDougall,Maksim Kuznetsov,Roman Schutski,Rim Shayakhmetov,Maxim Malkov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了通用大语言模型（LLM）在复杂三维空间约束下的药物设计能力，发现其虽落后于专用扩散模型，但在同时处理多种空间约束方面表现出潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在基于结构的药物设计（SBDD）三维分子生成中占据主导地位，LLM在该领域也展现出竞争力。然而，LLM在处理物理和三维空间环境方面的推理能力尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在系统分析当前通用LLM在处理复杂三维约束方面的能力，并与专业扩散模型等基线方法进行比较。

Method: 研究考虑了基于蛋白质口袋的三维配体生成，并结合配体和相互作用衍生的空间约束，包括锚定片段、药效团点和强制的口袋-配体相互作用。为此，研究者引入了3D-Fit——一种用于评估LLM在多条件空间分子生成任务上的性能的高效标记基准测试策略。

Result: 研究发现LLM在空间能力方面呈现出清晰的模式：虽然其在性能上仍落后于最先进的方法（如扩散模型），但它们表现出令人期待的潜力，能够同时处理多种空间约束，从而实现向异构设置的扩展。

Conclusion: 当前通用LLM在三维空间分子生成任务中展现出一定的空间推理能力，能够处理多种空间约束，尽管性能尚不及专业模型，但其发展潜力值得关注，为未来LLM在结构化药物设计中的应用提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) leverages the 3D structure of protein targets, often complemented by other spatial constraints, to generate candidate binding molecules. While diffusion models have dominated as a leading paradigm for high-quality 3D molecule generation, LLM-based methods are rapidly emerging in molecular design and have shown competitive performance in pocket-conditioned molecular generation. However, their ability to reason about physics and 3D spatial environments is largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze whether current general-purpose LLMs are capable of navigating complex 3D constraints compared to established baselines such as specialized diffusion models. We consider 3D ligand generation conditioned on protein pockets together with ligand- and interaction-derived spatial constraints, including anchor fragments, pharmacophore points, and mandatory pocket-ligand interactions. To enable this evaluation, we introduce 3D-Fit - a token-efficient benchmarking strategy for assessing LLM performance on multi-conditioned spatial molecule generation. Our findings reveal a clear pattern in LLM spatial capabilities: while they still lag behind state-of-the-art approaches, they are promising and can handle multiple spatial constraints simultaneously, enabling scaling to heterogeneous setups.

</details>


### [223] [Totally Positive Matrices and the Highest-Order Coefficients of the Characteristic Polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18148)
*Tiago Closs,Leandro Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过特征多项式最高阶系数(a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3})可以有效区分全正矩阵与非全正矩阵，不同结构化全正矩阵族在三维系数空间中呈现可分离的椭球几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全正矩阵是否可以通过其特征多项式的最高阶系数进行区分，以及确定哪些系数最具判别信息。

Method: 利用神经网络分类器和特征归因方法分析系数重要性；在维度5、10、30下构建由正双对角矩阵乘积、Vandermonde矩阵、Cauchy矩阵等结构化全正矩阵族组成的数据集；采用Mahalanobis椭球对三维系数空间进行几何刻画。

Result: 系数(a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3})已包含强判别信息，可在5、10、30维下有效分离全正与非全正矩阵；分离呈现明显非线性，并可通过Mahalanobis椭球自然描述；不同结构化全正矩阵族具有不同的椭球特征，且随维度增加分离程度增强。

Conclusion: 提出了关于结构化全正矩阵族在三个最高阶特征系数所确定空间中几何分离的猜想。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which totally positive matrices can be distinguished through the highest-order coefficients of their characteristic polynomials. To identify the most informative coefficients, we also employed neural-network classifiers together with feature-attribution methods. Using datasets built from several structured totally positive families, including products of positive bidiagonal matrices, Vandermonde matrices, and Cauchy matrices, we find that the coefficients (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) already contain strong discriminatory information for separating totally positive from non-totally positive matrices in dimensions 5, 10, and 30. The resulting separation is markedly nonlinear and admits a natural geometric description in the corresponding three-dimensional coefficient space by means of Mahalanobis ellipsoids. These ellipsoids enclose the totally positive samples while excluding most non-totally positive ones. Moreover, different structured totally positive families exhibit distinct ellipsoidal signatures, and the separation between these signatures increases with the dimension. These observations lead us to formulate a conjecture on the geometric separation of structured totally positive families in the space determined by the three highest-order characteristic coefficients.

</details>


### [224] [Differentiable Logic Gate Networks for Low-Latency EEG Classification on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18149)
*Shyamal Y. Dharia,Stephen D. Smith,Camilo E. Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出可微分逻辑门网络(Diff-Logic)用于边缘设备上的实时EEG分类，通过布尔电路实现高效推理，在痴呆检测任务上优于MLP基线，在情感识别上以较小性能代价换取了显著的速度和内存优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络在边缘设备上进行实时EEG分类时，浮点运算成为主要瓶颈。现有方法难以同时满足低延迟、低功耗和小模型尺寸的需求，特别是在资源受限的便携式脑机接口应用场景中。

Method: 采用可微分逻辑门网络(Diff-Logic)作为硬件原生替代方案，将模型编译为纯布尔电路，通过位级CPU操作执行。在四个EEG数据集上进行严格的等参数实验，涵盖痴呆检测(二分类)和情感识别(三分类)两个任务，在四个复杂度层级(50k-500k参数)下与匹配容量的MLP和二值神经网络(BNN)基线进行对比。在7W功耗的Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano CPU(单核)上进行部署评估。

Result: 在痴呆筛查任务上，Diff-Logic达到80.2% Macro F1，比MLP基线提高6.8%。在情感识别任务上，MLP保持中等性能优势但推理延迟高2.3倍、模型尺寸大14倍。关键发现：Diff-Logic推理时间在模型规模增加10倍时几乎保持恒定，在最大复杂度层级上相比MLP实现2.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑的神经架构是资源受限脑机接口的实用范式，能够在满足便携式边缘部署的延迟和内存约束的同时，实现竞争性或优越的性能。该研究为边缘设备上的实时EEG处理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Real-time EEG classification on edge devices is bottlenecked by the floating-point arithmetic of conventional neural networks. We investigated Differentiable Logic Gate Networks (Diff-Logic) as a hardware-native alternative that compiles models into pure Boolean circuits executable via bitwise CPU operations. Through rigorous iso-parameter experiments across four EEG datasets spanning two classification tasks, binary dementia detection and 3-class emotion recognition, we compared Diff-Logic against matched-capacity Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) and Binarized Neural Network (BNN) baselines at four complexity tiers (50k-500k parameters). On dementia screening, Diff-Logic achieved 80.2% Macro F1, outperforming the MLP baseline by 6.8%. On emotion recognition, the MLP retained a moderate performance advantage but incurred a 2.3$\times$ higher latency and 14$\times$ larger model size when deployed on a power-constrained (7W) Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano CPU (Single-core). Critically, Diff-Logic inference time remained nearly constant across a 10$\times$ increase in model scale, achieving a peak speedup of 2.9$\times$ over MLPs at the largest complexity tier. Our results establish logic-based neural architectures as a practical paradigm for resource-constrained brain-computer interfaces, achieving competitive or superior performance while natively satisfying the latency and memory constraints of portable edge deployment. Code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/Shyamal-Dharia/eeg-difflogic

</details>


### [225] [The Calibration Channel Determines the Bayes-Error Proxy: An Exact Law for Temperature-Induced Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18162)
*Shreyas Pradeepkumar Khandale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了温度缩放校准对软标签贝叶斯误差估计器的扭曲效应，证明了该估计器可被调整至任意值，并通过理论和实验揭示了校准温度与误差估计稳定性之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明软标签贝叶斯误差估计器在后验概率不真实时具有脆弱性，即使完美校准的软标签也可能产生显著不准确的估计。现有工作提出等渗校准作为一致性补救方案，但尚未研究最广泛使用的后处理校准方法——温度缩放——如何扭曲该估计值。本文旨在精确刻画温度缩放对误差估计的影响机制。

Method: 提出理论证明建立温度缩放估计值与分类器间隔分布之间的精确关系，证明了严格单调性和连续双射性质；在高斯logit模型下推导出两参数闭式解；在CIFAR-10、Fashion-MNIST和SVHN的八个二分类任务上进行实证验证，对比理论预测与实际观测结果。

Result: 证明了温度缩放估计器随温度严格单调变化，固定分类器可产生任意估计值（0, 1/2区间）；实验显示在恒定测试误差下估计值变化达56-980倍，闭式解与实证曲线误差在0.018以内；最小化期望校准误差的温度与稳定估计值不重合。

Conclusion: 给出了校准扭曲机制的精确理论刻画，强化了实践建议：误差估计值仅在与产生概率的机制共同考虑时才有意义。这支持了校准作为补救措施的理论基础，并警示了单独解读估计值的风险。

Abstract: The soft-label Bayes-error estimator beta(z) = E[min(z, 1-z)] of Ishida et al. estimates the irreducible error of a binary task directly from probability-valued labels. Recent work by Ushio et al. showed that this estimator is fragile when the probabilities are not the true posterior: even perfectly calibrated soft labels can yield a substantially inaccurate estimate, and they propose isotonic calibration as a consistent remedy. We complement that line of work by characterizing exactly how the most widely used post-hoc calibration map -- temperature scaling -- distorts the proxy. We prove an exact, model-free identity reducing the temperature-scaled proxy to the classifier's margin distribution, from which we obtain (i) strict monotonicity in the temperature and (ii) a continuous bijection from the temperature axis onto the open interval (0, 1/2), so that a fixed classifier -- with fixed decisions and fixed 0-1 error -- can be made to report any proxy value whatsoever. Under a Gaussian model of the logits we further derive a two-parameter closed form for the entire proxy-versus-temperature curve. Across CIFAR-10, Fashion-MNIST, and SVHN (eight binary tasks), the proxy varies by 56x to 980x at constant test error, the closed form reproduces the empirical curve to within 0.018, and the calibration temperature that minimizes the expected calibration error does not coincide with any stable proxy value. Our results give a precise, predictive account of the distortion whose existence motivates calibration-based remedies, and they reinforce the practical recommendation that a proxy value is meaningful only together with the mechanism that produced its probabilities.

</details>


### [226] [OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163)
*Chinmay Rane,Kanishka Tyagi,Michael Manry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Output Reset (OR)平滑饱和规则作为PPO和GRPO中裁剪代理目标的替代方案，在LLM后训练中的效果。实验表明PPO-OR在GAE下奖励得分提升0.305，但GRPO-OR在组相对优势下虽减少方差却未提升得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO和GRPO中使用的裁剪代理目标在有利方向饱和时会引入目标函数导数的突变，这种不连续性可能影响优化稳定性。作者探索Output Reset (OR)——一种平滑的单侧饱和规则，作为大型语言模型后训练中裁剪机制的替代方案。

Method: 提出PPO-OR和GRPO-OR方法，用基于rollout相对token log-ratio空间的OR平方边界损失替代裁剪策略项：优势符号决定更新方向，token越过有利边界后贡献零直接OR残差。使用Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct在Anthropic hh-rlhf数据集上进行实验，采用共享奖励模型，每种方法三个随机种子，对比PPO-clip与PPO-OR（GAE设置）以及GRPO与GRPO-OR（组相对优势设置）。

Result: 在GAE下，PPO-OR的平均最终训练时奖励模型得分比PPO-clip高0.305，但跨种子方差更大；在组相对优势下，GRPO-OR未获得更高平均得分，但展现出更小的方差、接近零的终端OR残差和下降的超调比例，而GRPO裁剪目标轨迹保持变化。两种组相对方法的rollout-to-current log-ratio位移显著大于GAE方法，OR并未一致减少该位移。G=2时GRPO-OR的诊断指标未转化为奖励得分提升。

Conclusion: OR在两种匹配对比中都改变了优化行为，但观察到的奖励效应在两者间存在差异。OR是裁剪机制的有用替代选择，但其效果依赖于具体方法设置。在G=2时GRPO-OR的诊断改进未转化为奖励得分增益，更大组规模是否会改变此结果仍是开放问题。所报告得分为训练时奖励模型测量值，非held-out人类偏好性能。

Abstract: PPO and the GRPO baseline studied here use clipped surrogate objectives whose favorable-direction saturation introduces an abrupt change in the scalar objective's derivative. We ask whether Output Reset (OR), a smooth one-sided saturation rule, offers a useful alternative for large language model post-training. PPO-OR and GRPO-OR replace the clipped policy term with an OR squared-margin loss in rollout-relative token log-ratio space; the advantage sign determines the update direction, and a token contributes zero direct OR residual after crossing the favorable margin. We compare PPO-clip with PPO-OR under generalized advantage estimation (GAE), and GRPO with GRPO-OR under group-relative advantages, using \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} on Anthropic \texttt{hh-rlhf} with one shared reward model and three seeds per method. Under GAE, PPO-OR has a mean final training-time reward-model score $0.305$ higher than PPO-clip, with a larger observed across-seed spread. Under group-relative advantages, GRPO-OR does not have a higher mean score, but shows a smaller observed spread, a near-zero terminal OR residual, and a declining overshoot fraction, while the matched GRPO clipped-objective trace remains variable. Both group-relative methods exhibit substantially larger rollout-to-current log-ratio displacement than the GAE methods, and OR does not consistently reduce it. Thus, OR changes optimization behavior in both matched comparisons, but the observed reward effect differs between them. At $G=2$, the GRPO-OR diagnostics do not translate into a reward-score gain. Whether larger groups change this outcome remains open. The reported scores are training-time reward-model measurements, not held-out human-preference performance.

</details>


### [227] [A Continual Validation, Updating, and Decision-Making Framework for Self-Adaptive Digital Twins via Robust Model Predictive Control: A Case Study in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18164)
*Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Vispi Karkaria,Seul Lee,Daniel Apley,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自适应数字孪生框架，通过基于Fisher分数的漂移检测器、低秩适应（LoRA）和Mann-Whitney U检验，解决了概念漂移下代理模型退化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生依赖代理模型实时映射物理系统，但在运行条件变化时会出现概念漂移导致模型退化。现有自适应框架缺乏更新时机检测、有限流数据高效适应以及更新效果统计验证的机制，特别是在需要捕获偶然性不确定性的场景下，维持代理模型保真度仍是开放挑战。

Method: 提出了一种自适应数字孪生框架，集成三个核心组件：1）基于Fisher分数的多变量漂移检测器，用于监控代理模型置信度；2）低秩适应（LoRA）实现参数高效的持续学习，仅需微调不到1%的模型参数；3）Mann-Whitney U检验进行在线统计验证，在部署更新前认证预测性能改进。

Result: 在随机线性系统和定向能量沉积增材制造过程两个案例研究中，该框架成功检测到分布漂移（延迟较短），并在突发性和渐进性漂移下恢复了预测精度和不确定性量化能力。框架能够触发针对性的模型微调，并通过统计认证确保更新确实带来改进。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一条统计严谨且计算可行的途径，用于在整个运营生命周期中维持基于神经网络的数字孪生的可信度，为解决概念漂移下的代理模型适应问题提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Digital Twins rely on surrogate models to mirror physical systems in real time, yet these models can degrade as operating conditions evolve, a phenomenon known as concept drift. Maintaining surrogate fidelity under drift, particularly when models must also capture aleatoric uncertainty, remains an open challenge. Existing adaptive frameworks lack principled mechanisms for detecting when updates are needed, for efficiently adapting models from limited streaming data, and for certifying that updates genuinely improve predictive performance. Here we present an adaptive Digital Twin framework that integrates a Fisher score--based multivariate drift detector, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient continual learning, and a Mann--Whitney $U$ test for online statistical validation. The framework monitors surrogate-model confidence via Fisher score vectors, triggers targeted fine-tuning of fewer than 1% of model parameters upon drift detection, and statistically certifies predictive improvement before deploying the updated surrogate. Applied to a stochastic linear system and a directed energy deposition additive manufacturing process as case studies, the framework successfully detects distributional shifts with short delays and restores both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification under abrupt and incremental drift. These results establish a statistically rigorous and computationally tractable pathway for sustaining the trustworthiness of neural-network--based Digital Twins throughout their operational life cycle.

</details>


### [228] [FlashRT: Agent Harness for Guiding Agents to Deploy Real-Time Multimodal Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18171)
*Krish Agarwal,Zhuoming Chen,Yanyuan Qin,Zhenyu Gu,Atri Rudra,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FlashRT，一个利用AI编码代理将简单的参考实现转换为优化多GPU部署的框架，在视频世界模型和多模态LLM等应用中实现了最高约70倍延迟降低和3.6倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的服务系统和自动并行编译器采用有限的转换和固定的工作负载假设，导致在新应用上实现高性能需要手工打造高效的实现，缺乏灵活性。多模态实时应用（如语音代理和交互式视频生成）需要异构模型组成流水线，其高效部署需要针对应用的放置、流式传输和模型内并行决策。

Method: 采用chain-of-program范式，引导通用编码代理通过多阶段转换过程：1) 将参考实现转换为中间表示（IR）以捕获数据依赖和持久状态范围；2) 通过顺序解释器验证IR；3) 进行静态分析识别候选转换；4) 在测量门控优化循环中迭代实现、验证和基准测试每个候选方案，生成跨越不同硬件预算的有效部署。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200 GPU上，FlashRT实现了最高约70倍延迟降低和2.8倍吞吐量提升；在AMD MI355X GPU上，匹配峰值延迟降低同时将峰值吞吐量提升增加到3.6倍。对于Qwen3-Omni文本到音频推理，FlashRT在AMD MI355X上相比专家vLLM-Omni实现减少了65%的响应延迟。

Conclusion: 代理驱动的优化在成熟度较低的平台上更具可扩展性，FlashRT成功证明了通过AI编码代理自动优化多GPU部署的可行性，为实时多模态应用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time multimodal applications, including voice agents and interactive video generation, compose heterogeneous models into pipelines whose efficient deployment requires application-specific decisions about placement, streaming, and intra-model parallelism. Existing serving systems and auto-parallelism compilers commit to limited transformations and fixed workload assumptions, so achieving high performance on a new application requires hand-crafting an efficient implementation. We present FlashRT, an agent harness that guides coding agents to lift simple developer-written reference implementations into optimized multi-GPU deployments that flexibly weigh target metrics like latency and throughput. Using a new chain-of-program paradigm, FlashRT directs a generic coding agent through a multi-pass transformation process where an agent transforms the reference into an intermediate representation (IR) to capture data dependencies and persistent-state scopes, validates this IR via a sequential interpreter, and performs static analyses to identify candidate transformations. Then, the agent iteratively implements, verifies, and benchmarks each candidate under a measurement-gated optimization loop to produce effective deployments that span different hardware budgets. Across various applications, including video world models and multimodal LLMs, FlashRT converts reference implementations into highly efficient deployments, delivering up to ~70x latency reduction and 2.8x throughput improvement on NVIDIA B200 GPUs. On AMD MI355X GPUs, FlashRT matches the peak latency reduction while increasing peak throughput improvement to 3.6x, demonstrating that agent-driven optimization can be more scalable on platforms with less mature expert optimization. In fact, for Qwen3-Omni text-to-audio inference, FlashRT reduces response latency by 65% compared to the expert vLLM-Omni implementation on AMD MI355X.

</details>


### [229] [Three-Body Scattering for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18198)
*Peng Sun,Zhenglin Cheng,Deyuan Liu,Jun Xie,Xinyi Shang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三体散射建模（TBSM）方法用于一步生成，通过将能量距离转化为恒定大小的投射体交互，在ImageNet-256上实现了FID=2.23（像素空间）和FID=1.63（潜空间）的一步生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型通常依赖对抗式批评器、预定义的噪声到数据路径或自回归因子分解。本文探索了一种替代方案：利用适当的分布能量诱导样本级运动，为一步生成器提供直接的回归监督。

Method: 提出三体散射建模（TBSM），将能量距离转化为恒定大小的投射体交互：每个投射体被一个真实源吸引并被一个独立生成的源排斥。基于投射体及其条件，其期望等于2-Wasserstein梯度流速度。使用冻结目标事件批次产生O(B)样本级损失，每个损失使用一个参考作为条件，而非Drifting Models等方法使用的批次级全对场。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上训练一步生成器：像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1）。提供了将扩散相关监督、Drift类动力学和GAN类目标关联的设计图。

Conclusion: 跟踪散射为高维一步生成提供了一条有效路径，建立了分布能量诱导样本级运动的新范式，无需对抗训练或复杂的噪声注入路径即可实现高质量一步生成。

Abstract: Modern generative models typically rely on an adversarial critic, a prescribed noise-to-data path, or an autoregressive factorization. Instead, we show that a proper distributional energy can induce sample-level motion and provide direct regression supervision for a one-step generator. Three-Body Scattering Modeling (TBSM) for generation turns the energy distance into a constant-size per-projectile interaction: each projectile is attracted toward one real source and repelled from one independently generated source. Conditioned on the projectile and its condition, its expectation equals the $2$-Wasserstein gradient-flow velocity of $\frac12D_E^2(P_θ,Q)$. A batch of $B$ frozen-target events yields $O(B)$ sample-level losses, each using one reference for its condition instead of the minibatch-wide all-pairs field used by methods such as Drifting Models. Tracking this conditional expectation online can reduce field noise. Using scattering in frozen image features, TBSM trains one-step generators on ImageNet-256, achieving FID${}=2.23$ with pixel-space PixelDiT-XL and FID${}=1.63$ with latent-space DiT-XL at NFE${}=1$. We provide a design map relating diffusion-related supervision, Drift-like dynamics, and GAN-like objectives. These results establish tracked scattering as a route to high-dimensional one-step generation. Code: https://github.com/sp12138/TBSM.

</details>


### [230] [Causal Discovery on Irregular Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18226)
*Martim Penim,Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro Bizarro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将PCMCI+方法扩展到不规则采样数据场景，通过聚合预定义时间窗口内的因果影响来替代固定滞后的依赖关系，在不规则采样事件流数据上表现出色，相比标准PCMCI+具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果发现方法通常依赖于规则的离散滞后结构，仅适用于规则采样数据，然而许多现实任务需要处理不规则采样的事件流，例如传感器数据、医疗数据和金融交易，因此需要开发能够处理不规则时间序列的因果发现方法。

Method: 扩展PCMCI+方法以处理不规则时间序列，不采用固定滞后依赖建模因果关系，而是通过在预定义时间窗口内聚合因果影响来捕捉因果关系，从而适应不规则采样事件流的特点。

Result: 在具有已知因果结构的合成不规则事件流数据上，在不同信噪比条件下，该方法能够一致地恢复潜在的因果图结构，并且在不规则采样数据上显著优于标准PCMCI+方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功将PCMCI+扩展到不规则采样时间序列场景，填补了不规则事件流因果发现的方法空白，为传感器监测、医疗健康和金融交易等领域的实际应用提供了有效的因果发现工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods have shown strong performance in temporal systems, but they typically rely on regular and discrete lag structures, limiting their applicability to regularly sampled data. However, many real-world tasks require dealing with irregularly sampled streams of events, such as sensor streams, healthcare data, and financial transactions. In this work, we propose an extension of PCMCI+, a state-of-the-art method for causal discovery on regular multivariate time series, to allow for handling irregular time series. Instead of modelling causal relations through fixed-lag dependencies, our method aggregates causal influence over predefined temporal windows. We evaluate our method on synthetic irregular event streams with known causal structures under different signal-to-noise ratios, showing that it consistently recovers the underlying causal graph and substantially outperforms the standard PCMCI+ on irregularly sampled data.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Automated Hardware Validation Test Plan Generation for Large Scale AI Datacenter Platforms Using a Generative AI Multi-Agents Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16388)
*Mohammed-Khalil Ghali,Saurabh Kulkarni,Prathamesh Kulkarni,Rohan Kulkarni,Sangwon Yoon,Daehan Won*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式AI多智能体架构，用于自动化生成硬件验证测试计划，在两个生产平台上实现了74.2%和51.4%的覆盖率提升，将编写时间从数天缩短至数小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI数据中心平台包含数千个异构硬件组件，其验证需要全面的故障注入测试计划。当前测试计划由工程师手工编写，存在劳动密集、易出错、依赖机构知识、覆盖缺口发现晚、可追溯性差且需重复工作等问题。

Method: 采用生成式AI多智能体架构，包含三个核心智能体：(1)摄取智能体将异构输入规范化为标准表示；(2)分类智能体通过上下文推理将组件映射到功能域；(3)生成智能体通过组合规范化故障模式与域分类数据合成测试用例，填补缺口并生成边缘案例。输出符合标准化模式，可直接导入内部验证软件。

Result: 在两个生产平台上评估，相对于手工基线实现了74.2%和51.4%的覆盖率扩展，将编写时间从数天缩短至数小时。实现了从每个测试用例到源规范的完全可追溯映射，自动化和专家评估确认100%提取保真度，新场景接受率高。

Conclusion: 该框架作为稳健的人在回路力量倍增器得到验证，其多智能体分解在平台代际间具有可移植性，能够有效解决传统手工测试计划编写的主要痛点。

Abstract: Large-scale AI datacenter platforms comprise thousands of heterogeneous hardware components whose validation requires comprehensive fault injection test plans. Today these plans are authored manually: engineers review hardware self-healing validation documents and bills of materials, enumerate failure modes per field-replaceable unit, and produce flat lists of single-layer test cases. This process is labor-intensive, error-prone, and dependent on institutional knowledge; coverage gaps surface late, traceability to source specifications is implicit, and the effort is largely repeated per platform. This paper presents a generative AI multi-agent architecture that automates the generation of structured hardware validation test plans from two canonical inputs: self-healing validation documents, which enumerate known failure modes and their detection and remediation behaviors per field-replaceable unit, and component Bills of Material. An ingestion agent normalizes heterogeneous inputs into a canonical representation; a classification agent maps components to functional domains via contextual reasoning over part descriptions and sub-category hierarchies; and a generation agent synthesizes test cases by combining normalized failure modes with domain-classified data, filling gaps and producing edge cases. The output conforms to a standardized schema for direct import into internal validation software. Evaluated on two production platforms against manual baselines, the framework achieves coverage expansions of 74.2% and 51.4%, cutting authoring from days to hours. It yields fully traceable mappings from each test case to its source specification, and its multi-agent decomposition is portable across platform generations. Automated and expert evaluations confirm 100% extraction fidelity and high acceptance of new scenarios, validating the framework as a robust human-in-the-loop force multiplier.

</details>


### [232] [Laplacian Spectral Shaping for Non-Uniform Scaling Formation Control of Open Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16709)
*Tao He,Gangshan Jing*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式协议，通过调整拉普拉斯矩阵的部分权重，在动态拓扑变化下保持多智能体系统的非均匀缩放编队控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放的多智能体系统中，智能体加入/离开、边的增减和领导者选择会动态改变矩阵值拉普拉斯矩阵，破坏编队控制所需的谱性质（正半定性、正确的核、跟随者块的正定性），导致无法维持期望的编队形状。

Method: 开发分布式协议，策略性地调整拉普拉斯矩阵的部分权重。将期望的编队形状编码为矩阵值拉普拉斯的核，通过协议在动态拓扑变化（智能体加入/离开、边的增减）下保持所需的编队谱性质，同时支持任意智能体对作为领导者。

Result: 所提协议能够在智能体加入、边添加、智能体离开和边移除等动态拓扑变化下保持期望的编队谱性质，支持任意智能体对作为领导者，并通过理论分析和数值仿真验证了有效性。相比现有方法，需要更稀疏的感知图，避免预定义的父子层次结构，支持领导者重新分配。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式权重调整协议成功解决了开放多智能体系统中动态拓扑变化下的非均匀缩放编队控制问题，相比现有拉普拉斯设计方法具有更低的通信需求、更灵活的结构和更强的适应性。

Abstract: Non-uniform scaling control enables a multi-agent formation to adjust its shape by compressing or stretching independently along different coordinate axes through inter-agent interactions, offering high flexibility in complex environments. The fundamental idea is encoding the desired formation shape as the kernel of a matrix-valued Laplacian. In open multi-agent systems, however, changes in number of agents, number of edges, and leader selection dynamically alter this Laplacian, destroying the required spectral properties: positive semidefiniteness, correct kernel, and positive definiteness of the follower block (we summarize these properties as the formation spectrum). In this paper, we develop distributed protocols to strategically adjust partial weights of the Laplacian matrix for formation control in arbitrary dimensional space. By implementing the protocols, the desired formation spectrum can be preserved under dynamic topology changes including agent joining, edge addition, agent leaving, and edge removal, while any pair of agents can serve as leaders. Unlike existing Laplacian design methods for affine formation control under topology changes, the proposed approach requires a sparser sensing graph, avoids a predefined parent-child hierarchical structure, and supports leader reassignment. The effectiveness of the proposed protocols is validated through both theoretical analysis and numerical simulations.

</details>


### [233] [When Is Heterogeneous Distance-Decay Facility Location Tractable? A Structural Classification, Exact Methods, and a Real-World Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16764)
*Zhou He,T. C. E. Cheng,Jichang Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续平面设施选址问题，考虑需求点捕获值随距离衰减且各点衰减尺度不同的情况。证明了目标函数的单调子模性质和凹性条件，提出了精确的离散方法和高效的启发式算法，并通过大规模实际数据验证了考虑衰减异质性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设施选址研究多假设需求点的衰减特性相同，但现实中衰减异质性普遍存在且未被充分利用。缺乏统一框架来同时处理衰减、聚类和中值目标，且现有方法难以有效处理连续平面上的异质性衰减问题。

Method: （i）理论分析：证明了离散目标函数具有单调子模性，保证贪婪算法(1-1/e)近似比；分析了连续合作目标的凹性条件，揭示了覆盖率规范中截断操作破坏凹性的原因。（ii）精确方法：构建候选点离散化的最大覆盖MIP模型，通过分支定界求解。（iii）启发式算法：提出力梯度/大邻域搜索算法，结合梯度下降和邻域优化。

Result: （i）MIP模型的LP松弛经验间隙接近0，500个需求点内秒级求解。（ii）启发式算法在30个实例上全面超越贪婪、Cooper交替、粒子群和加权k-means算法（p<10^-9），误差在最优解0.5%以内。（iii）592,667个城市配送订单数据显示：忽略衰减异质性导致损失高达9.7%的捕获需求，设施位置偏移达地图范围37%；零售数据标定衰减为指数型，尺度约1.4公里。

Conclusion: 异质性衰减对设施选址影响显著，不可忽略。所提理论框架统一了k-means、Weber/p-median和最大覆盖问题，算法在实际规模问题上表现优异。衰减异质性标定和建模是提升决策质量的关键。

Abstract: We study continuous planar facility location in which a demand point's captured value decays with distance, with the per-point decay scale varying across points. This heterogeneity is ubiquitous yet underexploited, and one nearest-facility objective unifies decay, clustering, and median goals, containing k-means, the Weber/p-median problem, and maximum covering as special cases. We make four contributions. (i) A tractability classification: the discrete objective is always monotone submodular, so the (1-1/e) greedy guarantee holds regardless of decay shape or heterogeneity, and the continuous cooperative objective is concave if and only if the decay is concave in distance; the clip max(0,d) in common coverage specifications is what destroys concavity, and the classification is tight. (ii) An exact discrete method: the candidate-discretized maximum-cover MIP has an empirically tight LP relaxation (~0% gap) and is solved by branch-and-bound in seconds for n <= 500. (iii) A force-as-gradient / large-neighborhood-search heuristic, within 0.5% of the discrete optimum, that outperforms the (1-1/e) greedy, Cooper-style alternating location-allocation, particle swarm optimization, and weighted k-means (30/30 per-instance wins at K=30, p<10^-9) and is competitive with bespoke solvers on k-means, Weber/p-median, and shape-demand instances. (iv) A real-world study: on 592,667 urban-delivery orders, ignoring the calibrated decay variation loses up to 9.7% of captured demand and relocates facilities by up to 37% of the map; a retail dataset calibrates the decay as exponential with scale R ~ 1.4 km.

</details>


### [234] [Auto Research for Materials: Auditable AI-Scientist Workflows with Held-Out Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17100)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Shanshan Zhong,Ji Zeng,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究评估了AI研究代理在材料建模中的可泛化性，发现在未见过的验证集上，90%的选择仍保持最佳性能，且代码可重复使用，揭示了仅基于成分和基于结构两种不同的建模改进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究代理可能仅在被看到的评分数据上改进，而未找到真正可泛化的建模改进。本研究提出更严格的问题：经过重复实验后，所选改动是否能在从未进入循环的独立数据上存活，且其代码能否被复用？

Method: 将搜索分解为特征、模型、表示和训练数据四类改动；在十个Matbench任务端点上进行七次搜索，产生701个评估改动；代理仅接收五个内部折叠的平均值；冻结所选代码并在未触及的留存集上一次性评估；将组合式改进进行整合测试。

Result: 在留存集上，90%（9/10）的选择仍是最佳单次干预；在成分任务中，特征、模型和表示改动均可显著降低MAE（带隙降17.4%，钢强度降18.6%）；在结构任务中，几何描述符和模型/校准改动分别降低MAE 14.6%和7.1%；组合特征与模型改动实现平均26.3%的改进；成分嵌入无法迁移至结构任务。

Conclusion: 闭环AI代理在材料预测中可产生在未见数据上存活的决策，且代码可跨任务复用和组合；研究揭示了成分建模与结构建模两种不同的改进机制；提供了超越反馈循环的可执行发现评估设计。

Abstract: An AI research agent can improve the score it sees without finding a modelling change that works on new materials. We ask a stricter question. After repeated experiments, does the selected change survive on data that never entered the loop, and can its code be reused? We separate the search into changes to features, models, representations, and training data. Seven searches produce 701 evaluated changes across ten Matbench endpoints. Agents receive only the mean over five inner folds, reducing reliance on any single development split. We then freeze the selected code and evaluate it once on an untouched holdout. Nine of ten choices remain the best tested single intervention. The surviving changes reveal two materials modelling regimes. With composition alone, feature, model, and representation changes provide comparable routes to improvement. They include held-out MAE reductions of 17.4\% for band gap and 18.6\% for steel strength, as well as gains on both classification endpoints, while screened external data adds little. For structure tasks, richer geometry descriptors and model or calibration changes lower mean held-out MAE by 14.6\% and 7.1\% and lead on different property families, whereas composition embeddings do not transfer. Combining separately found feature and model changes yields a 26.3\% mean held-out improvement. These results show in materials prediction that closed-loop agents can produce decisions that survive unseen evidence and code changes that can be reused across tasks and combined. More broadly, they provide an evaluation design for testing executable discoveries beyond the feedback loop.

</details>


### [235] [The Optimization Trilemma: Efficiency, Comfort and Fairness in Decentralized Multi-agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17311)
*Jovan Nikolic,Maciej Krzysztof Zuziak,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新颖的去中心化多智能体协调模型，能够同时优化系统效率、个体舒适度和公平性三个目标，在不需要显著增加通信和计算开销的情况下实现更公平的资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体协调算法主要关注中心化设置下的系统效率与个体舒适度平衡，但忽视了完全去中心化场景下的公平性资源优化问题，特别是如何公平地重新分配智能体之间的不适成本，防止因过度不适导致的激励缺失或极化现象。

Method: 设计了一个新颖的模型，在去中心化多智能体协调中同时优化三个正交目标：(i)系统整体效率、(ii)个体舒适度和(iii)公平性（即不适成本的平衡），同时不显著增加通信和计算开销。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上进行的实验验证表明，该模型能够实现更公平的优化结果，同时满足智能体的偏好和系统目标。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了去中心化场景下公平多智能体协调的空白，证明了在去中心化设置中同时实现效率、舒适度和公平性三个目标优化的可行性。

Abstract: The problem of fair multi-agent coordination in decentralized settings is one of the most pressing challenges for building efficient collaborative systems. Resource allocation is based on optimized collective arrangements accounting for agents' needs. Such coordination should not only be computationally efficient but also account for fairness, i.e., equitable redistribution of costs incurred by all agents. Recent literature has proposed several algorithms that efficiently determine optimal plan combinations balancing system-wide efficiency and individual discomfort of agents in a centralized setting. However, these works do not address equitable resource optimization in fully decentralized scenarios, specifically, the optimized redistribution of discomfort among coordinating agents so that none experiences a discomfort level that could lead to loss of incentive or polarization that can disrupt planned operations. In this work, we study the problem of optimizing three objectives: (i) system-wide efficiency, (ii) individuals' comfort and (iii) fairness (i.e., balancing of incurred discomfort costs) in decentralized multi-agent coordination. We design a novel model to optimize those three orthogonal objectives, without any substantial increase in communication and computational overhead. Through experiments on two real-world datasets, we validate the model and demonstrate that it can achieve fairer optimization outcomes, while satisfying agents' preferences and system goals.

</details>


### [236] [A Digital Twin-Based Method for Evaluating Local Collective Tariffs in Distribution-Level Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17640)
*Kristoffer Christensen,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生的评估方法，用于分析配电级能源系统中的集体电价机制。通过丹麦本地集体电价的案例验证，发现聚合异构需求可降低峰值重合并实现成本节约，但成本中性受时间匹配度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对配电级能源系统中已实施电价机制缺乏工程化评估方法的问题，需要在真实行为和基础设施条件下对本地集体电价进行系统性评估。

Method: 提出基于数字孪生的评估方法，在统一仿真环境中整合：基于智能体的家庭消发用电建模、通过共享计量抽象实现的虚拟聚合、以及电价逻辑的显式表示。以丹麦本地集体电价为案例，在包含光伏、储能和电动汽车充电的典型住宅能源社区场景中进行验证。

Result: 异构需求曲线聚合降低了峰值重合度，使电价组分分配更高效，在社区层面实现了可量化的成本节约；成本中性程度对消发用电的时间匹配度表现出敏感性；数字孪生方法能有效捕捉基础设施、用户行为与监管设计之间的交互作用。

Conclusion: 数字孪生评估方法为配电级电价机制的系统化评估提供了可行框架，可支持超越具体案例的电价结构分析与比较，为电价设计决策提供工程化依据。

Abstract: This work addresses the need for engineering-grounded evaluation of implement-ed tariff mechanisms in distribution-level energy systems. A digital twin-based method is proposed for assessing local collective tariffs under realistic behavioral and infrastructural conditions. The approach integrates agent-based modeling of household consumption and generation, virtual aggregation through a shared metering abstraction, and explicit representation of tariff logic within a unified simulation environment. The method is demonstrated through its application to the Danish Local Collective Tariff across representative residential energy community configurations, including scenarios with photovoltaic generation, battery storage, and electric vehicle charging. Results indicate that aggregation of heterogeneous demand profiles reduces peak coincidence and enables more efficient allocation of tariff components, leading to measurable cost reductions at the community level. At the same time, the outcomes reveal sensitivity to the temporal alignment of consumption and generation, influencing the degree of cost neutrality across participants. The findings illustrate how digital twin-based evaluation can support systematic assessment of tariff mechanisms by capturing the interaction between infrastructure, user behavior, and regulatory design. The proposed approach provides a basis for analyzing and comparing tariff structures in distribution-level energy systems beyond the specific case considered.

</details>


### [237] [Value-Aware Prediction for Robust Multi-Agent Coordination Under Communication Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17914)
*Kemal Devrim Kafadar,Eren Özaltun,Mahmud Efnan Şanlı,Feyza Orak,Emirhan Gazi,Kubilay Kağan Kömürcü,Nazım Kemal Üre*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Value-Aware MARO方法，通过基于优势估计的动态加权损失函数，使预测器专注于策略强化的高回报动态，在低通信可靠性场景下相比基线方法平均回报提升超过20%，性能方差降低64.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协调严重依赖智能体间通信，但实际部署中常因物理和环境约束导致通信中断。现有基于标准重建目标的预测器将所有转移视为同等重要，在强化学习场景下迫使模型浪费容量学习随机探索噪声和次优策略的过时动态，导致预测性能下降。

Method: 提出Value-Aware MARO方法，利用底层Actor-Critic架构产生的优势估计对预测器的损失函数进行动态加权，将预测器的学习过程与策略演化显式耦合，使模型容量聚焦于策略主动强化的有意向、高回报动态上。

Result: 在Multi-Agent Particle Environment多个任务和不同通信可靠性水平下的实验表明：在通信可靠性低于40%时保持性能稳定；在高损失场景下平均回报提升超过20%，性能方差平均降低64.7%；在基线已维持高协调性的任务中表现相当。

Conclusion: 基于价值感知的加权机制有效解决了标准预测器在高通信损失场景下的性能崩溃问题，显著提升了多智能体系统在通信不可靠环境中的鲁棒性和稳定性，为实际部署提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Robust multi-agent coordination relies heavily on inter-agent communication, which is frequently disrupted by physical and environmental constraints in real-world deployments. To maintain operation during these intermittent communication failures, agents can employ internal prediction models to estimate missing shared state information. However, predictors trained with standard reconstruction objectives treat all transitions equally. In a Reinforcement Learning context, this forces the model to waste capacity learning stochastic exploration noise and the outdated dynamics of suboptimal policies. In this paper, we propose a value-aware extension of Multi-Agent Observation Sharing under Communication Dropout (MARO) to patch communication gaps; we refer to this method as Value-Aware MARO. By dynamically weighting the predictor's loss function using advantage estimates derived from the underlying actor-critic architecture, our objective explicitly couples the predictor's learning process to the policy's evolution. This formulation focuses the model's capacity on the intentional, high-return dynamics actively reinforced by the agents. We evaluate our framework on several tasks within the Multi-Agent Particle Environment under varying communication reliability levels. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach maintains performance under declining communication reliability, particularly below 40%. While our method performs comparably in tasks where the baseline already maintains high coordination, our value-aware weighting effectively prevents the performance collapse observed in the standard predictor during high-attrition scenarios. In these environments, our method achieves an average improvement in mean returns of more than 20% and reduces performance variance by a mean of 64.7% compared to the standard unweighted baseline.

</details>


### [238] [PRIME: Plasticity Recovery in Multi-Agent Environments for UAV-Assisted Emergency Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17922)
*Wen Qiu,Zhiqiang He,Wei Zhao,Hiroshi Masui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PRIME方法，通过同时检测神经元的激活休眠状态和梯度静默状态来解决多智能体强化学习中的神经元休眠问题，在非平稳环境下相比MAPPO提升24.9%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习控制器大多假设平稳环境，少数处理非平稳性的方法只关注外部环境变化而忽略了网络内部状态。本文发现持续的非平稳性会直接破坏内部状态：随着目标偏移，神经元逐渐进入休眠状态，共享策略丧失学习能力。简单的重置休眠神经元在共享参数的多智能体训练中是不安全的，因为许多看似不活跃的神经元仍在接收强训练梯度。

Method: 提出PRIME（Plasticity Recovery In Multi-agent Environments）方法：1）将双向静默神经元框架扩展到协作多智能体强化学习；2）在完整团队批次上聚合激活和梯度统计信息；3）从训练损失已沉积的梯度读取反向信号，而非手工设计的代理；4）仅对同时满足激活休眠和梯度静默的神经元进行重初始化，从而保留有用表示并恢复学习能力。

Result: 在相位切换的无人机应急通信仿真器上：PRIME相比MAPPO将四分位均值回报提升24.9%；将休眠神经元比例维持在10-20%，而MAPPO为40-45%；消融实验表明性能提升来源于梯度信号和团队级聚合，而非特定的重置操作符。动态遗憾界限表明扰动代价与小的静默子空间维度成正比，而非完整参数数量。

Conclusion: PRIME成功解决了非平稳环境下多智能体强化学习中的神经元休眠问题，通过同时验证激活和梯度状态确保重置操作的安全性，在保持有用表示的同时恢复学习能力，为动态环境下的多智能体系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Most reinforcement learning controllers for these networks assume stationary conditions, and the few that handle change react to the external environment while leaving the network's internal state unexamined. We show that sustained non-stationarity damages this internal state directly: as objectives shift, neurons progressively fall dormant and the shared policy loses the capacity to learn. The obvious remedy, resetting dormant neurons, is unsafe under shared-parameter multi-agent training: many neurons that appear inactive are still receiving strong training gradients, and whether a neuron appears dormant depends on which agent's observations it processes. PRIME (Plasticity Recovery In Multi-agent Environments) therefore verifies both directions before intervening. Extending the bidirectional Silent Neuron framework to cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, it aggregates activation and gradient statistics over the full team batch, reads the backward signal from the gradient the training loss has already deposited , not from a hand-crafted proxy, and reinitializes only neurons that are simultaneously activation-dormant and gradient-silent. Useful representations are preserved while learning capacity is restored. On a phase-switching UAV emergency communication simulator, PRIME improves interquartile mean return by 24.9\% over MAPPO and holds dormant neuron fractions at 10--20\% versus 40--45\%; ablations attribute the gains to the gradient signal and team-level aggregation rather than to the specific reset operator. A dynamic regret bound shows that the perturbation cost scales with the small silent-subspace dimension rather than the full parameter count.

</details>


### [239] [Aggregate in the Advantage, Not the Ratio: A Canonical-Form Analysis of Cooperative Multi-Agent Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17924)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究提出了多智能体策略优化的理论框架，揭示了优势函数和比率矩阵的乘积结构，提出了“在优势函数中聚合邻居、比率保持单智能体”的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体策略优化方法（如IPPO、MAPPO、HAPPO等）在聚合邻居智能体的设计上缺乏系统性理论指导，具体表现为在优势函数和比率两个维度上的聚合策略不一致且未经充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白，为多智能体合作学习提供明确的设计原则。

Method: 引入支持矩阵SA（优势函数聚合）和SR（比率聚合）来形式化这两个设计维度，并证明期望多智能体策略优化目标函数仅依赖于矩阵乘积S=SR·SA。通过分析该结构的冗余性和方差特性，推导出设计原则。具体地，证明优势函数以加和方式聚合奖励（方差随邻居数线性增长），而比率以乘积方式聚合似然比（方差呈指数增长且无偏差降低收益）。

Result: 发现两个关键性质：（1）冗余性：SA和SR在信号上可互换，不存在本质上的优劣；（2）方差排序：优势函数的聚合方差可控且存在最优邻居规模（耦合邻域），而比率的聚合方差不可控且无益处。最终结论是在优势函数中聚合至耦合邻域规模的邻居，比率保持单智能体级别。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体策略优化提供了统一的理论视角，澄清了现有方法的设计空间，并给出了明确的设计准则：优势函数应聚合耦合邻域内的智能体，比率应保持单智能体。这一原则可指导未来算法设计并解释现有方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent policy optimization, exemplified by PPO-based methods, is a key branch of cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). A central design question is how many neighboring agents\footnote{In this paper, "neighbors" refer not only to physical proximity but also to agents whose actions influence one another.} to aggregate in order to effectively utilize global information for cooperation. This decision must be made along two dimensions: in the advantage (which agents' rewards contribute to the credit signal) and in the ratio (which agents' likelihood ratios form the clipped importance weight). Existing methods occupy scattered, underexplored points on these two axes: IPPO treats both separately; MAPPO pairs a team-level advantage with per-agent ratios; HAPPO employs sequential ratios with per-agent advantages; and single-agent reductions operating on factorized joint policies aggregate both into fully joint products. We formalize these two design choices as support matrices $\SA$ and $\SR$, and prove a canonical structure: the expected multi-agent policy optimization objective depends on the pair $(\SA,\SR)$ only through their matrix product $\tS=\SR\SA$. This yields two key consequences: (i) Redundancy: the two support matrices are interchangeable with respect to the signal, meaning neither aggregation pattern is inherently superior.(ii) Variance Ordering: the advantage aggregates rewards as a sum (additive variance with an interior bias-variance optimum at the coupling neighborhood), whereas the ratio aggregates likelihood ratios as a product (multiplicative variance that grows exponentially with support size, with no accompanying bias reduction). The resulting design principle is unambiguous: aggregate neighbors in the advantage, sized to the coupling neighborhood, and keep the ratio per-agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva,Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了RLHF中的一种结构化混淆因素：偏好标签可能不仅反映输出质量，还反映了标注者在持续压力状态下的偏好变化，这种"标注者状态偏移"会通过奖励建模和政策优化传播。提出了理论框架、可证伪预测和审计协议来检测这种偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF研究假设成对偏好标签仅反映对输出质量的判断，但标注者在持续压力或困扰状态下可能产生随时间变化的偏好偏移。这种偏移不同于普通分歧或随机噪声，它与状态相关，可在相似条件下的标注者间共享，并能通过奖励模型和政策优化传播，构成了RLHF偏好数据中一个被忽视的结构化偏置来源。

Method: 定义了标注者状态偏移、标注者状态混淆和相关标注者状态偏置等概念；提出"生存水平情感真实性"作为可测量的响应模式（使用词汇、语用、话语和安全相关特征）；分析了相关标注者状态偏置如何通过聚合进入学习到的奖励信号；推导出五个可证伪的预测和效应量阈值；设计了审计协议和试点研究方案。

Result: 成功建立了标注者状态偏移作为RLHF偏好数据中结构化偏置来源的理论假设；证明了相关标注者状态偏置能够在数据聚合过程中存活并传播到学习的奖励信号中；提供了具体的可检验预测和效应量阈值；开发了可应用于公开指令调优模型的审计框架。

Conclusion: 标注者状态偏移是RLHF中一个合理且可测试的结构化偏置来源，需要通过系统审计来识别和量化。本文提供的理论框架、可证伪预测和审计协议为检测这种偏置奠定了基础，有助于改进RLHF数据质量和模型可靠性。

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [241] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight 1D CNN for Affective Touch Classification in Soft Plush Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16196)
*Aleksandrs Vališevskis,Aleksandrs Okss,Inese Tīģere,Aleksejs Kataševs,Dina Bethere,Anete Hofmane,Airisa Šteinberga,Undīne Gavriļenko,Santa Meļķe,Lucie Matoušková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个基于MATLAB的开源框架，用于软体交互伴侣的情感触觉识别。通过构建包含25名参与者的1326个标注手势序列的FAIR数据集，并系统探索468个CNN模型，发现紧凑的扩张一维卷积神经网络(13.2k参数)可实现75%测试准确率和85%交叉验证准确率，量化部署仅需3.2 MMAC/窗口，可在目标微控制器上实现20Hz实时运行。研究提出混合推理流水线策略，将即时启发式过滤与CNN细微手势分类相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体传感器化伴侣为社交辅助技术提供了物理安全且情感直观的接口，但其可变形性和多通道触觉感知使得人类情感的鲁棒解释变得复杂。现有系统在检测微妙社交触觉方面存在不足，需要一种能够在资源受限的嵌入式平台上实时运行的解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建FAIR合规数据集：收集25名参与者(儿童、青少年、成人)的1326个标注手势序列
2) 系统性架构探索：训练并评估468个CNN模型
3) 模型优化：识别出紧凑的扩张一维卷积神经网络为最优方案(13.2k参数)
4) 部署分析：理论推理时间分析和量化部署优化
5) 实时仿真验证：基于PC的实时仿真与物理玩具传感器数据流测试
6) 混合推理策略：结合启发式过滤与CNN分类

Result: 1) 模型性能：13.2k参数模型达到75%测试准确率和85%平均留一受试者交叉验证准确率
2) 计算效率：量化部署仅需3.2 MMAC/窗口，支持目标微控制器上20Hz实时运行
3) 功能验证：CNN成功检测到之前启发式系统无法识别的微妙社交触觉，而高力度负面交互可由简单阈值逻辑更可靠捕获
4) 混合策略有效性：提出的混合推理流水线结合了两种方法的优势

Conclusion: 研究证明了在软体治疗伴侣中直接嵌入情感意义丰富且隐私保护的触觉解释在计算上是可行的。提出的开源框架和公开数据集为未来情感触觉识别研究提供了可复用资源。混合推理流水线(启发式过滤+CNN分类)作为嵌入式部署策略，将在后续研究中解决硬件集成问题。

Abstract: Soft, sensorized companions offer a physically safe and emotionally intuitive interface for socially assistive technologies, yet their deformability and multichannel tactile sensing complicate the robust interpretation of human affect. This study presents a complete open-source MATLAB-based framework for the development and validation of compact deep learning models for affective touch recognition in soft interactive companions. As a primary contribution, a diverse FAIR-compliant dataset of 1326 labelled gesture sequences collected from 25 participants spanning children, teenagers, and adults is made publicly available, providing a reusable resource for future research in affective touch recognition. Through systematic architecture and hyperparameter exploration across 468 CNN models, the study identifies compact dilated one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D CNNs) as the most effective solution, with a 13.2k-parameter model achieving 75% test accuracy and 85% mean leave-one-subject-out cross-validation accuracy. Theoretical inference-time analysis shows that quantized deployment requires 3.2 MMAC per window, compatible with 20 Hz real-time operation on the target microcontroller. PC-based real-time simulation with the physical toy streaming sensor data demonstrates that the CNN resolves subtle social touches that the previous heuristic system failed to detect, whereas high-force negative interactions are captured more reliably by trivial threshold-based logic. The resulting hybrid inference pipeline - instantaneous heuristic filtering followed by CNN-based nuanced gesture classification - is proposed as the embedded deployment strategy. The study demonstrates that emotionally meaningful, privacy-preserving touch interpretation is computationally feasible for direct embedding within soft therapeutic companions, with hardware integration addressed in a forthcoming study.

</details>


### [242] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过跨域框架对六个大型语言模型（LLMs）的风险态度进行系统测试，发现大多数LLMs表现出稳定的任务内一致性和跨任务风险姿态稳定性，且其风险态度分布收敛于更受限的范围，为AI系统的评估和对齐提供了新维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能系统越来越多地被部署在开放式、高风险环境中，如何将感知风险转化为行动这一关键维度尚未得到充分测量。研究旨在填补这一空白，系统性探索大型语言模型是否表现出稳定、一致的风险态度。

Method: 提出跨域框架，解耦上下文风险信念与类别决策。对六个代表性LLMs和100名人类参与者进行空间导航、临床分诊和金融配置三类任务测试。使用回归模型提取每个智能体的信念到决策映射，量化其风险敏感性和风险态度偏差。

Result: 发现大多数测试的LLMs表现出：（i）稳健的任务内一致性，即在固定任务域内从上下文信念到风险决策的映射稳定；（ii）跨域排序稳定性，在不同任务间保持相对风险姿态；（iii）风险态度分布收敛于比人类基准更受限的范围。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了风险态度是LLM行为的一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度，为在开放式决策环境中评估和对齐AI系统奠定了基础，并激励进一步研究这些内在行为倾向的起源。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [243] [A Survey on GNN-based Link Prediction: Techniques, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16198)
*Chengcheng Sun,Yajie Song,Cheng Zhai,Jiayun Tian,Jia Yang,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Zhixiao Wang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从GNN视角系统综述了基于图神经网络的链路预测方法，提出了新的分类法，涵盖了关键架构和应用场景，并讨论了挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏针对底层GNN架构和多样化图结构的系统性探索，本文旨在填补这一关键空白，提供从GNN角度出发的全面综述。

Method: 提出创新分类法，从技术角度分析关键GNN编码器架构（包括GCN、GAE、GAT和GFormer等方法），从应用角度聚焦知识图谱和推荐系统中的链路预测用例。

Result: 系统梳理了基于GNN的链路预测技术发展，详细阐述了各类方法的优势与局限，展示了链路预测在实际应用中的影响。

Conclusion: 总结了当前挑战，展望了有前景的未来研究方向，为该领域研究者提供了系统性参考。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as the leading paradigm for link prediction, enabling the inference of missing connections and the anticipation of potential future links. However, existing reviews lack systematic exploration specifically targeting underlying GNN architectures and diverse graph structures. To address this critical gap, this paper provides a comprehensive review of GNN-based link prediction from a novel and dedicated GNN perspective. We propose an innovative taxonomy that categorizes recent advancements based on techniques and applications. From a technique perspective, we focus on key GNN encoder architectures, including GCN-based, GAE-based, GAT-based, and GFormer-based methods, discussing their strengths and limitations. From an application perspective, we highlight prominent use cases of link prediction in knowledge graphs and recommendation systems, demonstrating their real-world impact. In addition, we examine the current challenges and discuss promising future directions.

</details>


### [244] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体LLM系统中规划阶段的安全漏洞，提出PlanFlip攻击框架，发现更强模型反而更脆弱，同构系统存在盲点，并提出异构模型多样性是系统安全的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统依赖规划器将目标分解为子任务序列，作者识别出规划阶段是一个关键攻击面：对规划器上下文的单次注入可实现级联放大，同时污染所有下游子任务。现有系统假设更强模型更安全，且同构冗余能提供保护，但这些假设缺乏验证。

Method: 提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击：目标替换（PF-1）、优先级反转（PF-2）、上下文污染（PF-3）和角色混淆（PF-4），每种攻击都伪装为合理的工具输出以规避关键词过滤。在9个前沿LLM上评估3,479个回合，并提出两种防御机制：目标锚定检查（D1）和跨智能体共识（D2）。

Result: 发现三个关键发现：(1)能力放大漏洞——GPT-5达到最高攻击成功率(ASR=0.68)，反驳了更强模型更安全的假设；(2)同构管道存在相关智能体盲点——GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR接近0但隐蔽性=1.00、步骤偏移>0，攻击重构计划而同骨干评论器报告一致；(3)推理增强模型抵抗注入——DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击中步骤偏移=0.00。防御机制实现高达1.00的检测率，在16个单元中的15个优于同构基线。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统安全的先决条件；同构骨干内的冗余无法抵御规划阶段攻击。更强的模型并不一定更安全，能力提升反而可能放大漏洞。推理增强模型展现出更好的抗攻击能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [245] [Deterministic Replay for AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16200)
*Rasheed Mudasiru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: agrepl是一个用于AI智能体确定性重放的CLI框架，通过MITM代理拦截外部交互并序列化为执行轨迹，实现了在零网络访问的隔离环境中忠实重放执行过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体系统（LLM+外部工具/API）本质上是不可预测的，LLM采样方差、外部API状态、CDN基础设施头部和执行环境噪声共同导致无法忠实地重新执行任何先前的智能体运行。现有的可观测性平台只能捕获执行日志，无法在隔离环境中复现运行过程。

Method: 提出agrepl框架，通过中间人(MITM)代理在传输层拦截所有外部交互，将其序列化为结构化执行轨迹，并在严格隔离的零出站网络访问环境中重放。形式化定义了智能体执行模型、请求键匹配函数K(s)，证明了确定性不变式，并引入噪声感知差分算法将HTTP头部差异分类为信号和噪声层。

Result: 在五个工作负载上进行了实证评估(n=250个重放实例)，实现了重放保真度F=1.0，每步延迟中位数减少98.3%。agrepl用Go语言实现，以单一静态二进制文件形式发布，采用MIT许可证开源。

Conclusion: agrepl成功解决了AI智能体系统的确定性重放问题，为开发者提供了可靠的执行复现能力，显著降低了调试延迟，具有重要的实用价值和推广意义。

Abstract: AI agent systems that couple large language models (LLMs) with external tools and APIs are inherently non-deterministic: LLM sampling variance, external API state, CDN infrastructure headers, and execution-environment noise collectively prevent any prior agent run from being faithfully re-executed. Existing observability platforms capture execution logs but cannot reproduce a run in isolation. We present agrepl, a developer-first CLI framework for deterministic replay of agent executions. agrepl intercepts all external interactions at the transport layer via a man-in-the-middle (MITM) proxy, serialises them as structured execution traces, and replays them in a strictly isolated environment with zero outbound network access. We formalise the agent execution model, define the request-key matching function K(s), and prove the determinism invariant. We introduce a noise-aware diff algorithm classifying HTTP header divergence into signal and noise tiers.
